**וּלְאֶחָיו אֵין בָּנִים רַבִּים וְכֹל מִשְׁפַּחְתָּם לֹא הִרְבּוּ עַד בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה.** לֹא הִרְבּוּ בָנִים כִּבְנֵי יְהוּדָה; לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ מוּעָטִים וְחֵלֶק יְהוּדָה רַב מֵהֶם, קִבֵּל יְהוּדָה שִׁמְעוֹן בְּחֶלְקוֹ. כִּי שִׁמְעוֹן לֹא הָיָה לוֹ חֵלֶק בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״אֲחַלְּקֵם בְּיַעֲקֹב וַאֲפִיצֵם בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל״ (בראשית מט:ז): וְשָׁנִינוּ בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (בראשית רבה צח:ה): אֲחַלְּקֵם לְהַעֲמִיד סוֹפְרִים בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּבָתֵּי כְנֵסִיּוֹת וְכוּ׳. וּמִנַּיִן שֶׁקִּבְּלוֹ? דִּכְתִיב ״מֵחֶבֶל בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה נַחֲלַת בְּנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן״ (יהושע יט:ט) כִּי הָיָה חֵלֶק בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה רַב מֵהֶם וַיִּנְחֲלוּ בְנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן בְּתוֹךְ נַחֲלָתָם פֶּן תִּרְבֶּה עֲלֵיהֶם חַיַּת הַשָּׂדֶה (דברים ז:כב). וּמַה שֶּׁכָּתוּב ״וַיַּעַל הַגּוֹרָל לִבְנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן״ (יהושע יט:א), כָּךְ הָיָה הַמַּעֲשֶׂה: הִפִּילוּ גּוֹרָל אֵיזֶה שֵׁבֶט יְקַבֵּל שִׁמְעוֹן בְּחֶלְקוֹ וְנָפַל הַגּוֹרָל בְּחֶלְקוֹ שֶׁל יְהוּדָה; וְגַם מַה שֶּׁכָּתוּב ״וַיֹּאמֶר יְהוּדָה לְשִׁמְעוֹן אָחִיו עֲלֵה אִתִּי בְגוֹרָלִי וְנִלָּחֲמָה בַּכְּנַעֲנִי וְהָלַכְתִּי גַם אֲנִי אִתָּךְ״ (שופטים א:ג) – בַּחֵלֶק שֶׁנָּתַתִּי לְךָ:


**but his brothers did not have many sons and all their family did not multiply as much as the children of Judah** They did not have as many children as the children of Judah. Since they were few, and Judah’s territory was too large for them, Judah accepted Simeon in its territory, because Simeon had no share in the land of Israel, as it says (Gen. 49:7): “I shall divide them in Jacob and scatter them in Israel,” and we learned in *Genesis Rabbah* (*Shittah Hadashah*): “I shall divide them among the Children of Israel to station scribes in Israel in the synagogues, etc.” Now how do we know that they agreed [to give them a share of their territory]? For it is written (Jos. 19:9): “Out of the lot of the children of Judah was the inheritance of the children of Simeon, for the portion of the children of Judah was too large for them; therefore the children of Simeon inherited in the midst of their inheritance,” lest the beasts of the field outnumber them. Now as regards the verse (ibid. v. 1): “And the second lot came out to Simeon,” this is what happened: They cast lots to choose which tribe would accept Simeon in its territory, and the lot fell upon the territory of Judah. And the meaning of the verse (Jud. 1:3): “And Judah said to Simeon his brother: Come up with me into my lot, and we will fight against the Canaanites, and I will also go with you into your lot,” refers to “the lot I have given you.”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 4:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:28:1)


**וַיֵּשְׁבוּ בִּבְאֵר שֶׁבַע.** בְּנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן יָשְׁבוּ בְּאוֹתָן עֲיָירוֹת שֶׁל יְהוּדָה:


**They dwelt in Beersheba** The children of Simeon dwelt in those towns of Judah.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 4:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:31:1)


**אֵלֶּה עָרֵיהֶם עַד מְלֹךְ דָּוִד.** פִּתְרוֹן: כְּשֶׁהִרְבּוּ בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה, עָשׂוּ תַּרְעוֹמוֹת עַל בְּנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן מִימֵי שָׁאוּל, וְרָצוּ לְגָרְשָׁם מֵאַרְצָם, וְלֹא הָיָה יָכוֹל לְגָרְשָׁם, וְאַף כִּי הָיָה כָּל יָמָיו בְּטִרְדָּה וּבְמִלְחָמוֹת; וּכְשֶׁמָּלַךְ דָּוִד בָּאוּ שִׁבְטוֹ לִשְׁאֹל מִבְּנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן אֶת אַרְצָם שֶׁהִשְׁאִילוּ לָהֶם, וְהָלַךְ דָּוִד וְגָר שָׁם; כִּי שָׁאוּל לֹא חָשַׁשׁ בְּכָךְ, מִפְּנֵי שִׂנְאַת דָּוִד שֶׁהָיָה מִיהוּדָה; וְזֶהוּ שֶׁכָּתַב: אֵלֶּה עָרֵיהֶם עַד מְלֹךְ דָּוִד, וּכְשֶׁמָּלַךְ גֵּרְשָׁם. מִפִּי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בַּר מְשֻׁלָּם ז״ל, וְכֵן פֵּרֵשׁ לִי רַבִּי שְׁלֹמֹה בַּר לֵוִי אֲחִי רַבִּי מֹשֶׁה הַדַּרְשָׁן:


**these were their cities until David reigned** The meaning is that when [the population of] the children of Judah grew, they complained about the children of Simeon in the days of Saul and wanted to drive them out of their land, but they could not drive them out, especially since all his [Saul’s] days were occupied with trouble and wars; but when David reigned, his tribe came to request from the children of Simeon the land that they had lent them, and David went and drove them out, for Saul had not been concerned with this matter because of [his] hatred for David, who was from Judah. This is the meaning of what is written: “... these were their cities until David reigned,” but when he reigned, he drove them out. [I heard this] from Rabbi Eliezer the son of Meshullam of blessed memory, and so did Rabbi Solomon the son of Levi, the brother of Rabbi Moshe Hadarshan explain it.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 4:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:32:1)


**וְחַצְרֵיהֶם.** הַכְּפָרִים מִי הָיוּ: **עֵיטָם וְעַיִן וְרִמּוֹן וְגוֹ׳**:


**And their villages** The villages which were they? Etam, and Ain, Rimmon, etc.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 4:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:33:1)


**זֹאת מוֹשְׁבוֹתָם וְהִתְיַחְשָׂם לָהֶם.** פִּתְרוֹן: אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיִּחֲסָם כָּאן מִקְצָתָן, אַל תֹּאמַר שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ כִּי אִם אוֹתָן, שֶׁהֲרֵי יֵשׁ סֵפֶר יִחוּס אַחֵר, וְשָׁם הָיוּ יִחוּסִין כָּל שֵׁבֶט וְשֵׁבֶט, כָּךְ הָיָה לְפָרֵשׁ יִחוּסוֹ בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ:


**this was [the account of] their settlements, and they had their genealogical record** The meaning is that although he traced part of their lineage here, do not say that there were only those, for there is another genealogical record in which the genealogy of every tribe appears. So it was, i.e. [this record] delineated the genealogy [of each tribe] by itself.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 4:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:34:1)


**וּמְשׁוֹבָב.** וְכָל הַכְּתוּבִין:


**And Meshobab** and all those written.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 4:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:38:1)


**אֵלֶּה הַבָּאִים בְּשֵׁמוֹת נְשִׂיאִים בְּמִשְׁפְּחוֹתָם וּבֵית אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם פָּרְצוּ לָרֹב.** לֹא הָיָה לָהֶם מָקוֹם לֵישֵׁב:


**These mentioned by name were princes in their families, and their fathers’ house spread exceedingly** and they had no place to dwell in.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 4:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:39:1)


**וַיֵּלְכוּ.** עַל כֵּן **לִמְבוֹא גְדֹר וְגוֹ׳ לְבַקֵּשׁ מִרְעֶה לְצֹאנָם**:


**And they went** therefore to the approach to Gedor, etc. to seek pasture for their flocks.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 4:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:40:1)


**כִּי מִן חָם הַיּוֹשְׁבִים שָׁם לְפָנִים.** בְּשֶׁקֶט וּבְשַׁלְוָה הָיוּ יוֹשְׁבִים, וְלֹא בָּא אָדָם לְהָרַע לָהֶם, לְפִיכָךְ לֹא נִזְהֲרוּ כְּשֶׁבָּאוּ עֲלֵיהֶם לְהִלָּחֵם; וְדוּגְמָתוֹ בְּשׁוֹפְטִים ״וַיָּבֹאוּ עַל לַיִשׁ עַל עַם שֹׁקֵט וּבֹטֵחַ וְגוֹ׳, וְדָבָר אֵין לָהֶם עִם אָדָם״ (שופטים יח:כז-כח) – לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ שְׁקֵטִים, לֹא הָיוּ יְרֵאִים וְלֹא כָּרְתוּ בְּרִית עִם שׁוּם אָדָם לְעָזְרָם, לְפִיכָךְ נִצְּחוּם אוֹתָם חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת; וְגַם כְּתִיב לְשָׁם ״כְּמִשְׁפַּט צִידֹנִים שֹׁקֵט וּבֹטֵחַ וְגוֹ׳״ (שופטים יח:ז):


**for the dwellers of yore were from Ham** They dwelt in peace and tranquility, and no one came to harm them; therefore, they did not beware when they came upon them to fight, and an example of this is found in Judges (18:27): “... and they came to Laish, to a people tranquil and secure etc.,” (verse 7): “... and they had no bond with any man.” Because they were tranquil, they were not afraid, and they did not make a pact with anyone to help them; therefore, five hundred men defeated them. It is also written above (verse 7): “... after the manner of the Zidonians, tranquil and secure, etc.”



### Verse 43

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 4:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/4:43:1)


**וַיַּכּוּ אֶת שְׁאֵרִית הַפְּלֵיטָה.** מֵהַכְרָתַת דָּוִד, כִּי דָוִד ״הִכְרִית כָּל זָכָר בֶּאֱדוֹם״ (מלכים א יא:טז):


**And they smote the remnant... that had escaped** from being annihilated by David, for David destroyed every male in Edom.



## Chapter 5


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:1:1)


**וּבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן בְּכוֹר יִשְׂרָאֵל.** וְאִם תֹּאמַר: אִם כֵּן שֶׁהָיָה בְּכוֹר, לָמָּה לֹא יָצָא מִמֶּנּוּ מַלְכוּת? **כִּי הוּא** בְּוַדַּאי **הַבְּכוֹר וּבְחַלְּלוֹ יְצוּעֵי אָבִיו נִתְּנָה בְּכֹרָתוֹ לִבְנֵי יוֹסֵף**. וְאִם תֹּאמַר: אִם כֵּן, לָמָּה לֹא מָלְכוּ בְּנֵי יוֹסֵף? לְכָךְ אָמַר: **וְלֹא לְהִתְיַחֵשׂ לַבְּכֹרָה** – חֵלֶק בְּכוֹרוֹת וְלֹא בְּכוֹר (בראשית רבה פב:יא). כִּי יְהוּדָה, אֲפִלּוּ לֹא חִלֵּל רְאוּבֵן הַבְּכוֹרָה וְלֹא נִלְקְחָה מִמֶּנּוּ, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי הָיָה הָגוּן יְהוּדָה לַמֶּלֶךְ:


**And the sons of Reuben the firstborn of Israel** Now if you ask: since he was the firstborn, why did the kingship not emanate from him? He was indeed the firstborn, but when he desecrated his father’s bed, his birthright was given to the sons of Joseph. Now if you ask, if so, why did the sons of Joseph not reign? He therefore says, “but not to be reckoned in the genealogy as the first born,” [i.e., the sons of Joseph were not meant] to take the share of the kingship, but only the birthright, because Judah was the most fit to reign even if Reuben had not defiled the birthright and it had not been taken from him.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:2:1)


**כִּי יְהוּדָה גָּבַר בְּאֶחָיו.** שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״גּוּר אַרְיֵה יְהוּדָה״ (בראשית מט:ט):


**Because Judah prevailed over his brothers** as it says (Gen. 49:9): “Judah is a lion’s cub.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:2:2)


**וְלְנָגִיד מִמֶּנּוּ.** שֶׁיָּצָא מִמֶּנּוּ מַלְכוּת; וְעַל שֵׁם כָּךְ נִקְרָא הַמֶּלֶךְ נָגִיד, שֶׁהוּא הַמּוֹצִיא וְהַמֵּבִיא. וְהַבְּכוֹרָה וְהַמַּלְכוּת נִטְּלָה מֵרְאוּבֵן וְנִתְּנָה הַמַּלְכוּת לִיהוּדָה, כְּדִכְתִיב **כִּי יְהוּדָה גָּבַר בְּאֶחָיו**, אֲבָל חֵלֶק **הַבְּכוֹרָה** נִתְּנָה **לְיוֹסֵף** לִשְׁנֵי בָנָיו:


**and the one appointed as prince was to be from him** the kingship emanated from him, and for this reason the king was called נָגִיד, since he is the one who leads [the people] out and brings them in [the Aramaic root נגד means to pull or lead.] And the birthright and the throne were taken from Reuben, and the throne was given to Judah, as it is written: “Because Judah prevailed over his brothers”; but the share of the birthright was given to Joseph, to his two sons.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:3:1)


**בְּנֵי רְאוּבֵן בְּכוֹר יִשְׂרָאֵל.** מַתְחִיל עַתָּה כְּבָרִאשׁוֹנָה:


**The sons of Reuben the firstborn of Israel** He now commences as before.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:4:1)


**בְּנֵי יוֹאֵל.** דּוֹרוֹת דּוֹרוֹת קָחָשִׁיב:


**The sons of Joel** He enumerates various generations.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:6:1)


**אֲשֶׁר הֶגְלָה תִּלְּגַת פִּלְנְאֶסֶר.** קוֹדֶם לְסַנְחֵרִיב; וְכֵן הָיָה סִדְרָן: ״פּוּל״ (מלכים ב טו:יט) הָיָה כְּשֶׁמָּלַךְ מְנַחֵם בֶּן גָּדִי, וּבִימֵי פֶּקַח בֶּן רְמַלְיָה בָּא **תִּגְלַת פִּלְנְאֶסֶר מֶלֶךְ אַשֻּׁר** (מלכים ב טו:כט), וּבִימֵי הוֹשֵׁעַ בֶּן אֵלָה בָּא ״שַׁלְמַנְאֶסֶר מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר״ (מלכים ב יז:ג), וּבִימֵי חִזְקִיָּהוּ – סַנְחֵרִיב (מלכים ב יח:יג). וּפֵרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (סנהדרין צד.): פּוּל – וְכֻלָּם סַנְחֵרִיב:


**whom Tillegath Pilneser exiled** He preceded Sannecherib, and this was their sequence: Pul was [king] when Menahem the son of Gadi was crowned (II Kings 15:19f.), and (ibid. verse 29): “...in the days of Pekah the son of Remaliah, Tiglath Pileser the king of Assyria came,” and in the days of Hoshea the son of Elah, Shalmaneser the king of Assyria came (ibid. 17:3), and in the days of Hezekiah was Sannecherib (ibid. 18:13), but our Rabbis explained (Sanh. 94a) that Pul and all the rest of them are identical with Sannecherib.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:6:2)


**הוּא נָשִׂיא לָרְאוּבֵנִי.** בְּאוֹתוֹ זְמַן שֶׁהִגְלָהוּ **תִּלְּגַת פִּלְנְאֶסֶר**:


**he was the prince of the Reubenites** at the time that Tiglath Pileser exiled him.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:7:1)


**בְּהִתְיַחֵשׂ לְתוֹלְדוֹתָם הָרֹאשׁ יְעִיאֵל וּזְכַרְיָהוּ.** פִּתְרוֹן: הֲרֵי לֹא יִחַסְתִּי לְךָ מִן רְאוּבֵן כִּי אִם קְצָת, וְעִקַּר יִחוּסוֹ בִּמְקוֹם יִחוּסוֹ עַל דִּבְרֵי הַיָּמִים לְמַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל הָיָה יְעִיאֵל וּזְכַרְיָה הָרֹאשׁ, שֶׁעַל יָדָן כָּלָה יִחוּסָן:


**tracing their genealogy to their generations; the heads were Jeiel and Zechariah** The meaning is: I traced only part of the genealogy of Reuben, but the main part of his genealogy is in the chronicles of the kings of Israel. Jeiel and Zechariah were the heads, through whom their genealogy was completed.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:8:1)


**וְעַד נְבוֹ וּבַעַל מְעוֹן.** שֶׁבְּיָמִים קַדְמוֹנִים, קוֹדֶם שֶׁבָּאוּ לִידֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, נִקְרְאוּ נְבוֹ וּבַעַל מְעוֹן עַל שֵׁם תּוֹעֲבוֹתָם; כִּי כֵן הָיָה מִנְהַג עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת עַד הַיּוֹם הַהוּא, לִקְרֹאת תּוֹעֲבוֹתֵיהֶם בְּשֵׁם עֲיָירוֹתֵיהֶם, אֲבָל כְּשֶׁבָּאוּ לִידֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, הֵסֵבּוּ שְׁמוֹתָם, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וְאֶת נְבוֹ וְאֶת בַּעַל מְעוֹן מוּסַבּוֹת שֵׁם״ (במדבר לב:לח) – כְּלוֹמַר: יִשְׂרָאֵל הֵסֵבּוּ שְׁמוֹתָם; ״וְאֶת שִׂבְמָה״ (במדבר לב:לח) – לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא נִקְרְאָה עַל שֵׁם תּוֹעֲבוֹתֶיהָ לֹא הֵסֵבּוּ שְׁמָהּ; וּבִמְגִלָּה מְפָרֵשׁ (מגלה כה:), כִּי נְבוֹ וּבַעַל מְעוֹן שֵׁם עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים הֵם:


**and until Nebo and Baal Meon** For in the ancient days, before they came into Israel’s hands, they were called Nebo and Baal Meon after their pagan deities, (lit. their abominations), for it was the custom of the heathens to call their pagan deities after their cities until that day. But when they came into Israel’s hands, they changed their names, as it is written (Num. 32:38): “Nebo and Baal Meon their names being changed,” i.e., Israel changed their names, but Sibmah, since it was not named for their pagan deities, they did not change its name. In *Megillah* (unknown) it is explained that Nebo and Baal Meon are names of pagan deities.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:10:1)


**עִם הַהַגְרִאִים.** הֵם הַיִּשְׁמְעֵאלִים; וְעַל שֵׁם אִמָּם קוֹרְאָם הַגְרִאִים, בְּנֵי הָגָר הָיוּ:


**with the Hagarites** with the Ishmaelites, and by their mother’s name he calls them Hagarites, because they were the sons of Hagar.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:10:2)


**וַיֵּשְׁבוּ בְּאָהֳלֵיהֶם.** שֶׁכֵּן מִנְהַג עַרְבִיִּים לֵישֵׁב בָּאֹהָלִים:


**and they dwelt in their tents** for it is the custom of the Arabs to dwell in tents.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:11:1)


**וּבְנֵי גָד לְנֶגְדָּם יָשְׁבוּ.** אֶצְלָם, כְּמוֹ ״וְאֵלְכָה לְנֶגְדֶּךָ״ (בראשית לג:יב):


**And the sons of Gad dwelt opposite them** alongside them, like (Gen. 33:12): “...and I will go alongside you (לְנֶגְדֶּךָ).”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:13:1)


**וַאֲחֵיהֶם לְבֵית אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם.** בִּמְקוֹם יִחוּסָן:


**And their brothers to the house of their fathers** in the place of their genealogy.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:13:2)


**מִיכָאֵל וּמְשֻׁלָּם.** אֵלּוּ הָיוּ רָאשֵׁי אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם שֶׁל אוֹתוֹ הַיַּחַס, כִּי **כֻּלָּם הִתְיַחֲשׂוּ בִּימֵי יוֹתָם מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה וּבִימֵי יָרָבְעָם מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל**:


**Michael and Meshullam** These were the heads of their fathers of that genealogy, for they all traced their genealogy in the days of Jotham the king of Judah and in the days of Jeroboam the king of Israel.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:18:1)


**בְּנֵי רְאוּבֵן וְגָד וַחֲצִי שֵׁבֶט מְנַשֶּׁה מִן בְּנֵי חַיִל וְגוֹ׳.** כְּלוֹמַר: כֻּלָּם נִתְקַבְּצוּ וְ**עָשׂוּ מִלְחָמָה עִם הַהַגְרִיאִים**:


**The sons of Reuben, and the Gadites, and half the tribe of Manasseh, of men of valor, etc.** That is to say: they all gathered and made war with the Hagarites.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:19:1)


**וִיטוּר וְנָפִישׁ.** בָּנָיו שֶׁל יִשְׁמָעֵאל הֵם:


**and Jetur and Naphish** They were sons of Ishmael (Gen. 25:14).



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:20:1)


**וַיֵּעָזְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶם.** כִּי ה׳ עֲזָרָם:


**And they were assisted against them** for the Lord helped them.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:20:2)


**וַיִּנָּתְנוּ בְיָדָם הַהַגְרִיאִים וְכֹל שֶׁעִמָּהֶם.** אוּמּוֹת אֲחֵרִים לְעָזְרָם:


**and the Hagarites and all who were with them** other nations [that came] to their aid.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:20:3)


**וְנַעְתּוֹר לָהֶם.** דֻּגְמַת ״וַיֵּעָתֶר לָהֶם ה׳״ (בראשית כה:כא):


**and He accepted their prayers** וְנַעְתּוֹר, similar to if וַיֵּעָתֵר לוֹ accepted his prayer (Gen. 25:21).



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:25:1)


**וַיִּזְנוּ אַחֲרֵי אֱלֹהֵי עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ אֲשֶׁר הִשְׁמִיד אֱלֹהִים מִפְּנֵיהֶם.** פִּתְרוֹן: הֵם זָנוּ אַחֲרֵי אֱלֹהֵי עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ, וְאֵיזֶה עַם? אֲשֶׁר הִשְׁמִידָם אֱלֹהִים מִפְּנֵיהֶם; שֶׁאֱלֹהֵיהֶם לֹא הָיָה יָכוֹל לְעוֹזְרָם; וְלֹא שָׂמוּ עַל לִבָּם אַחֲרֵי הִשְׁמִידָם מִפְּנֵיהֶם: וְלֹא הָיָה יָכוֹל לְעָזְרָם, וְלָנוּ כְּמוֹ כֵן לֹא יַעֲזוֹר:


**and they strayed after the gods of the peoples of the land whom God had destroyed from before them** The meaning is, they went astray after the gods of the peoples of the land. And which people? [Those] whom God had destroyed from before them [Israel], whose god could not help them [the pagans]. And they [Israel] did not ponder the matter, [reasoning] that since He destroyed them [the pagans] from before them [Israel], and it [their god] could not help them, it would certainly not be able to help us.

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:36:1)


**הוּא אֲשֶׁר כִּהֵן בַּבַּיִת אֲשֶׁר בָּנָה שְׁלֹמֹה.** יֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים: **עֲזַרְיָה** הוּא הָיָה עֲזַרְיָה הָרִאשׁוֹן, וּמֵעֲזַרְיָה עַד שְׂרָיָה שֶׁהָיָה בְּחֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת שְׁמֹנָה כֹּהֲנִים; וּמִכָּאן הֻגְּהוּ בְּיוֹמָא בְּפֶרֶק רִאשׁוֹן (יומא ט.) וּבְוַיִּקְרָא רַבָּה (ויקרא רבה כא,ט) וּבְסִפְרִי פָּרָשַׁת פִּנְחָס (ספרי קלא): שְׁמוֹנָה כֹּהֲנִים שִׁמְּשׁוּ בַּבַּיִת הָרִאשׁוֹן; וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים שְׁמוֹנָה עָשָׂר. וְלֹא זֶה וְזֶה יֵשׁ בָּהֶם רוּחַ חַיִּים, כִּי צָדוֹק הוּא הָיָה הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁכִּהֵן בִּימֵי שְׁלֹמֹה; וְעוֹד, אֵיזֶה אֲמַרְיָה הָיָה בִּימֵי יְהוֹשָׁפָט, דִּכְתִיב ״וְהִנֵּה אֲמַרְיָהוּ כֹהֵן הָרֹאשׁ״ (דברי הימים ב יט,יא), וְהוּא (אֲמַרְיָה) בֶּן עֲזַרְיָהוּ שֶׁהָיָה בִּימֵי עֻזִּיָּהוּ, בִּימֵי חִזְקִיָּה, שֶׁהָיָה אַחֲרֵי יְהוֹשָׁפָט. וְאִם תֹּאמַר: עֲזַרְיָהוּ אֲבִי שְׂרָיָה הָיָה, וְהֵיכָן חִלְקִיָּהוּ הַכֹּהֵן הַגָּדוֹל שֶׁהָיָה בִּימֵי יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ? אֶלָּא מִצָּדוֹק יֵשׁ לַחְשֹׁב: שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר כֹּהֲנִים הָיוּ מִצָּדוֹק עַד שְׂרָיָה; וְכֵן מָצִינוּ בְּוַיִּקְרָא רַבָּא מְדֻיָּק. וּמַה שֶּׁאָמַר בַּעֲזַרְיָה הוּא אֲשֶׁר כִּהֵן בַּבַּיִת – מְפֹרָשׁ בְּסִפְרִי יְרוּשַׁלְמִי כָּךְ: וְכִי הוּא לְבַדּוֹ כִּהֵן בִּימֵי שְׁלֹמֹה (כהן), וַהֲלֹא גַּם שְׁאָר כֹּהֲנִים כְּמוֹ כֵן כִּהֲנוּ! אֶלָּא, לְפִי שֶׁמָּסַר נַפְשׁוֹ עַל קְדֻשַּׁת הַבַּיִת, שֶׁלֹּא הִנִּיחַ אֶת עֻזִּיָּהוּ לְהַקְטִיר, לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר: הוּא אֲשֶׁר כִּהֵן בַּבַּיִת אֲשֶׁר בָּנָה שְׁלֹמֹה; וְהוּא עֲזַרְיָה שֶׁהָיָה בִּימֵי עֻזִּיָה (דברי הימים ב כו:יח) וּבִימֵי חִזְקִיָּהוּ. וַעֲזַרְיָהוּ בֶּן אֲחִימַעַץ הוּא אֲמַרְיָהוּ שֶׁכִּהֵן בִּימֵי יְהוֹשָׁפָט, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּקְרָא עֻזִּיָּה עֲזַרְיָה, דִּכְתִיב בִּמְלָכִים ״בִּשְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים וָָָָָָשֶׁבַע שָׁנִים לְיָרָבְעָם וְגוֹ׳ מָלַךְ עֲזַרְיָה״ (מלכים ב טו:א). וְנִקְרָא עֲזַרְיָה בֶּן אֲחִימַעַץ זְכַרְיָהוּ, דִּכְתִיב ״וַיְהִי לִדְרֹשׁ אֱלֹהִים בִּימֵי זְכַרְיָהוּ הַמֵּבִין בְּיִרְאַת אֱלֹהִים וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב כו:ה):


**he is the one who served in the Temple that Solomon built** Some explain that Azariah was the first Azariah, and from Azariah to Seraiah, who lived at the time of the destruction of the Temple, were eight priests. Accordingly, they emended in *Yoma* in the first chapter (9a), and in *Leviticus Rabbah* (21:9), and in *Siphre*, *parashath Phineas* (Num. 25:12), that eight high priests served in the First Temple. Some say that there were eighteen. But neither one is a viable theory because Zadok was the first who ministered in the time of Solomon. Moreover, which Amariah was in the days of Jehoshaphat, about whom it is written (II Chron 19:11): “... and behold, Amariah the head priest”? Now he was Amariah the son of Azariah, who lived in the days of Uzziah and in the days of Hezekiah, who was after Jehoshaphat. If you say [that it was] Azariah the father of Seraiah, then where was Hilkiah the High Priest who lived in the days of Josiah? Rather we must count from Zadok, and there were twelve priests from Zadok until Seraiah, and so we find in an exact version of *Leviticus Rabbah*. Now that which the Scripture writes: “... he was the one who served in the Temple,” is explained in *Sefer Yerushalmi* (source unknown. Perhaps *Sifre Zuta, Korah* 18:7 is meant) as follows: Did he alone serve, and did he serve in the days of Solomon? Did not other priests serve as well? But because he risked his life for the sanctity of the Temple by not allowing Uzziah to burn incense, it is said: “he was the one who served in the Temple that Solomon built,” and he was the Azariah who lived in the days of Uzziah (ibid. 26: 17, 20) and in the days of Hezekiah, and Azariah the son of Ahimaaz was Amariah the priest who served in the days of Jehoshaphat, just as Uzziah is called Azariah, as is written (II Ki. 15:1): “In the twenty-seventh year of Jeroboam etc, Azariah... became king.” And Azariah the son of Ahimaaz was called Zechariah, as it is written (II Chron. 26:5): “And he was wont to seek God in the days of Zechariah who understood the fear of God, etc.”



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 5:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/5:41:1)


**וִיהוֹצָדָק הָלַךְ בְּהַגְלוֹת ה׳ אֶת יְהוּדָה וִירוּשָׁלִָם.** וּמַה שֶּׁאָמַר בְּסֵפֶר ״יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בֶּן יְהוֹצָדָק הַכֹּהֵן הַגָּדוֹל״ (חגי א:א), לֹא יְהוֹצָדָק שִׁמֵּשׁ מֵעוֹלָם בִּכְהֻנָּה גְדוֹלָה, דְּהָא הוּגְלָה לְבָבֶל בִּימֵי יְכָנְיָה, כְּדִכְתִיב וִיהוֹצָדָק הָלַךְ בְּהַגְלוֹת וְגוֹ׳, אֶלָּא יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בְּנוֹ הָיָה כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל כְּשֶׁעָלוּ מִבָּבֶל בְּבַיִת שֵׁנִי. וּמַה שֶּׁלֹּא הָיָה עֶזְרָא בֶּן שְׂרָיָה הַסּוֹפֵר כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל, וּבֶן אָחִיו יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בֶּן יְהוֹצָדָק כִּהֵן, זֶהוּ הַטַּעַם, לְפִי כִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ עָלָה עִם זְרֻבָּבֶל כַּמָּה יָמִים וְשָׁנִים קֹדֶם שֶׁעָלָה עֶזְרָא:


**And Jehozadak went when the Lord exiled Judah and Jerusalem** And the verse in the Book of Haggai (1:1): “Joshua the son of Jehozadak, the [High] Priest,” does not [mean] that Jehozadak ever served in the high priesthood, for he was exiled to Babylon in the days of Jeconiah, as it is written: “And Jehozadak went when... exiled etc.,” but Joshua his son was the High Priest when they ascended from Babylon during the time of the Second Temple. Now why was Azariah the son of Seraiah the scholar not the High Priest, but [instead] his nephew Joshua the son of Jehozadak? This is the reason: because Joshua ascended with Zerubbabel many days and years before Ezra ascended.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:13:1)


**וּבְנֵי שְׁמוּאֵל הַבְּכוֹר וַשְׁנִי וַאֲבִיָּה.** וְשָׁנִי זֶה ״יוֹאֵל״ (שמואל א ח:ב). פִּתְרוֹן: וְהַשֵּׁנִי אֲבִיָּה, כְּלוֹמַר: שֵׁנִי לָרִאשׁוֹן הוּא הָיָה אֲבִיָּה, וְשֵׁנִי לַאֲבִיָּה הוּא הָיָה יוֹאֵל שֶׁלֹּא נִכְתַּב; וְדוּגְמָתָם ״כִּי אִם הַסּוּס אָסוּר וַחֲמוֹר אָסוּר״ (מלכים ב ז:י) – כְּמוֹ וְהַחֲמוֹר, וְכֵן ״הַפָּר שֵׁנִי״ דְּגִדְעוֹן (שופטים ו:כו), וְכֵן הָרִאשׁוֹן (שופטים ו:כה):


**And the sons of Samuel: the firstborn was Vashni, and Abiah** This Abiah was Joel (below v. 18, I Sam. 8:2), or the meaning is: and the second one (וְהַשֵּׁנִי) was Abiah, i.e., the second to the first was Abiah, and the second to Abiah was Joel, whose name was not written. A similar case [in which the ה הַיְדִיעָה, the definite article, is omitted] is (II Kings 7:10): “... but the horses are tethered and the donkeys (וַחֲמוֹר) are tethered,” like וְהַחֲמוֹר ; and similarly (Jud. 6:25): “and the second bull (הַפָּר הַשֵּׁנִי)” similarly, “the first (הָרִאשׁוֹן).”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:16:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה אֲשֶׁר הֶעֱמִיד דָּוִד וְגוֹ׳.** כְּשֶׁהֱשִׁיבוּהוּ מִבֵּית עוֹבֵד אֱדוֹם, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וַיַּעֲזֹב שָׁם לִפְנֵי אֲרוֹן בְּרִית ה׳ לְאָסָף וּלְאֶחָיו לְשָׁרֵת לִפְנֵי הָאָרוֹן וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים א טז:לז), וּכְתִיב ״וְעֹבֵד אֱדֹם... בֶּן יְדוּתוּן וְחֹסָה לְשֹׁעֲרִים וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים א טז:לח). לִפְנֵי מִשְׁכַּן ה׳ – בַּבָּמָה אֲשֶׁר בְּגִבְעוֹן, וּשְׁלֹמֹה הֶעֱמִידָן בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**And these are the ones that David stationed, etc.** when they returned it from the house of Obed Edom, as it is written (below 16:37): “And he left there before the Ark of the Covenant of the Lord, Asaph and his brothers to serve before the Ark constantly, etc.” And it is written (verse 38f.): “... and Obed Edom the son of Jeduthun and Hosah as gate sentries before the Tabernacle of the Lord in the high place that was in Gibeon, and Solomon stationed them in the Temple in Jerusalem.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:17:1)


**וַיַּעַמְדוּ כְמִשְׁפָּטָם עַל עֲבוֹדָתָם.** כַּמִּשְׁפָּט אֲשֶׁר הֶעֱמִידָן דָּוִד:


**according to their custom** according to the custom to which David had stationed them.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:24:1)


**וְאָחִיו אָסָף הָעוֹמֵד עַל יְמִינוֹ.** שֶׁל הֵימָן וּבָנָיו (לעיל יח):


**And his brother Asaph, who stood at his right** [at the right] of Heman and his sons.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:29:1)


**וּבְנֵי מְרָרִי אֲחֵיהֶם עַל הַשְּׂמֹאוּל.** (אֲחֵיהֶם) דּוּגְמַת שֶׁאָמַר בִּלְשׁוֹן אַשְׁכְּנַ״ז גִּיבְרוּדִי״ר, **עַל הַשְּׂמֹאל** שֶׁל הֵימָן וּבָנָיו שֶׁהֵימָן וּבָנָיו בָּאֶמְצַע:


**And the sons of Merari their brethren were on the left** like what is said (in German, gebreder). On the left of Heman and his sons, for Heman and his sons were in the middle.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:33:1)


**וַאֲחֵיהֶם הַלְוִיִּם נְתוּנִים לְכָל עֲבוֹדַת מִשְׁכַּן בֵּית הָאֱלֹהִים.** לְשׁוֹעֲרִים וּלְהַפְשִׁיט, כְּדִכְתִיב לְקַמָּן ״וְהַלְוִיִּם מַפְשִׁיטִין״ (דברי הימים ב לה:יא):


**And their brethren the Levites were appointed for the entire service of the Tabernacle of the House of God** for gate sentries and to flay [the sacrificial animals], as is written further: (II Chron. 35: 11): “...and the Levites were flaying.”



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:35:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן.** הַמַּקְטִירִים עַל מִזְבַּח הָעוֹלָה וְעַל מִזְבַּח הַקְּטֹרֶת:


**And these are the sons of Aaron** who were offering on the altar of the burnt offering and on the altar of the incense.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:38:1)


**צָדוֹק בְּנוֹ אֲחִימַעַץ בְּנוֹ.** לֹא חָשַׁב כִּי אִם אוֹתָם שֶׁהָיוּ עַד שְׁלֹמֹה (שמואל ב טו:כז):


**Zadok his son, Ahimaaz his son** He enumerates only those who were until Solomon.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:39:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה מוֹשְׁבוֹתָם לְטִירוֹתָם בִּגְבוּלָם וְגוֹ׳.** עַד **כִּי לָהֶם הָיָה הַגּוֹרָל** לַלְוִיִּם וְלַכֹּהֲנִים מִנַּחֲלָתָם, כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל הִפִּילוּ גּוֹרָלוֹת עַל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל כַּמָּה מֵאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל יָבֹא לָהֶם וְלַכֹּהֲנִים וְלַלְוִיִּם, וַיָּבֹא לִיהוּדָה הַגּוֹרָל מִתְּחִלָּה; וְזֶהוּ **כִּי לָהֶם הָיָה הַגּוֹרָל** בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה, לָתֵת לָהֶם אוֹתָן עֲיָירוֹת שֶׁכָּתוּב כָּאן:


**And these were their settlements for their palaces in their border, etc.** until “for theirs was the lot,” i.e., for the Levites and for the priests. All Israel cast lots [to determine] how much of the land of Israel would go to them and to the priests and to the Levites, and the lot fell to Judah first. That is the meaning of “for theirs was the lot”; they were the first to give them the towns mentioned here.



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:41:1)


**נָתְנוּ לְכָלֵב.** כִּי מֹשֶׁה נָתַן לוֹ (יהושע יד:ט):


**they gave to Caleb** because Moses gave it to him.



### Verse 45

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:45:1)


**וּמִמַּטֵּה בִנְיָמִין אֶת גֶּבַע.** כִּי הַגּוֹרָל נָפַל לְבִנְיָמִין לָתֵת אֵלּוּ הָעֲיָירוֹת:


**and to the tribe of Benjamin, Geba** because the lot fell to Benjamin to give these towns.



### Verse 46

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:46:1)


**וְלִבְנֵי קְהָת הַנּוֹתָרִים.** כִּי הַכֹּהֲנִים יָצְאוּ מִקְּהָת, וּכְבָר אָמְרוּ (לעיל מ) מַה שֶּׁנָּתְנוּ לַכֹּהֲנִים בְּנֵי קְהָת:


**And to the sons of Kehath who remained** for the priests came from Kehath, and they already said what they gave to the priests, the sons of Kehath.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:46:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:46:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:46:2)


**וְלִבְנֵי קְהָת הַנּוֹתָרִים מִמִּשְׁפַּחַת הַמַּטֶּה.** שֶׁל לֵוִי:


**And to the sons of Kehath who remained from the family of the tribe** of Levi.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:46:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:46:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:46:3)


**מִמַּחֲצִית מַטֵּה חֲצִי מְנַשֶּׁה בַּגּוֹרָל עָרִים עָשֶׂר.** וְלַחֲצִי שֵׁבֶט הַמְּנַשֶּׁה נָפַל הַגּוֹרָל לִיתֵּן לִבְנֵי קְהָת הַלְוִיִּם עָרִים עֶשֶׂר:


**from half the tribe of Manasseh, by lot ten cities** And to half the tribe of Manasseh, the lot fell to give the sons of Kehath, the Levites, ten cities.



### Verse 50

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:50:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:50:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:50:1)


**וַיִּתְּנוּ בַגּוֹרָל מִמַּטֵּה בְנֵי יְהוּדָה וּמִמַּטֵּה בְנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן.** אַף־עַל־פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הִזְכִּיר שִׁמְעוֹן בִּנְתִינָתָן, הוּא נָתַן חֶלְקוֹ מַה שֶּׁנָּתַן לוֹ יְהוּדָה; וְגַם יְהוּדָה אֵינוֹ נִזְכָּר לְמַעְלָה בְּעִקַּר הַנְּתִינָה, וְכָל מַה שֶּׁמַּזְכִּיר לְמַעְלָה מִ**וַיִּתְּנוּ לָהֶם אֶת חֶבְרוֹן** עַד **וּמִמַּטֵּה בִנְיָמִין** – הַכֹּל נָתְנוּ בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה וּבְנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן. וּלְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הִזְכִּירוֹ לְמַעְלָה, מַזְכִּירוֹ עַתָּה כָּאן:


**And they gave by lot from the tribe of the sons of Judah and from the tribe of the sons of Simeon** Though he did not mention Simeon [in the preceding verses], as giving cities, it gave its share from what Judah gave it. Neither is Judah mentioned above regarding the giving itself, and whatever he mentioned above from (v. 40): “And they gave them Hebron” until (v. 48): “And from the tribe of Benjamin,” the sons of Judah and the sons of Simeon gave; and since he did not mention it above, he mentions it here now.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:50:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:50:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:50:2)


**אֲשֶׁר יִקְרְאוּ אֶתְהֶם בְּשֵׁמוֹת.** כְּלוֹמַר: אֲשֶׁר קָרָאנוּ אוֹתָם בְּשֵׁמוֹת לְמַעְלָה. לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה **בִנְיָמִין** חָבֵר עִמָּהֶם לִבְנֵי אַהֲרֹן, מַזְכִּירוֹ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהִזְכִּירוֹ כְּבָר:


**that they called by names** That is to say, that they were called by names previously. Since [the tribe of] Benjamin was friendly with the sons of Aaron, [Scripture] mentions them here, even though it had mentioned them previously.



### Verse 51

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:51:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:51:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:51:1)


**וּמִמִּשְׁפְּחוֹת בְּנֵי קְהָת וַיְהִי עָרֵי וְגוֹ׳.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנָּתְנוּ לָהֶם חֲצִי שֵׁבֶט מְנַשֶּׁה, נָתְנוּ לָהֶם גַּם אֶפְרַיִם עַל פִּי הַגּוֹרָל:


**And from the families of the sons of Kehath** the cities of... were Although half the tribe of Manasseh gave to them, Ephraim also gave to them by lot.



### Verse 55

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:55:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:55:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:55:1)


**וּמִמַּחֲצִית מַטֵּה מְנַשֶּׁה.** עַד חֲצִי מְנַשֶּׁה שֶׁבְּעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן שֶׁבְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**And from half the tribe of Manasseh, etc.** until “half the tribe of Manasseh” that was in trans-Jordan and in the land of Israel.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:55:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:55:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:55:2)


**לְמִשְׁפַּחַת לִבְנֵי קְהָת הַנּוֹתָרִים.** הֵם הַלְוִיִּם. וּפִתְרוֹן: הַכֹּל נָתְנוּ אֶפְרַיִם וּמְנַשֶּׁה לְאוֹתוֹ הַנּוֹתָר שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה **וְלִבְנֵי קְהָת הַנּוֹתָרִים מִמִּשְׁפְּחוֹת הַמַּטֶּה**:


**to the family of the remaining sons of Kehath** They are the Levites, and the meaning is that Ephraim and Manasseh gave everything to that remnant mentioned above (verse 46): “And to the sons of Kehath who remained from the family of the tribe.”



### Verse 56

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:56:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:56:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:56:1)


**לִבְנֵי גֵרְשׁוֹם מִמִּשְׁפַּחַת חֲצִי מַטֵּה מְנַשֶּׁה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנָּתְנוּ לִבְנֵי קְהָת עַל פִּי הַגּוֹרָל, נָפַל גַּם לָהֶם הַגּוֹרָל לָתֵת כְּמוֹ כֵן לְגֵרְשׁוֹם:


**To the sons of Gershom, from the family of half the tribe of Manasseh** Although they gave to the sons of Kehath by lot, the lot fell to them also to give likewise to the sons of Gershom.



### Verse 62

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 6:62:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:62:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/6:62:1)


**לִבְנֵי מְרָרִי הַנּוֹתָרִים מִמַּטֵּה זְבוּלֻן.** כְּלוֹמַר, שֶׁלֹּא הִגִּיעַ לָהֶם אוֹתָם שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה עֲיָירוֹת שֶׁנָּתְנוּ לָהֶם רְאוּבֵן וְגָד וּזְבוּלֻן; וְזֶהוּ הַנּוֹתָרִים מִמַּטֵּה זְבוּלֻן – מֵחֵלֶק זְבוּלֻן נָתְנוּ לִבְנֵי מְרָרִי הַנּוֹתָרִים:


**To the sons of Merari who remained, from the tribe of Zebulun** i.e., those who did not receive those twelve cities that Reuben, Gad, and Zebulun gave them, and this is the meaning of “who remained from the tribe of Zebzulun”: i.e., from the share of Zebulun, they [the tribe of Naphtali] gave to the remaining sons of Merari.



## Chapter 7


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:1:1)


**וְלִבְנֵי יִשָּׂשכָר תּוֹלָע וּפוּאָה יָשׁוּב וְשִׁמְרוֹן.** בַּחוּמָּשׁ כְּתִיב ״וְיוֹב״ (בראשית מו:יג)! ״יוֹב״ הָיָה שְׁמוֹ, וּלְפִי שֶׁנִּתְיַשְּׁבוּ לִלְמוֹד תּוֹרָה, דִּכְתִיב ״מִבְּנֵי יִשָּׂשכָר יוֹדְעֵי בִינָה לַעִתִּים״ (דברי הימים א יב:לג), לְכָךְ זָכָה וְנִקְרָא יָשׁוּב:


**And of the sons of Issachar: Tola, and Puah, and Jashub, and Shimron** In the Pentateuch (Gen. 46:13) it is written: “and Job.” Job was his name [originally], but since they settled themselves (נִתְיַשְּׁבוּ) to learn Torah, as it is written (below 12:33): “And of the sons of Issachar, who possessed understanding of the times,” he merited and was called Jashub (יָשוּב).



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:2:1)


**וּבְנֵי תוֹלָע. מִסְפָּרָם בִּימֵי דָוִיד עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁנַיִם אֶלֶף וְשֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת**. כָּל אֵלֶּה בְּנֵי תוֹלָע לְבַד מִ**בְּנֵי עֻזִּי**:


**And the sons of Tola, etc.** “Their number in David’s time was twenty-two thousand and six hundred.” All these were the sons of Tola, aside from the sons of Uzzi.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:3:1)


**וּבְנֵי עֻזִּי וְגוֹ׳ שְׁלֹשִׁים וְשִׁשָּׁה אֶלֶף כִּי הִרְבּוּ נָשִׁים וּבָנִים.** וְכָל אֵלֶּה הָיוּ לְבַד מֵאוֹתָן **עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁנַיִם אֶלֶף וְשֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת** שֶׁל בְּנֵי תוֹלָע:


**And the sons of Uzzi, etc.** “thirty-six thousand for they had many wives and sons.” And all these were aside from those twenty-two thousand and six hundred of the sons of Tola.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:5:1)


**וַאֲחֵיהֶם לְכֹל מִשְׁפְּחוֹת.** בֵּין **בְּנֵי תוֹלָע** בֵּין **בְּנֵי עֻזִּי** בֵּין כָּל **גִּבּוֹרֵי** הַחֲיָלִים שֶׁל דָּוִד – **שְׁמוֹנִים וְשִׁבְעָה אֶלֶף**:


**And their brethren of all the families** both of the sons of Tola, both of the sons of Uzzi, both all David’s mighty warriors were eighty-seven thousand.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:5:2)


**הִתְיַחְשָׂם לַכֹּל.** פִּתְרוֹן: לְכָל הַיַּחַס שֶׁלָּהֶם:


**all having genealogical records** This means: according to all their genealogical records.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:6:1)


**בִּנְיָמִן בֶּלַע וָבֶכֶר וִידִיעֲאֵל.** זֶה שֶׁקּוֹרֵא בְּסֵפֶר הַיָּשָׁר ״אַשְׁבֵּל״ (בראשית מו:כא):


**Benjamin: Bela, and Becher, and Jediael** This is the one called Ashbel in the “Book of the Just.” (Gen. 46:21).



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:12:1)


**וְשֻׁפִּים וְחֻפִּים בְּנֵי עִיר חֻשִׁם בְּנֵי אַחֵר.** עֶזְרָא שֶׁכָּתַב סֵפֶר הַיַּחַס הַזֶּה לֹא הָיָה יוֹדֵעַ אִם הָיוּ מִבְּנֵי בִנְיָמִין אִם לָאו, לְפִיכָךְ כְּתָבָם לְבַדָּם; וְגַם מִשּׁוּם דִּכְתִיב **וּמָכִיר לָקַח אִשָּׁה לְחֻפִּים** – מְסַפְּקָא לֵיהּ:


**And Shuppim and Huppim, the sons of Ir, Hushim, the sons of Aher** Ezra, who wrote this book of genealogy, did not know whether they were of the sons of Benjamin or not. He therefore listed them alone. He was also in doubt because it is written: “And Machir took a wife of Huppim.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:13:1)


**בְּנֵי נַפְתָּלִי יַחֲצִיאֵל וְגוֹ׳.** מַה שֶּׁלֹּא נִתְיַחֵס יוֹתֵר, זֶהוּ הַטַּעַם, כְּמְפֹרָשׁ בִּשְׁלָהֵי מְגִלַּת יְרוּשַׁלְמִי (תענית ד:): שָׁלֹשׁ סְפָרִים מָצָא עֶזְרָא, וְכָל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד מִן הַיַּחַס, וּמַה שֶּׁמָּצָא כָּתַב, וּמַה שֶּׁלֹּא מָצָא לֹא כָּתַב, וּמִן בְּנֵי נַפְתָּלִי לֹא מָצָא יוֹתֵר. וְזֶהוּ הַטַּעַם, שֶׁכָּל הַיַּחַס הַזֶּה מְיַחֵד בְּדִלּוּגִים, כִּי דִּלֵּג מִסֵּפֶר זֶה אֶל סֵפֶר זֶה וְחִבְּרָם, וּמַה שֶּׁלֹּא הָיָה יָכוֹל לִכְתֹּב בְּסֵפֶר זֶה, כָּתַב בְּסֵפֶר עֶזְרָא. תֵּדַע, כִּי נֶאֱמַר בְּסָמוּךְ ״וְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל הִתְיַחֲשׂוּ וְהִנָּם כְּתוּבִים עַל סֵפֶר מַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיהוּדָה הָגְלוּ לְבָבֶל״ (דברי הימים א ט:א) – פִּתְרוֹן: אִם רְצוֹנְךָ לֵידַע יַחַס עֲשֶׂרֶת שְׁבָטִים, לֵךְ לְחֲלַח וְחָבוֹר נְהַר גּוֹזָן וְעָרֵי מָדַי (מלכים ב יז:ו), כִּי סֵפֶר דִּבְרֵי הַיָּמִים שֶׁלָּהֶם גָּלָה עִמָּהֶם, אֲבָל יְהוּדָה מָצָאתִי סִפְרָן בְּבָבֶל וּמַה שֶּׁמָּצָאתִי כָּתַבְתִּי:


**The sons of Naphtali: Jahziel, etc.** And why was no more of his genealogy traced? This is the reason, as explained at the end of *Megillah Yerushalmi*0 (see Com. Dig. 8:29): Ezra found three books, and each one of them contained part of the genealogy; that which he found he did not write, and of the sons of Naphtali he did not find any more. For this reason, the entire genealogy is traced with omissions, because he skipped from one book to another and combined them. And that which he could not write in this Book, he wrote in the Book of Ezra. You should know [that this is true] because it is written close by (9:1): “And all Israel traced their genealogy, and behold they are written in the book of the kings of Israel, and Judah was exiled to Babylon because of its treachery.” The meaning is that if you wish to know the genealogy of the Ten Tribes, go to Halah and Habor, the Gozan River, and the cities of Media, because their annals were exiled with them, but as for Judah I found their records in Babylon, and that which I found, I wrote.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:14:1)


**בְּנֵי מְנַשֶּׁה אַשְׂרִיאֵל אֲשֶׁר יָלְדָה.** פֵּירוּשׁ: אֲשֶׁר יָלְדָה לוֹ אִשְׁתּוֹ; דּוּגְמָא ״אֲשֶׁר יָלְדָה אֹתָהּ לְלֵוִי בְּמִצְרָיִם״ (במדבר כו:נט); אֲבָל **פִּילַגְשׁוֹ הָאֲרַמִּיָּה יָלְדָה אֶת מָכִיר**:


**The sons of Manasseh: Asriel whom she bore** The meaning is: whom his wife bore to him. A similar case is (Num. 26:59): “... whom she bore to Levi in Egypt.” But his Aramean concubine bore Machir.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:21:1)


**וּבְנֵי אֶפְרַיִם שׁוּתֶלַח וְגוֹ׳ וַהֲרָגוּם אַנְשֵׁי גַת וְגוֹ׳ כִּי יָרְדוּ לָקַחַת אֶת מִקְנֵיהֶם.** וְלֹא הָיוּ בְּקִיאִים בְּמוֹצָאֵי הַדְּרָכִים וּבְמוֹצָאֵי הָאָרֶץ, וַהֲרָגוּם אַנְשֵׁי גַת הַנּוֹלָדִים בָּאָרֶץ, כִּי הֵם הָיוּ בְּקִיאִים בָּאָרֶץ הַהִיא:


**and the men of Gath, etc. slew them because they came down to take their cattle** They were unfamiliar with the ways of the roads and the ways out of the land, and the men of Gath, the natives of the land, slew them because they were familiar with that land.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:24:1)


**וּבִתּוֹ שֶׁאֱרָה וַתִּבֶן אֶת בֵּית חוֹרוֹן.** שֶׁאֱרָה בְּנָאַתּוּ:


**And his daughter was Sheerah, and she built... Beth Horon** Sheerah built it.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:24:2)


**וְאֵת אֻזֵּן שֶׁאֱרָה.** עַל שְׁמָהּ קָרְאָה שֵׁם הָעִיר:


**and Uzzen Sheerah** She named the city after herself.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:25:1)


**וְרֶפַח בְּנוֹ.** שֶׁל **בְּרִיעָה**:


**And Rephah was his son** [i.e., the son] of Beriah.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:25:2)


**וְרֶשֶׁף וְתֶלַח.** בָּנִים שֶׁל רֶפַח, כְּמוֹ כֵן חָשַׁב דּוֹרוֹת:


**and Resheph and Telah** the sons of Rephah. This is the way he counts the generations.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:29:1)


**וְעַל יְדֵי בְנֵי מְנַשֶּׁה בֵּית שְׁאָן.** פִּתְרוֹן: סָמוּךְ לִבְנֵי מְנַשֶּׁה; כְּמוֹ ״אִישׁ עַל יָדוֹ לְדִגְלֵיהֶם״ (במדבר ב:יז):


**And alongside the sons of Manasseh were: Beth Shean** Heb. וְעַל יְדֵי, lit., on the hands of or on the place of. The meaning is: alongside the sons of Manasseh, like (Num. 2:17): “every one in his position עַל יָדוֹ according to their standards.”



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 7:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/7:31:1)


**הוּא אֲבִי בִרְזָיִת.** שַׂר אוֹתָהּ הָעִיר. וּבִבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (בראשית רבה עא:י): רַבִּי לֵוִי וְרַבִּי סִימוֹן. רַבִּי לֵוִי אוֹמֵר: שֶׁהָיוּ בְּנוֹתָיו נָאוֹת וּנְשׂוּאוֹת לְכֹהֲנִים שֶׁנִּמְשְׁחוּ בְּשֶׁמֶן זַיִת; רַבִּי סִימוֹן אוֹמֵר: שֶׁהָיוּ בְּנוֹתָיו נָאוֹת וּנְשׂוּאוֹת לִמְלָכִים שֶׁנִּמְשְׁחוּ בְּשֶׁמֶן הַמִּשְׁחָה. וּלְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ לָהֶם שֶׁמֶן הַרְבֵּה הָיוּ מוֹשְׁחִין בּוֹ, לְכָךְ הָיוּ נָאוֹת, דְּהָא בְּאָשֵׁר כְּתִיב ״וְטֹבֵל בַּשֶּׁמֶן רַגְלוֹ״ (דברים לג:כד); וְאָמַר רַבִּי חֲנַנְיָה (חולין כד:): חַמִּין וְשֶׁמֶן שֶׁסָּכַתְנִי אִמִּי הֶחֱזִירוּנִי לְנַעֲרוּתִי:


**the father of Birzaith** the governor of that city. But in *Genesis Rabbah* (71:9)[it says]: “Rabbi Levi and Rabbi Simon [gave different interpretations of Birzaith]. Rabbi Levi says: It means that his daughters were beautiful and were married to [high] priests who were anointed with olive oil. Rabbi Simon says: [It means] that his daughters were beautiful and married to kings who were anointed with the anointment oil, and since they had plenty of oil, they [the daughters] would anoint themselves with it. Therefore, they were beautiful, for concerning Asher it is written (Deut. 33:24): ‘and dip his foot in oil,’ and Rabbi Hanina said: “The warm water and the oil with which my mother anointed me restored me to my youth.”



## Chapter 8


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:1:1)


**וּבִנְיָמִן הוֹלִיד אֶת בֶּלַע בְּכוֹרוֹ.** כְּבָר יִחֲסוֹ (דברי הימים א ז:ו), וּלְפִי שֶׁרָצָה לְיַחֵס עַד שָׁאוּל הַמֶּלֶךְ, מַתְחִיל לְיַחֵס מִבִּנְיָמִן; וְשֵׁמוֹת אֲחֵרִים קוֹרְאָם כָּאן, וְכָל הַיַּחַס לְמַפְרֵעַ. וְהָרוֹצֶה לְדַקְדֵּק רוֹב עָרֵי הַלְוִיִּם וּמִגְרְשֵׁיהֶם הָאֲמוּרִים כָּאן לְמַפְרֵעַ הֵן בִּיהוֹשֻׁעַ (יהושע כא), כִּי עֶזְרָא סְפָרִים מָצָא:


**And Benjamin begot Bela his firstborn** He already traced his genealogy (above 7:6-11), but since he wished to trace his genealogy until Saul, he commences to trace it from Benjamin. We read other names here and the entire genealogy above, and whoever wishes to study with exactitude the majority of the Levitic cities and the open land around them - they are in Joshua, because Ezra found various books.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:6:1)


**וַיַּגְלוּם אֶל מָנָחַת.** מִי הִגְלָם? וּמְפָרֵשׁ אַחֲרָיו בַּפָּסוּק: ״וְנַעֲמָן וַאֲחִיָּה וְגֵרָא הוּא הִגְלָם״ (להלן ח:ז):


**And they exiled them to Manahath** Who exiled them? He explains this in the [next] verse: And Naaman and Ahiah and Gera - he exiled them.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:6:2)


**מָנָחַת.** שֵׁם מְדִינָה, כְּדִכְתִיב ״הָרוֹאֶה חֲצִי הַמְּנֻחוֹת״ (דברי הימים א ב:נב), וּכְתִיב ״וַחֲצִי הַמָּנַחְתִּי״ (דברי הימים א ב:נד):


**Manahath** This is the name of a province, as it is written (above 2:52): “who ruled over half the Menuhoth,” and it is written (ibid. verse 54): “and half the Manahtites.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:8:1)


**וְשַׁחֲרַיִם הוֹלִיד.** הוּא הָיָה אֶחָד מִן הַגּוֹלִים, וּלְאַחַר שֶׁשִּׁלְּחוּ אוֹתוֹ מִן הַגָּלוּת הוֹלִיד אֶת אֵלֶּה:


**And Shaharaim begot** He was one of the exiles, and after they sent him out of exile, he begot these.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:8:2)


**חוּשִׁים וְאֶת בַּעֲרָא נָשָׁיו.** כָּךְ הָיוּ שְׁמוֹתֵיהֶם שֶׁל נָשָׁיו:


**Hushim and Baara were his wives.** These were the names of his wives.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:11:1)


**וּמֵחֻשִׁים הוֹלִיד.** זֹאת **חוּשִׁים** אִשְׁתּוֹ שֶׁהִזְכִּירָהּ לְמַעְלָה, וּלְפִי שֶׁהִפְסִיקָה בְּ**חֹדֶשׁ אִשְׁתּוֹ**:


**And from Hushim he begot** This is Hushim his wife, mentioned above, and because there was an interruption with Hodesh his wife.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 8:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:14:1)


**וְאַחְיוֹ שָׁשָׁק.** כָּל אֵלֶּה **בְּנֵי אֶלְפָּעַל**. עִנְיָן אַחֵר וְעִיקָּר: מִן **אַחְיוֹ שָׁשָׁק** עַד **וְיוֹחָא** – כָּל אֵלֶּה **בְּנֵי בְרִיעָה**, וּמִ**זְבַּדְיָה** עַד **יוֹבָב** – כָּל אֵלֶּה **בְּנֵי אֶלְפָּעַל**, וּמִן **וְיָקִים** עַד **וְשִׁמְרָת** – כָּל אֵלֶּה **בְּנֵי שִׁמְעִי**:


**And Ahio, Shashak** All these were the sons of Elpaal. Another explanation, which is more correct, is that from “Ahio, Shashak” until “and Joha,” all these are the sons of Beriah, and from “Zebadiah” until “Jobab,” all these are the sons of Elpaal, and from “And Jakim,” until “and Shimrath,” all these are the sons of Shimei.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 8:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/8:29:1)


**וּבְגִבְעוֹן יָשְׁבוּ אֲבִי גִבְעוֹן.** פָּרָשָׁה זוֹ עַד **כָּל אֵלֶּה בְּנֵי אָצֵל** כְּתוּבָה שְתֵּי פְּעָמִים בְּסֵפֶר זֶה; גַּם פָּרָשַׁת ״וְהַיּוֹשְׁבִים הָרִאשֹׁנִים אֲשֶׁר בַּאֲחֻזָּתָם בְּעָרֵיהֶם״ (דברי הימים א ט:ב) וּפָרָשַׁת ״וּמִן הַכֹּהֲנִים יְדַעְיָה וִיהוֹיָרִיב״ (שם י), כְּמוֹ כֵן בְּסֵפֶר עֶזְרָא (נחמיה יא:ג), דִּכְתִיב ״וְאֵלֶּה רָאשֵׁי הַמְּדִינָה״, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁמְּפָרֵשׁ בְּסוֹף מְגִלַּת יְרוּשַׁלְמִי: שְׁלוֹשָׁה סְפָרִים מָצָא עֶזְרָא: סֵפֶר מְעוֹנִים, סֵפֶר זָאטוּטֵי, סֵפֶר הָאֲחִים. וּבִטְּלוּ דִּבְרֵי הָאֶחָד וְקִיְּמוּ דִּבְרֵי הַשְּׁנַיִם. וְכֵן מָצְאוּ הַרְבֵּה סִפְרֵי יִחוּסִין. כְּשֶׁנִּמְצָא שׁלוֹשָׁה אוֹ חֲמִשָּׁה בִּטְּלוּ הַמּוּעָט וְקִיְּמוּ הַמְרֻבִּים. וּכְשֶׁנִּמְצְאוּ זוּגוֹת כְּמוֹ: ״וּבְגִבְעוֹן יָשְׁבוּ אֲבִי גִבְעוֹן״ (דברי הימים א ט:לה) הֻצְרַךְ לִכְתֹּב שְׁתֵּי פְּעָמִים, שֶׁאֵין סֵדֶר יִחוּסָן שָׁוֶה. וְכֵן ״הַיּוֹשְׁבִים הָרִאשֹׁנִים״ (דברי הימים א ט:) מָצָא זוּגוֹת, וַחֲלוּקִים זֶה עַל זֶה, כְּתָבוּהוּ שְׁנֵי פְּעָמִים בְּכָאן וּבְסִפְרוֹ. וְלָמָּה לֹא כָּתַב שְׁתֵּיהֶן כָּאן, אוֹתָם שֶׁבְּעֶזְרָא ״וְאֵלֶּה רָאשֵׁי הַמְּדִינָה וְגוֹמֵר״ (נחמיה יב״א)? וּלְפִי שֶׁיִּיחֵס לְשָׁם עַד ״וְאֵלֶּה הַכֹּהֲנִים הַלְוִיִּם״ (נחמיה יב:א) וּמְיַחֵס עַד ״יְדַעְיָה״ שֶׁהָיָה לְאַחַר עֶזְרָא, וּמַה עִנְיָן לְפָרֵשׁ וּלְיַיחֵס בְּכָאן בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים דּוֹרוֹת יַחַד וּלְאַחַר עֶזְרָא, כִּי אִם אוֹתָם שֶׁהָיוּ בְּבַיִת רִאשׁוֹן וּבַפָּרָשָׁה הָאַחֶרֶת שֶׁל ״וּבְגִבְעוֹן יָשְׁבוּ וְגוֹ׳״. וְעוֹד אָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים בְּסִפְרֵי: ״וּלְאָצֵל שִׁשָּׁה בָנִים״ (דברי הימים א ט:מד) תְּלִיסַר אַלְפֵי גְּמַלֵי טְעוּנֵי מִדְרָשׁוֹת:


**And in Gibeon dwelt the leader of Gibeon** This section until (verse 38): “...all these were the sons of Azel,” is written twice in this Book. Also the section (9:2): “And the first settlers, who were in their inheritance in their cities,” and the section (9:10): “And of the priests: Jedaiah and Jehoirib” are likewise in the Book of Nehemiah, as it is written (11: 3): “And these are the heads of the province.” This is what is explained at the end of Megillath Yerushalmi. Ezra found scrolls: the scroll of מְעוֹנִים, the scroll of זַאַטוּטֵי and the scrolls of הָאַחִים, and they rejected the words of the one and accepted the words of the two. Likewise, they found many genealogical records. When they found three or five, they rejected the minority and accepted the majority. When they found an even number, e.g. “And in Gibeon dwelt the leader of Gibeon,” he had to write it twice because the order of their genealogy is not uniform, and likewise, [in] “the first settlers,” he found an even number, which differed one from the other. [Therefore], it was written twice: here and in his [Ezra’s] book. Now why did he not write both of them here, including those that are in Ezra: “And these are the heads of the province, etc.”? Because he traced the genealogy there until, “And these are the priests and the Levites,” and he traced the genealogy here until Jedaiah, who was after Ezra. Now what purpose would it have to explain and to trace the genealogy here in Chronicles, the generations and the genealogy after Ezra, except for those who were alive during the First Temple period?



## Chapter 9


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:1:1)


**וְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל הִתְיַחֲשׂוּ.** אָמַרְתִּי לְךָ קְצָת מִן יִחוּסָן מַה שֶּׁמָּצָאתִי, אֲבָל עִיקַּר יִחוּסָן **עַל סֵפֶר מַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיהוּדָה(qb) שֶׁ<b>הָגְלוּ לְבָבֶל בְּמַעֲלָם**, וְעִמָּהֶם הָיִיתִי, אֲנִי עֶזְרָא, וְיִחוּסָם מָצָאתִי כָּתוּב וְכָתַבְתִּיו:


**And all Israel traced their genealogy** I related to you part of their genealogy, which I found, but the greater part of their genealogy is in the Book of the Kings of Israel, but the Judeans, who were exiled to Babylon because of their treachery, with whom I, Ezra, was - I found their genealogy written, and I recorded it.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:18:1)


**וְעַד הֵנָּה בְּשַׁעַר הַמֶּלֶךְ מִזְרָחָה.** פִּתְרוֹן: עַד מֵהָלְאָה עַד שַׁעַר שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ שַׁעַר הַמֶּלֶךְ הַמִּזְרָחָה:


**And from here until the king’s gate to the east** The meaning of עַד is: from here until a gate named the King’s Gate to the east.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:18:2)


**הֵמָּה הַשּׁוֹעֲרִים.** שַׁלּוּם וְאֶחָיו מִמַּחֲנוֹת בְּנֵי לֵוִי:


**they are the gate sentries** Shallum and his brethren of the camps of the sons of Levi.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:19:1)


**שׁוֹמְרֵי הַסִּפִּים לָאֹהֶל.** שַׁלּוּם בֶּן קוֹרֵא וְאֶחָיו לְבֵית אָבִיו **הַקָּרְחִים שֹׁמְרֵי הַסִּפִּים** שֶׁל אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד, שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ מַנִּיחִים שׁוּם אָדָם לָבוֹא אֶל אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד, כִּי אִם הַכֹּהֲנִים בַּעֲבוֹדָתָן:


**the guards of the thresholds of the Tent** Shallum the son of Kore and his brethren of his father’s house, the Korahites, were the guards of the thresholds of the Tent of Meeting and did not allow any person to enter the Tent of Meeting, except the priests in their service



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:20:1)


**וּפִינְחָס בֶּן אֶלְעָזָר נָגִיד הָיָה עֲלֵיהֶם לְפָנִים ה׳ עִמּוֹ.** שֶׁנִּתְנַבֵּא לְפָנִים; וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (בראשית רבה ס:ג): הוּא פִּינְחָס הַכֹּהֵן, וְעַל שֶׁלֹּא הִתִּיר לְיִפְתָּח נִדְרוֹ, נִסְתַּלְּקָה הַשְּׁכִינָה מִמֶּנּוּ:


**And Phinehas the son of Eleazar was the ruler over them in time past, the Lord being with him** [This means] that he prophesied in time past, and our Sages said that this is Phinehas the priest, and because he did not annul Jephthah’s vow, the Shechinah left him.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:22:1)


**הֵמָּה יִסַּד דָּוִיד וּשְׁמוּאֵל הָרֹאֶה בֶּאֱמוּנָתָם.** בְּקִיּוּמָם:


**whom David and Samuel the seer established permanently** - Heb. בֶּאֱמוּנָתָם, in their permanence.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:25:1)


**לְשִׁבְעַת הַיָּמִים.** לְסוֹף שִׁבְעַת הַיָּמִים:


**for seven days** at the end of the seven days.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:25:2)


**מֵעֵת אֶל עֵת.** מִשִּׁבְעָה לְשִׁבְעָה, כָּל מִשְׁמֶרֶת בְּשַׁבָּתָם:


**from time to time** from seven to seven, each watch in its week.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:27:1)


**וְהֵם עַל הַמַּפְתֵּחַ.** עַל מַפְתֵּחַ הָעֲזָרָה:


**and they were [appointed] over the key[s]** over the key[s] of the forecourt.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:27:2)


**וְלַבֹּקֶר לַבֹּקֶר.** לִפְתּוֹחַ:


**and every morning** to open.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:28:1)


**וּמֵהֶם עַל כְּלֵי.** כְּלֵי שָׁרֵת, **בְּמִסְפָּר יוֹצִיאוּם וּבְמִסְפָּר יְבִיאוּם**:


**And some of them were in charge of the service vessels** כְּלֵי הָעֲבוֹדָה, the service vessels - they would take them out by number and bring them by number.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:33:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה הַמְשׁוֹרְרִים וְגוֹ׳ פְּטוּרִים.** מִכָּל דָּבָר הַמְּלָאכָה, כִּי אִם לָשִׁיר שִׁירָה:


**And these are the singers, etc. who were exempt** from any manner of work, except to sing a song



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:39:1)


**אֶשְׁבָּעַל.** הוּא ״אִישׁ בֹּשֶׁת״ (שמואל ב ב:ח), וּלְכָךְ כִּנָּהוּ ״בֹּשֶׁת״, לְפִי שֶׁבַּעַל לְשׁוֹן אֲדֹנִי הוּא, וְכִנָּהוּ ״בֹּשֶׁת״ לְשׁוֹן גְּנַאי:


**Eshbaal** That is Ish Bosheth, and he is nicknamed “bosheth,” because “baal” is an expression of lordship, and he nicknamed him “bosheth,” an expression of disgrace.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 9:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/9:40:1)


**מְרִיב בַּעַל.** זֶה ״מְפִיבֹשֶׁת״ (שמואל ב ד:ד): אָמַר רַבִּי יִצְחָק בַּר שְׁמוּאֵל מִנַּרְבּוֹנָא: מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁעָבְדוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַבַּעַל נִקְרָא הַבַּעַל שֵׁם בּוֹשֶׁת, דִּכְתִיב ״לְקַטֵּר לַבֹּשֶׁת לְקַטֵּר לַבָּעַל״ (ירמיהו יא:יג); כִּי הַבַּעַל הוּא בּוֹשֶׁת, וְגִדְעוֹן נִקְרָא ״יְרֻבַּעַל״ עַל שֶׁסֵּרֵב לוֹ בַּעַל (שופטים ו:לב), וְנִקְרָא ״יְרֻבֶּשֶׁת״, דִּכְתִיב עַל אֲבִימֶלֶךְ ״מִי הִכָּה... אֲבִימֶלֶךְ בֶּן יְרֻבֶּשֶׁת וְגוֹ׳״ בְּסֵפֶר שְׁמוּאֵל (שמואל ב יא:כא); וְנִקְרָא ״יְרֻבֶּשֶׁת״ וִ״יְרֻבַּעַל״, כִּי בַּעַל וּבֹשֶׁת אֶחָד הוּא:


**Merib-Baal** That is Mephibosheth (II Sam 4:4). Said Rabbi Isaac the son of Samuel of Narbonne: From the time that Israel worshipped the Baal, the Baal was called by the name of Bosheth (shame), as it is written (Jer. 11:13): “altars for the shameful thing, etc.,” for the Baal is a shameful thing. Gideon was called Jerubaal, based on the expression (Jud. 6:32): “Let Baal contend with him.” He was also called Jerubesheth, for it is written concerning Abimelech: “who smote Abimelech the son of Jerubesheth, etc.” in the Book of Samuel (II 11: 21). He is called Jerubesheth and Jerubaal, because Baal and Bosheth are synonymous.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:1:1)


**וּפְלִשְׁתִּים נִלְחֲמוּ בְיִשְׂרָאֵל.** אֵינוֹ מְסַפֵּר כִּי אִם מַפַּלְתּוֹ שֶׁל שָׁאוּל, וְכַאֲשֶׁר בָּא לְסַפֵּר מַעֲשֵׂה דָּוִד אֵינוֹ מְסַפֵּר זִילוּתוֹ, אֶלָּא גְּבוּרָתוֹ וּגְדֻלָּתוֹ כִּי הַסֵּפֶר שֶׁלּוֹ וּלְמַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה:


**And the Philistines fought with Israel** He relates only Saul’s downfall; when relating the story of David, he does not tell of his disgrace, only of his heroism and greatness, because the Book pertains to him and to the kings of Judah.

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:3:1)


**וַיִּמְצָאֻהוּ הַמּוֹרִים בַּקָּשֶׁת.** בּוֹא וּרְאֵה, כִּי אֵין חָכְמָה וְאֵין תְּבוּנָה לְנֶגֶד ה׳ (משלי כא:ל), כִּי נֶגֶד ה׳ דַּרְכֵי אִישׁ: בִּנְיָמִין הָיוּ שׂוֹנְאֵיהֶם נוֹפְלִים לִפְנֵיהֶם בַּקֶּשֶׁת, כִּי הֵם בְּקִיאִים בַּקֶּשֶׁת, כְּדִכְתִיב ״אִטֵּר יַד יְמִינוֹ וְגוֹ׳״ בְּשׁוֹפְטִים (שופטים כ:טז), וְכֵן בִּיהוֹנָתָן ״צְדָה אוֹרֶה לְשַׁלַּח לִי לְמַטָּרָה״ (שמואל א כ:כ), וְכָתִיב לְמַטָּה ״בִּנְיָמִן נוֹשְׁקֵי קֶשֶׁת מִימִינִים וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים א יב:ב), וְכֵן בְּאָסָא בְּסֵפֶר זֶה ״וּמִבִּנְיָמִן נוֹשְׂאֵי מָגֵן וְדוֹרְכֵי קֶשֶׁת וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב יד:ז); אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהַשֵּׁם סָר מֵעַל שָׁאוּל, נָפַל בְּיַד רוֹבֵה קֶשֶׁת – בְּדָבָר שֶׁהוּא אוּמָּנוּתוֹ. **וַיָּחֶל מִן הַיּוֹרִים.** כְּמוֹ ״וַתִּתְחַלְחַל הַמַּלְכָּה״ (אסתר ד:ד):


**and the archers discovered him** Come and see that (Prov. 21:30): “... there is neither wisdom nor understanding, etc. against the Lord,” because man’s ways are before the Lord. Benjamin’s enemies would fall before them [when they used] the bow because they were well trained with the bow, as it is written: “[Ehud the son of Gera, the Benjamite,] a man with a shriveled right hand,” (Judges 3:15). And so it is written, concerning Jonathan (I Samuel 20:20): “And I shall shoot three arrows to the side, as though I shot at a mark.” And it is written below (I Chron. 12:2) concerning Benjamin: “Armed with bows, shooting with the right and left hands, etc.” and concerning Asa in this Book (II 14:7): “... and out of Benjamin, who bore shields and drew bows, etc.” But when the Lord turned away from Saul [who was from the tribe of Benjamin], he fell into the hands of the archers, into something that was his own craft. and he was terrified of the archers וַיָחֶל like (Esther 4:4): “...and the queen was extremely terrified (וַתִּתְחַלְחַל).”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 10:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:12:1)


**וַיְבִיאוּם יָבֵישָׁה.** לְיָבֵשׁ גִּלְעָד:


**and brought them to Jabesh** to Jabesh Gilead


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 10:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:12:2)


**וַיָּצוּמוּ שִׁבְעַת יָמִים.** וְלָמָּה מָסְרוּ עַצְמָם בְּסַכָּנָה, לָקַחַת גּוּפוֹת שָׁאוּל וּבָנָיו מֵהַר גִּלְבּוֹעַ, יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר יִשְׂרָאֵל? לְפִי שֶׁעָשָׂה לָהֶם שָׁאוּל טוֹבָה, שֶׁהִצִּילָם כְּשֶׁעָלָה נָחָשׁ הָעַמּוֹנִי עֲלֵיהֶם (שמואל א יא:א); וּכְנֶגֶד אוֹתָם שִׁבְעַת יָמִים שֶׁנָּתְנוּ לָהֶם זְמַן אִם יָבֹא לָהֶם מוֹשִׁיעַ (שמואל א יא:ג) וְהָיוּ צָמִים וּמִתְעַנִּים אוּלַי יָבֹא לָהֶם מוֹשִׁיעַ, וּבָא שָׁאוּל וְהוֹשִׁיעָם, כְּנֶגְדָּן נִתְעַנּוּ עַתָּה גַּם הֵם שִׁבְעַת יָמִים; וְגַם מִפְּנֵי אֲבֵילוּת שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ שִׁבְעַת יָמִים, כִּי גַּם קְרוֹבֵיהֶם הָיוּ: כְּשֶׁהָרְגוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת יוֹשְׁבֵי גִּבְעָה בְּמַעֲשֵׂה פִּילֶגֶשׁ בְּגִבְעָה (שופטים כא), נָתְנוּ אַנְשֵׁי יָבֵשׁ בְּנוֹתֵיהֶם לְבִנְיָמִין כְּשֶׁחֲטָפוּם מִן הַמְּחוֹלוֹת (שופטים כא:כג):


**and they fasted seven days** Why did they imperil their lives more than the rest of Israel to remove the bodies of Saul and his sons from Mount Gilboa? Because Saul had done them a favor by saving them when Nahash the Ammonite marched upon them. [They fasted seven days] corresponding to the seven days that they [the Ammonites] gave them [to see] if a savior would come [to save] them, [at which time] they fasted seven days; perhaps a savior would come to them. Since Saul came and saved them, they fasted now too seven days, and also because of the mourning which was customary to observe seven days, because they were also their kinsmen, for when Israel slew the inhabitants of Gibeah in the incident of the concubine in Gibeah (at the end of Judges), the people of Jabesh Gilead gave their daughters to Benjamin, when they grabbed them from the dances.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 10:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:13:1)


**וַיָּמָת שָׁאוּל בְּמַעֲלוֹ אֲשֶׁר מָעַל.** שְׁתֵּי מְעִילוֹת מָעַל בִּשְׁאֵלַת אוֹב: אַחַת שֶׁשָּׁאַל אַחֲרֵי שְׁמוּאֵל, וְאַחַת שֶׁדָּרַשׁ; וּבָאַגָּדָה דִּשְׁמוּאֵל שָׁנִינוּ (מדרש שמואל כד, ז): חָמֵשׁ מְעִילוֹת מָעַל:


**And Saul died because of the treachery that he had committed** He committed two treacherous acts by inquiring of the familiar spirit: one when he inquired after Samuel, and one when he inquired of familiar spirits, and in the Aggadah of Samuel (24:6) we learn that he committed five treacherous acts


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 10:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/10:13:2)


**עַל דְּבַר ה׳ אֲשֶׁר לֹא שָׁמָר.** מַה שֶּׁצִּוָּהוּ שְׁמוּאֵל, כְּדִכְתִיב ״שִׁבְעַת יָמִים תּוֹחֵל״ (שמואל א י:ח), וְעַל אֲשֶׁר מָעַל בְּמִלְחֶמֶת עֲמָלֵק (שמואל א טו):


**concerning the word of the Lord that he did not keep** that which Samuel had commanded him, as it is written (I Sam. 10:8): “Seven days shall you wait,” and because he dealt treacherously in the war of Amalek [by sparing Agag].



## Chapter 11


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:2:1)


**אַתָּה תִרְעֶה אֶת עַמִּי.** עַל זֶה אָמַר דָּוִד: ״ה׳ רֹעִי לֹא אֶחְסָר״ (תהלים כג:א) – אַתָּה אוֹמֵר לִי: אַתָּה תִרְעֶה, מָה אֲנִי יָכוֹל לִרְעוֹת? אֵין הַדָּבָר תָּלוּי בִּי, אֶלָּא ״ה׳ רֹעִי לֹא אֶחְסָר״, וְגַם אֲנִי לֹא אֶחְסַר לָכֶם; וְדֻגְמָא בִּשְׁמוּאֵל: ״אָז נִשְׁבְּעוּ אַנְשֵׁי דָוִד לוֹ לֵאמֹר לֹא תֵצֵא עוֹד אִתָּנוּ בַּמִּלְחָמָה וְלֹא תְכַבֶּה אֶת נֵר יִשְׂרָאֵל״ (שמואל ב כא:יז) – אָמַר לָהֶם דָּוִד: ״כִּי אַתָּה נֵרִי ה׳ וַה׳ יַגִּיהַּ חָשְׁכִּי״ (שמואל ב כב:כט):


**You shall shepherd My people** Concerning this, David said, (Ps. 23:1): “The Lord is my Shepherd, I shall not want.” “You say to me: You shall shepherd. How can I shepherd? The matter does not depend on me, but the Lord is my Shepherd, I shall not want, and I, too, shall not be wanting to you.” A similar instance is found in Samuel (II 21:17): “Then the men of David swore to him, saying, ‘You shall no longer go out with us in battle, so that you extinguish not the lamp of Israel.’” David replied (ibid. 22:29): “For You are my lamp, O Lord; and the Lord does light my darkness.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:3:1)


**וַיִּכְרֹת לָהֶם דָּוִד בְּרִית.** כְּאוֹתוֹ בְּרִית שֶׁכָּרַת יְהוֹיָדָע, דִּכְתִיב ״וַיִּכְרֹת יְהוֹיָדָע אֶת הַבְּרִית בֵּין ה׳ וּבֵין הַמֶּלֶךְ וּבֵין הָעָם לִהְיוֹת לְעָם לַה׳״ (מלכים ב יא:יז) – פִּתְרוֹן: לִהְיוֹת לַה׳ עֲבָדִים; וְגַם בֵּין הַמֶּלֶךְ וּבֵין הָעָם לִהְיוֹת לוֹ לַעֲבָדִים כְּמִשְׁפַּט הַמֶּלֶךְ, וְגַם הַמֶּלֶךְ – לַעֲשׂוֹת כַּחֹק לַעֲבָדָיו, לִלְחֹם מִלְחֲמוֹתָם:


**and David made with them a covenant** Like the covenant that Jehoiada made, as it is written (II Kings 11:17): “And Jehoiada enacted the covenant between the Lord and between the king and between the people, to be the people of the Lord.” The meaning is [that they agreed] to be servants of the Lord, and also “between the king and between the people,” to be his servants according to the king’s judgment, and also that the king should do according to the law for his servants; to wage their wars.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:3:2)


**לִפְנֵי ה׳.** וַהֲלֹא לֹא הָיָה הַמִּשְׁכָּן בְּחֶבְרוֹן! אֶלָּא מַהוּ לִפְנֵי ה׳? כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁעוֹשִׂין תְּנַאי אוֹ כּוֹרְתִין בְּרִית, הַמָּקוֹם שָׁם הוּא; דּוּגְמָא בְּיִפְתָּח: ״לִפְנֵי ה׳ בַּמִּצְפָּה״ (שופטים יא:יא), וְדוּגְמָא ״וּמָעֲלָה מַעַל בַּה׳ וְכִחֵשׁ בַּעֲמִיתוֹ״ (ויקרא ה:כא), כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ בְּתוֹרַת כֹּהֲנִים (ספרא נדבה פרשתא יב, פרק כב, ד):


**before the Lord** But is it not so that the Tabernacle was not in Hebron? What then is the meaning of “before the Lord”? Wherever people make a stipulation or enact a covenant, the Omnipresent is there. A similar instance [is found] in [the case of] Jephthah: (Jud. 11:11): “before the Lord in Mizpah.” [Another] similar instance (Lev. 5:21): “and commits an act of treachery against the Lord by making a denial to his neighbor,” as is explained in *Torath Kohanim* (ad loc.).



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:4:1)


**וַיֵּלֶךְ דָּוִד וְגוֹ׳.** הָלַךְ מִיָּד לַמִּלְחָמָה כְּשֶׁהִמְלִיכוּהוּ כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁלֹּא יֹאמְרוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל: כָּל הַמִּלְחָמוֹת שֶׁעָשָׂה דָּוִד בִּימֵי שָׁאוּל נוֹצֵחַ, מַזָּלוֹ שֶׁל שָׁאוּל הָיָה, וְעַתָּה דּוֹאֵג לְהִלָּחֵם; לְכָךְ הָלַךְ מִיָּד לַמִּלְחָמָה:


**And David... went, etc.** He went immediately to war when all Israel crowned him, so that Israel should not say [that in] all the wars that David waged in the days of Saul and was victorious it was Saul’s luck, and that he was now afraid to fight. Therefore he immediately went to war.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:5:1)


**וַיֹּאמְרוּ יֹשְׁבֵי יְבוּס לְדָוִיד לֹא תָבוֹא הֵנָּה.** אֵינוֹ מְפָרֵשׁ כָּאן לָמָּה, וּבִשְׁמוּאֵל מְפָרֵשׁ: ״וַיֹּאמֶר לְדָוִד לֹא תָבוֹא הֵנָּה כִּי אִם הֱסִירְךָ הָעִוְרִים וְהַפִּסְחִים״ (שמואל ב ה:ו). רָאוּ שֶׁלֹּא יוּכְלוּ לַעֲמֹד לִפְנֵי דָוִד, וְנָתְנוּ עִוְרִים וּפִסְחִים לִפְנֵי שַׁעַר הָעִיר, לֵאמֹר: לֹא יָבֹא דָוִד הֵנָּה, כִּי אִם יִלָּחֵם עִם הָעִוְרִים הַלָּלוּ; כִּי יָדְעוּ כִּי בָזוּי הוּא לַמֶּלֶךְ לְהִלָּחֵם עִם הָעִוְרִים, וּמִתּוֹךְ כָּךְ יָשׁוּב וְלֹא יִלָּחֵם עִמָּהֶם. וְדָוִד אָמַר בְּלִבּוֹ: אֱמֶת הוּא כִּי לֹא נָכוֹן וְהָגוּן לִי לְהִלָּחֵם עִם עִוְרִים וְעִם פִּסְחִים, אַךְ כָּל מִי שֶׁיַּכֶּה הַיְבוּסִי, הֵיאַךְ שֶׁיִּגַּע בַּצִּנּוֹר, וְיִמְשְׁכֵם וְיַשְׁלִיכֵם מִשָּׁם אֶת הָעִוְרִים; ״עַל כֵּן יֹאמְרוּ עִוֵּר וּפִסֵּחַ לֹא יָבֹא אֶל הַבָּיִת״ (שמואל ב ה:ח) – אֶל בֵּית דָּוִד, שֶׁיְּהֵא לוֹ גְּנַאי, פֶּן יֹאמְרוּ: עִם אֵלּוּ נִלְחַם דָּוִד:


**And the inhabitants of Jebus said to David, “You shall not come here”** He does not explain here why, but in Samuel (II 5:6), Scripture explains: “... and they said to David, saying: You shall not come here unless you remove the blind and the lame.” They saw that they were unable to withstand David. They therefore stationed blind and lame persons before the city gate, “as if to say: David shall not come here,” unless he wages war with these blind people, for they knew that it is disgraceful for a king to wage war against blind men, and because of this, he would turn back and not wage war with them. David said to himself, however, “It is true that it is neither right nor proper for me to wage war with the blind and with the lame, but ‘whoever smites the Jebusites’ in a way that he ‘reaches the tower’ shall pull them and cast them away from there, viz. the blind.” “Therefore they say: The blind and the lame shall not come into the house;” into the house of David, because it will be a disgrace for him, lest they say that David waged war with these [handicapped people].



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:8:1)


**וְיוֹאָב יְחַיֶּה אֶת שְׁאָר הָעִיר.** הוּא בָּנָה וְהֶחֱזִיק, לִבְנוֹת בִּדְקֵי הַחוֹמָה; דֻּגְמָא בְּעֶזְרָא ״הַיְחַיּוּ אֶת הָאֲבָנִים מֵעַרְמוֹת הֶעָפָר״ (נחמיה ג:לד):


**and Joab restored the rest of the city** Heb. יְחַיֶּה. He built and strengthened [it] by building [i.e., repairing] the cracks in the wall. A similar instance [is found] with Ezra (Neh. 3:34): “Will they revive (הַיְּחַיּוּ) the stones from the heaps of dust?”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:11:1)


**רֹאשׁ הַשְּׁלִישִׁים.** רֹאשׁ הַגִּבּוֹרִים, כְּמוֹ ״וְשָׁלִשִׁים עַל כֻּלּוֹ״ (שמות יד:ז):


**the head of the mighty men** רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁלִישִׁים like (Exod. 14:7): “and mighty men וְשָׁלִשִׁים over them all.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:11:2)


**עוֹרֵר אֶת חֲנִיתוֹ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר הוּא נִלְחָם בַּמִּלְחָמָה, לֹא הָיָה חוֹזֵר בַּמִּלְחָמָה עַד שֶׁיַּהֲרֹג בַּחֲנִיתוֹ **שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת חָלָל**:


**he stirred his spear** When he was engaged in battle, he would not return from the battle until he had slain three hundred men with his spear.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:11:3)


**בֶּן חַכְמוֹנִי.** בִּשְׁמוּאֵל (שמואל ב כג:ח) כָּתוּב ״תַּחְכְּמוֹנִי״, הוּא אָבִיו, וְיָפֶה כֹּחַ הָאָב מִכֹּחַ הַבֵּן (שבועות מח.), שֶׁכָּתוּב בּוֹ עַל שְׁמֹנֶה מֵאוֹת״ גִּבּוֹרִים ״חָלָל בְּפַעַם אֶחָת״:


**the son of Hachmoni** In Samuel (II 23:8), it is written: “Tahkemoni.” He is his father, and the father was stronger than the son, for it is written concerning him: “against eight hundred slain at one time.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:12:1)
