**הוּא בִּשְׁלוֹשָׁה הַגִּבּוֹרִים.** הוּא בִּכְלַל שְׁלוֹשָׁה הַגִּבּוֹרִים, כִּי הוּא אֶחָד מֵהֶם; וּשְׁלֹשָׁה אֵלּוּ גִּבּוֹרִים יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר שְׁלֹשִׁים. וּשְׁלֹשָׁה שֶׁנִּזְכַּר בְּסָמוּךְ, וְכָאן אֵינוֹ מַזְכִּיר כִּי אִם שְׁנַיִם: יָשָׁבְעָם וְאֶלְעָזָר; וּבִשְׁמוּאֵל מַזְכִּיר אַף הַשְּׁלִישִׁי, דִּכְתִיב ״וְאַחֲרָיו שַׁמָּא בֶן אָגֵא הָרָרִי״ (שמואל ב כג:יא):


**he was among the three mighty men** He was included in the three mighty men; he was one of them. And these three were mightier than the rest of the thirty three mentioned nearby. Here he counts only two: Jashobeam and Eleazar, but in Samuel (II 23:11), it mentions the third one as well, as it is written: “and after him [came] Shammah the son of Agei the mountaineer”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:13:1)


**בְּפַס דָּמִים.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם, וּבִשְׁמוּאֵל: ״בְּאֶפֶס דַּמִּים״ (שמואל א יז:א):


**in Pas-Dammim** a name of a place, and in Samuel (I 17:1): “in Ephes-Dammim.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:13:2)


**וְהַפְּלִשְׁתִּים נֶאֱסְפוּ שָׁם לַמִּלְחָמָה.** שֶׁנִּזְדָּרְזוּ לְהִנָּקֵם מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל וּמֵאוֹתָם גִּבּוֹרִים שֶׁחֵרְפוּ, כַּמְפֹרָשׁ בִּשְׁמוּאֵל ״בְּחָרְפָם בַּפְּלִשְׁתִּים״ (שמואל ב כג:ט):


**and the Philistines gathered there for war** they were eager to avenge themselves upon Israel and those mighty men who reviled them, as delineated in Samuel (II 23:9): “...when they reviled the Philistines.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:13:3)


**מְלֵאָה שְׂעוֹרִים.** וְרָצוּ פְלִשְׁתִּים לְקוֹצְרָהּ וּלְהַצִּיתָהּ בָּאֵשׁ:


**full of barley** and the Philistines wished to reap it and to set it afire.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:15:1)


**וַיֵּרְדוּ שְׁלוֹשָׁה מִן הַשְּׁלוֹשִׁים.** שְׁלֹשָׁה אֵלּוּ הָיוּ רֹאשׁ לְכָל הַשְּׁלוֹשִׁים:


**And three of the thirty** These three were the heads of the entire thirty.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:16:1)


**וּנְצִיב פְּלִשְׁתִּים אָז בְּבֵית לָחֶם.** שֶׁכְּבָר בֵּית לֶחֶם לָקְחוּ, וְהֶעֱמִידוּ בָהּ נְצִיב, שׁוֹפֵט. זֶה בִּתְחִלַּת מַלְכוּתוֹ, שֶׁעֲדַיִן פְּלִשְׁתִּים הָיוּ מוֹשְׁלִים בָּם, שֶׁכָּךְ מָצִינוּ שֶׁלִּפְנֵי בֹּא דָּוִד הָיוּ מוֹשְׁלִים עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּימֵי שִׁמְשׁוֹן וּשְׁמוּאֵל, דִּכְתִיב בִּשְׁמוּאֵל ״הִתְחַזְּקוּ וִהְיוּ לַאֲנָשִׁים פְּלִשְׁתִּים פֶּן תַּעַבְדוּ לָעִבְרִים וְגוֹ׳״ (שמואל א ד:ט):


**and a Philistine governor was then in Bethlehem** for they had already taken Bethlehem and stationed a governor therein, who judged [the region]. This was at the beginning of his reign, for the Philistines were still ruling over them, for so we find that prior to David’s arrival, they ruled over Israel in the days of Samson and Samuel, as it is written (I Samuel 4:9): “Strengthen yourselves and be valiant, Philistines, lest you serve the Hebrews, etc.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:17:1)


**מִי יַשְׁקֵנִי מַיִם מִבּוֹר בֵּית לֶחֶם.** זֶה הָיָה מְקוֹמוֹ שֶׁל דָּוִד דִּכְתִיב, ״וְדָוִד בֶּן יִשַׁי מִבֵּית הַלַּחְמִי״ (שמואל א יז:נח), וּלְפִי שֶׁהָיָה דָּוִד תָּדִיר מְלוּמָּד וְרָגִיל בְּאוֹתָן מַיִם, נִתְאַוָּה לָהֶם; וְכָל מַיִם וְכָל אֲוִיר שֶׁאָדָם רָגִיל בָּהֶם יָפִין לוֹ, וְשֶׁאֵין רָגִיל בָּהֶם – נִיזּוֹק. וְדוּגְמָא בְּרֵישׁ יוֹסִיפוֹן: מַלְכָּה אַחַת שֶׁחָלְתָה עַל שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת חוּצָה מֵאֶרֶץ מוֹלַדְתָּהּ, וְאָמְרוּ הָרוֹפְאִים: אֵין לָהּ רְפוּאָה עַד שֶׁתִּרְחַץ וְתֵהָנֶה מִמֵּימֵי מְקוֹמָהּ; וְכֵן עָשׂוּ לָהּ:


**If only someone would give me to drink water from the cistern of Bethlehem** This was David’s place, as it is written (I Sam. 17:58): “The son of your servant, Jesse the Bethlehemite,” and because he was accustomed and used to that water, he desired it; any water or air that a person is accustomed to is beneficial to him, and that which he is not used to is harmful to him. A similar case is [found] in the beginning of Josiphon about a queen who became ill because she was carried away from the land of her birth, and all the physicians said, “She has no cure until she bathes and derives benefit from the water of her place,” and they did this for her.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:18:1)


**וַיִּבְקְעוּ הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה בְּמַחֲנֵה פְלִשְׁתִּים.** בִּלְשׁוֹן אַשְׁכְּנַז (דור״ך ברעכ״ן ד״י שאר״א):


**And the three broke through the Philistine camp** (In German, *durchbrechen die schare*)


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:18:2)


**וַיִּשְׁאֲבוּ.** כְּשֶׁהַפְּלִשְׁתִּים עוֹמְדִים בִּפְנֵי הַבּוֹר, בָּאוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה גִּבּוֹרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּבָקְעוּ בֵּינֵיהֶם וְשָׁאֲבוּ עַל כָּרְחָם:


**drew** When the Philistines were standing before the cistern, the three mighty men of Israel broke through between them and drew [water] against their will.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:18:3)


**וַיְנַסֵּךְ אוֹתָם לַה׳.** בַּר קַפָּרָא אוֹמֵר (ירושלמי סנהדרין ב,ה): חַג הַסֻּכּוֹת הָיָה, וְנִסְּכָן עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


**and he poured it out as a libation to the Lord** Bar Kappara says: It was the festival of Succoth, and he poured them [the waters] out for a libation on the altar.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:19:1)


**כִּי בְנַפְשׁוֹתָם הֱבִיאוּם.** שֶׁמָּסְרוּ עַצְמָם לַהֲרִיגָה עַל הַמַּיִם:


**for they brought it with their lives** for they risked being killed for the water.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:20:1)


**וַאֲבְשַׁי אֲחִי יוֹאָב הוּא הָיָה רֹאשׁ הַשְּׁלוֹשָׁה.** לְאוֹתָם שְׁלֹשָׁה שֶׁבָּקְעוּ **בְּמַחֲנֵה פְּלִשְׁתִּים** וְשָׁאֲבוּ הַמַּיִם. וּמַה שֶּׁאֵינוֹ מַזְכִּיר אֶת יוֹאָב עִם הַגִּבּוֹרִים, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין כְּבוֹדוֹ לִמְנוֹתוֹ עִם אֵלּוּ, כִּי הוּא הָיָה שַׂר צָבָא, רֹאשׁ לְכֻלָּם:


**And Abshai, the brother of Joab, was the chief of the three** of those three who broke through the Philistine camp and drew the water. The reason he does not mention Joab with the chiefs of the mighty men is that it is beneath his dignity to count him with these men, because he was the general of the army, the chief of them all.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:20:2)


**וְלוֹ שֵׁם בַּשְּׁלֹשָׁה.** אוֹתוֹ הַשֵּׁם הָיָה לוֹ שֶׁגְּבוּרָתוֹ הָיְתָה כְּנֶגֶד כָּל גְּבוּרוֹת כֻּלָּם; וְטָעוּת הוּא:


**and he had a name among the three** It was reputed (lit., he had that name) that his heroism equaled all the heroic acts of them all, but it was an error.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:21:1)


**מִן הַשְּׁלוֹשָׁה בַשְּׁנַיִם.** הוּא נִכְבָּד, וְלֹא כְּאוֹתָן שְׁלֹשָׁה – כְּנֶגֶד הַשְּׁנַיִם הָיְתָה גְבוּרָתוֹ, וְלִכְלַל גְּבוּרוֹת הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה לֹא בָא; (וְזֶהוּ) **וְעַד הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה לֹא בָא**:


**Of the three, he was honored like two** He was not honored like those three. His heroism equalled that of the two, but he did not attain the amount of heroism of the three, and that is the meaning of “but to the three he did not come.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:22:1)


**בֶּן אִישׁ חַיִל.** דֻּגְמָא ״בֶּן בְּלִיַּעַל״ (שמואל א כה:יז). וּבִשְׁמוּאֵל (שמואל ב כג:כ) כְּתִיב ״בֶּן אִישׁ חַי״, כִּי כֵן מִנְהַג בְּנֵי הָאָדָם, כְּשֶׁרוֹאִים אָדָם זָרִיז אוֹמְרִים: זֶה כֻּלּוֹ חַי:


**a valiant man** בֶּן אִישׁ חַַיִל, similar to בֶּן בְּלִיַּעַל, an unscrupulous man, and in Samuel (II 23:20) it is written: בֶּן אִישׁ חַי for it is customary for people, when they see an agile person, to say, “This one is full of life.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:22:2)


**אֲרִיאֵל.** גִּבּוֹרִים בַּעֲלֵי כֹּחַ אֲרִי, גִּבּוֹר וְגַם אֵל, לְשׁוֹן ״אֵילֵי הָאָרֶץ״ (יחזקאל יז:יג):


**mighty men** אֲרִיאֵל mighty men, possessing strength of a lion, a mighty man, and [it] is also an expression of (Ezek. 17: 20): “...and he took the mighty (אֵילֵי) of the land.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:22:3)


**רַב פְּעָלִים.** פְּעֻלּוֹת שֶׁל גְּבוּרוֹת הַרְבֵּה עָשָׂה:


**who accomplished many feats** He accomplished many feats of heroism.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:22:4)


**וְהוּא יָרַד וְהִכָּה אֶת הָאֲרִי בְּתוֹךְ הַבּוֹר בְּיוֹם הַשָּׁלֶג.** וְלֹא חָשַׁשׁ מִן הַצִּנָּה וְהַקֹּר; וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: **בְּיוֹם הַשָּׁלֶג.** כָּל הַשָּׁנָה כֻּלָּהּ אֵין הָאֲרִי מְסֻכָּן יוֹתֵר מִיּוֹם הַשֶּׁלֶג, כְּשֶׁבָּא אָדָם נֶגְדּוֹ זוֹרֵק הַשֶּׁלֶג בְּרַגְלוֹ בֵּין עֵינֵי הָאָדָם עַד שֶׁלֹּא יָכוֹל לִרְאוֹת, וְהוֹרְגוֹ. וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי כָּךְ עַל זֹאת:


**and he descended and smote the lion in the midst of a pit on a snowy day** and he did not fear the cold, and some say [that the heroism] of the snowy day is that throughout the whole year the lion is not as dangerous as on a snowy day. When a person comes against him, he throws the snow with his feet between the person’s eyes until he is unable to see, and he kills him. I heard this [meaning] of this [verse].



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:23:1)


**אִישׁ מִדָּה.** שְׁאָר בְּנֵי אָדָם אֵין צָרִיךְ לִמְדֹּד, כִּי אִם מֵאוֹמֶד יָכוֹל לֵידַע; וְדוּגְמָא ״אֲשֶׁר רָאִינוּ בְתוֹכָהּ אַנְשֵׁי מִדּוֹת״ (במדבר יג:לב):


**a man of great stature** Other people do not require to be measured, as [their height] can be determined by estimate; a similar case is (Num. 13:32): “... whom we saw in its midst are men of a measure.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:23:2)


**כְּמְנוֹר אֹרְגִים.** עֵץ שֶׁכּוֹרְכִים עָלָיו הַבֶּגֶד:


**like a weavers’ beam** the pole upon which the cloth is wrapped.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:25:1)


**מִן הַשְּׁלוֹשִׁים הִנּוֹ נִכְבָּד הוּא וְאֶל הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה.** לִכְלַל גְּבוּרַת הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה **לֹא בָא**, לְאוֹתָן שְׁלֹשָׁה שֶׁמַּזְכִּיר לְמַעְלָה (פס׳ יא-יב), וְלָכֵן כְּתִיב לֹא בְּאָלֶף:


**Of the thirty, he was the most honored, but to the three he did not come** To the heroic feats of the three he did not reach; i.e., to those three mentioned above (verse 20). Therefore, לא is written above with an “aleph.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:25:2)


**עַל מִשְׁמַעְתּוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן מִנּוּי, וְכֵן ״וּבְנֵי עַמּוֹן מִשְׁמַעְתָּם״ (ישעיהו יא:יד):


**over his guard** עַל מִשְׁמַעְתּוֹ, an expression of appointment, and likewise, (Isa. 11:14): “... and the children of Ammon will be their appointees מִשְׁמַעְתָּם.”



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 11:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/11:26:1)


**וְגִבּוֹרֵי הַחֲיָלִים.** אַךְ לֹא כְּאוֹתָן שֶׁל מַעְלָה:


**And the mighty warriors** but not like those above.



## Chapter 12


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:1:1)


**עוֹד עָצוּר.** נֶחְבָּא:


**when he was still hiding** - Heb. עָצוּר, hiding.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:2:1)


**נֹשְׁקֵי קֶשֶׁת.** מְזֻיָּנִים בְּקֶשֶׁת וּבְחִצִּים, וּמוֹרִים לִפְנֵיהֶם וּלְאַחֲרֵיהֶם אַף **בָּאֲבָנִים**; הָיוּ נוֹתְנִים עַל יֶתֶר הַקֶּשֶׁת אֶבֶן מוֹרִים. וַאֲנִי רָאִיתִי כֵן הַרְבֵּה:


**Armed with bows** armed with a bow and with arrows and shooting in front of them and behind them. And they would even place stones on the bowstring and shoot. I personally saw many like these.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:2:2)


**מֵאֲחֵי שָׁאוּל מִבִּנְיָמִין.** לְפִי שֶׁכֻּלָּם יָצְאוּ מֵאִישׁ אֶחָד, מִבִּנְיָמִין, קְרוּיִים כֻּלָּם אַחִים; וְלִכְבוֹד גְּדֻלַּת דָּוִד אוֹמֵר מֵאֲחֵי שָׁאוּל, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ אֲחֵי שָׁאוּל בָּאוּ אֵלָיו בְּעוֹד שָׁאוּל חַי:


**of Saul’s brethren of Benjamin** Since they were all descended from one man, from Benjamin, they are all called brethren, and in honor of David’s greatness, the Chronicler says, “of Saul’s brethren,” [saying] that even Saul’s brethren came to him while Saul was still living.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:4:1)


**וְעַל הַשְּׁלֹשִׁים.** רֹאשׁ לַשְּׁלֹשִׁים:


**and over the thirty** the chief of the thirty.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:9:1)


**לְמְצַד מִדְבָּרָה.** לִמְצוּדוֹת הַמִּדְבָּר, שֶׁנֶּחְבָּא מִפְּנֵי שָׁאוּל, דִּכְתִיב ״וַיָּקוּמוּ וַיֵּלְכוּ זִיפָה לִפְנֵי שָׁאוּל וְדָוִד וַאֲנָשָׁיו בְּמִדְבַּר מָעוֹן בָּעֲרָבָה״ (שמואל א כג:כד), וּכְתִיב ״וַיַּעַל דָּוִד מִשָּׁם וַיֵּשֶׁב בִּמְצָדוֹת עֵין גֶּדִי״ (שמואל א כד:א):


**to the stronghold of the desert** to the strongholds of the desert where he hid from Saul, as it is written (I Sam. 23:24): “And they arose and went to Ziph before Saul, and David and his men were in the desert of Maon, in the plain,” and it is written (ibid. 24:1): “And David went up from there, and he stayed in the strongholds of En-Gedi.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:9:2)


**וּפְנֵי אַרְיֵה.** פְּנֵיהֶם מְפֻחָדִים כְּאַרְיֵה:


**and whose faces were [like] the faces of lions** Their faces were frightful like lions.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:9:3)


**וְכִצְבָאיִם עַל הֶהָרִים לְמַהֵר.** קַלִּים הָיוּ בִּמְרוּצָתָם, כִּי דַּרְכָּן שֶׁל צְבָאִים לָרוּץ עַל הֶהָרִים כְּשֶׁצָּדִין אוֹתָם:


**and as swift as gazelles on the mountains** They were swift in their running, for it is customary for gazelles to run on the mountains when people hunt them.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:15:1)


**אֵלֶּה מִבְּנֵי גָד.** כְּדִכְתִיב לְמַעְלָה **וּמִן הַגָּדִי נִבְדְּלוּ אֶל דָּוִיד**:


**these were some of the sons of Gad** as is written above (v. 8): “And some of the Gadites separated to David.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:15:2)


**אֶחָד לְמֵאָה הַקָּטָן.** הַקָּטָן שֶׁבְּכֻלָּם יִרְדֹּף מֵאָה:


**the smallest one a hundred** The smallest of them could pursue a hundred.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:15:3)


**וְהַגָּדוֹל לְאָלֶף.** וְהַגָּדוֹל שֶׁבְּכֻלָּם יִרְדֹּף אֶלֶף; כָּאן נִתְקַיֵּם הַפָּסוּק ״וְרָדְפוּ מִכֶּם חֲמִשָּׁה מֵאָה וּמֵאָה מִכֶּם וְגוֹ׳״ (ויקרא כו:ח):


**and the greatest a thousand** And the greatest of them could pursue a thousand. This is an actualization of the verse (Lev. 26:8): “Five of you will pursue one hundred, and one hundred of you, etc.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:16:1)


**אֵלֶּה הֵם אֲשֶׁר עָבְרוּ אֶת הַיַּרְדֵּן בַּחֹדֶשׁ הָרִאשׁוֹן.** בְּנִיסָן, שֶׁכְּבָר נִפְשְׁרוּ הַשְּׁלָגִים:


**These are the ones who crossed the Jordan in the first month** in Nissan, when the snows had already melted.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:16:2)


**וַיַּבְרִיחוּ אֶת כָּל הָעֲמָקִים לַמִּזְרָח וְלַמַּעֲרָב.** עָבְרוּ אֶת הַיַּרְדֵּן, וּבָקְעוּ בְּמָגִנֵּיהֶם אֶת כָּל עִמְקֵי הַמַּיִם לַמִּזְרָח וְלַמַּעֲרָב, וְעָבְרוּ בַּיַּבָּשָׁה. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: **וַיַּבְרִיחוּ אֶת כָּל הָעֲמָקִים** – עַמִּים הַיּוֹשְׁבִים בָּעֲמָקִים:


**and they put all the valley-dwellers to flight to the east and to the west** They crossed the Jordan and with their shields split all the depths of the water to the east and to the west and crossed on dry land. Some say that ויַּבְרִיחוּ אֶת כָּל הָעֲמָקִִים refers to the people dwelling in the valleys.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:17:1)


**מִן בְּנֵי בִנְיָמִין וִיהוּדָה עַד לַמְצָד לְדָוִיד.** אֶל הַמְּצוּדָה אֲשֶׁר דָּוִד נֶחְבָּא שָׁם מִפְּנֵי שָׁאוּל. וְלָקְחוּ עִמָּהֶם בֶּן אֲחוֹת דָּוִד, לְפִי שֶׁדָּאֲגוּ בְּנֵי בִנְיָמִן מִדָּוִד, שֶׁמָּא סָבוּר הוּא דָּוִד שֶׁבָּאוּ בְּנֵי בִנְיָמִין לְכָפְתוֹ וּלְתִתּוֹ אוֹתוֹ בְּיַד שָׁאוּל, לְפִיכָךְ לָקְחוּ עֲמָשַׂי עִמָּהֶם, וְגַם שְׁאָר מִיהוּדָה קְרוֹבֵי דָוִד, כְּדִכְתִיב: מִן בְּנֵי בִנְיָמִין וִיהוּדָה, כְּשֶׁיִּרְאֶה דָּוִד שֶׁבָּאוּ עִמָּהֶם בֶּאֱמוּנָתָם, שֶׁלֹּא יַחְשְׁדֵם בְּכָל דָּבָר:


**And there came some of the sons of Benjamin and Judah up to the stronghold, to David** to the stronghold where David was hiding from Saul. And they took David’s sister’s son with them, because the sons of Benjamin were concerned that David might think that the sons of Benjamin had come to bind him and deliver him into Saul’s hands. Therefore, they took Amasa with them to David, and also some of David’s kinsmen from the rest of Judah, as it is written, “some of the sons of Benjamin and Judah,” in order that David would see that they came with them in good faith, so that he should not suspect them of anything.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:18:1)


**וַיֵּצֵא דָוִיד לִפְנֵיהֶם.** מִן הַמְּצָד:


**And David came out before them** from the stronghold.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:18:2)


**וַיַּעַן וַיֹּאמֶר לָהֶם וְגוֹ׳ לֵבָב לְיָחַד.** – לְבַבְכֶם וּלְבָבִי יִהְיֶה כְּלֵב אֶחָד:


**my heart will be upon you as one** My heart and your heart will be like one heart.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:18:3)


**וְאִם לְרַמּוֹתַנִי לְצָרַי בְּלֹא חָמָס בְּכַפַּי.** כִּי מָה עָשִׂיתִי לְשָׁאוּל, כִּי הוּא צוֹדֶה אֶת נַפְשִׁי לְקַחְתָּהּ (שמואל א כד:יא) עַל חִנָּם:


**but if you have come to betray me to my adversaries although there is no injustice in my hands** for what have I done to Saul that he is stalking my soul to take it for nothing?



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:19:1)


**וְרוּחַ לָבְשָׁה אֶת עֲמָשַׂי.** טַלְטַנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, וְלֹא רוּחַ נְבוּאָה; כְּלוֹמַר: זֶה הָרוּחַ וְהָרָצוֹן לָבְשׁוֹ, שֶׁקָּפַץ בָּרֹאשׁ לְדַבֵּר לִפְנֵי בִּנְיָמִין וִיהוּדָה הַבָּאִים עִמּוֹ; וְדֻגְמָתוֹ ״וַתְּהִי עַל יִפְתָּח רוּחַ ה׳״ (שופטים יא:כט). וְאָמַר: **לְךָ דָוִיד.** אַתָּה אוֹמֵר **אִם לְשָׁלוֹם בָּאתֶם**, וַדַּאי לְשָׁלוֹם בָּאנוּ אֵלֶיךָ. **וְעִמְּךָ בֶן יִשַׁי.** אָנוּ רוֹצִים לִהְיוֹת עִמְּךָ וּלְעָזְרֶךָ:


**And a spirit enwrapped Amasai** - Heb. רוּחַ, talent, a will or desire, not a spirit of prophecy; i.e., this desire and will enwrapped him so that he sprang first to speak before the sons of Benjamin and Judah who came with him. A similar instance is (Jud. 11:29): “And a spirit of the Lord was upon Jephthah” and he said: **To you, David** You say, “Have you come to me in peace?” We have surely come to you in peace. **and with you, O son of Jesse** We wish to be with you and at your assistance.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:19:2)


**שָׁלוֹם שָׁלוֹם לְךָ וְשָׁלוֹם לְעֹזְרֶךָ.** וְאַתָּה שׁוֹאֵל אֵלַי לְעָזְרְךָ, וַדַּאי לְעָזְרְךָ בָּאנוּ; וְלָנוּ אֵין לְךָ לְהַחֲזִיק טוֹבָה, כִּי מֵאֵת הַשֵּׁם הוּא; וְזֶהוּ **כִּי עֲזָרְךָ אֱלֹהֶיךָ**:


**peace, peace to you and peace to your helper** And you ask me to help you? We have surely come to help you, but you need not thank us because it is from the Lord, and that is the meaning of “for your God has helped you.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:19:3)


**וַיְּקַבְּלֵם דָּוִד.** לְאוֹתָם בְּנֵי בִנְיָמִין, **וַיִּתְּנֵם בְּרָאשֵׁי הַגְּדוּד** שֶׁלּוֹ:


**and David received them** those sons of Benjamin and placed them at the head of his troop.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:20:1)


**וּמִמְּנַשֶּׁה נָפְלוּ עַל דָּוִיד.** לְעָזְרוֹ:


**And from Manasseh there defected to David** to help him.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:20:2)


**בְּבוֹאוֹ עִם פְּלִשְׁתִּים עַל שָׁאוּל לַמִּלְחָמָה.** בְּאוֹתָהּ מִלְחָמָה שֶׁנֶּהֱרַג בָּהּ שָׁאוּל:


**when he came with the Philistines against Saul in battle** in that war in which Saul was slain.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:20:3)


**וְלֹא עֲזָרוּם.** חַס וְשָׁלוֹם שֶׁעָזְרוּ לַפְּלִשְׁתִּים, אֶלָּא **בְּעֵצָה שִׁלְּחֻהוּ סַרְנֵי פְלִשְׁתִּים** – אֶלָּא כִּי עֵצָה נִתְיַעֲצוּ הַסְּרָנִים לְשַׁלְּחוֹ לְבֵיתוֹ, כִּי אָמְרוּ: **בְּרָאשֵׁינוּ יִפּוֹל אֶל אֲדֹנָיו**; דּוּגְמַת **נָפְלוּ עַל דָּוִיד**:


**but they did not help them** God forbid that they helped the Philistines, but with a counsel the chiefs of the Philistines sent him away. The chiefs of the Philistines took counsel to send him home because they said, “With our heads he will defect to his master,” an expression similar to “defected to David”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:22:1)


**עַל הַגְּדוּד.** שֶׁל עֲמָלֵקִים, כְּשֶׁנִּשְׁבּוּ נָשָׁיו, כִּי בְּאוֹתוֹ פֶּרֶק הָיָה, כִּי בְּלֶכְתּוֹ אֶל צִקְלַג כְּשֶׁשְּׁלָחוּהוּ סַרְנֵי פְלִשְׁתִּים וּבָא אֶל צִקְלַג, כְּבָר נִשְׁבּוּ (שמואל א ל):


**against the troop** of Amalekites when his wives were captured; it was at that time, for when he went to Ziklag when the chiefs of the Philistines sent him away and he came to Ziklag, they had already been captured.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:23:1)


**כִּי לְעֵת יוֹם בְּיוֹם יָבֹאוּ עַל דָּוִד לְעָזְרוֹ.** פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: מַה לְּךָ לְסַפֵּר יוֹתֵר מִי וָמִי בָּאוּ אֵלָיו, אֶלָּא סוֹף דָּבָר: בְּכָל עֵת וָעֵת בָּאִים אֶל דָּוִד:


**For from day to day [men] came to David to help him** The meaning is: What is the purpose of continuing to recount who came to him? The final analysis is that at all times people were coming to David.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:23:2)


**עַד לְמַחֲנֶה גָדוֹל כְּמַחֲנֵה אֱלֹהִים.** פִּתְרוֹן: לֹא הָיָה הַמַּחֲנֶה שֶׁל דָּוִד לִהְיוֹת כָּךְ גָּדוֹל, אֶלָּא כִּי מֵהַשֵּׁם הָיָה הַמַּחֲנֶה כָּךְ גָּדוֹל, שֶׁבָּאוּ לוֹ בַּעֲצַת אֱלֹהִים; וְזֶהוּ כְּמַחֲנֵה אֱלֹהִים; וְדוּגְמָא: ״מַחֲנֵה אֱלֹהִים״ דְּיַעֲקֹב (בראשית לב:ג). וְיֵשׁ לוֹמַר: מֵרוֹב חֲיָלוֹת קָרְהוּ מַחֲנֵה אֱלֹהִים:


**until it became a huge camp, like the camp of God** The meaning is that David’s camp should not have been so large, but from God the camp was so large, because they came to him by the counsel of God. And that is the meaning of “like the camp of God.” A similar instance of this is (Gen. 32: 2): “the camp of God” of Jacob. We can also say that because of the magnitude of his armies, it was called “the camp of God.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:24:1)


**לְהָסֵב מַלְכוּת שָׁאוּל אֵלָיו.** מִן בְּנֵי שָׁאוּל; וְדֻגְמָא: ״וַתִּסֹּב הַמְּלוּכָה״ (מלכים א ב:טו):


**to turn Saul’s kingdom to him** from Saul’s sons. A similar instance is (I Kings 2:15): “...but the kingdom turned about.”



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:25:1)


**בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה נֹשְׂאֵי צִנָּה וָרֹמַח שֵׁשֶׁת אֲלָפִים וּשְׁמוֹנֶה מֵאוֹת.** וְאַל תִּתְמַהּ שֶׁמִּיהוּדָה בָּאוּ לוֹ מְעַט מִכָּל שֵׁבֶט וָשֵׁבֶט שֶׁבָּא לוֹ, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵין צָרִיךְ לָבוֹא לוֹ שׁוּם אָדָם מִיהוּדָה לְהַמְלִיכֵהוּ, כִּי בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה הֵם הִמְלִיכוּהוּ כְּבָר בְּחֶבְרוֹן, אֲבָל יֶתֶר הַשְּׁבָטִים שֶׁהָיוּ עִם בְּנֵי שָׁאוּל עַד עַתָּה הֵם הֻצְרְכוּ לָבוֹא בְּחֶבְרוֹן לְהַמְלִיכוֹ:


**The sons of Judah, bearing shield and spear, six thousand and eight hundred** Do not wonder that the smallest number of all the [people from] the tribes that came to him were from Judah, because it was not necessary for anyone to come to him from Judah to crown him since the sons of Judah had already crowned him in Hebron. But the remaining tribes, who had been with Saul’s sons until now - had to come to Hebron to crown him.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:25:2)


**חֲלוּצֵי צָבָא.** הַמּוֹצִיאִים מִן הָעָם; וְדֻגְמָתוֹ: ״חֲלוּצֵי צָבָא״ דְּמַטּוֹת (במדבר לא:ה). וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: חֲלוּצִים – כְּמוֹ זְרִיזִים, כְּמוֹ ״רְצֵה וְהַחֲלִיצֵנוּ״ (ויקרא רבה לד:טו):


**armed men of the army** - Heb. חֲלוּצֵי צָבָא, those taken out of the people, and a similar case is found in חֲלוּצֵי צָבָא, of the Portion מַטּוֹת. Some say that חֲלוּצִים means armed, like רְצֵה וְהַחֲלִיצֵנוּ: be pleased and strengthen us.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:28:1)


**וִיהוֹיָדָע הַנָּגִיד לְאַהֲרֹן.** הוּא אָבִיו שֶׁל בְּנָיָהוּ (דברי הימים א יא:כד):


**And Jehoiada was the ruler of Aaron** He is the father of Benaiah.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:28:2)


**הַנָּגִיד לְאַהֲרֹן.** הַמּוֹצִיא וְהַמֵּבִיא:


**the ruler of Aaron** who would lead them out and bring them in.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:28:3)


**וְעִמּוֹ שְׁלֹשֶׁת אֲלָפִים וּשְׁבַע מֵאוֹת.** כֻּלָּם מִבְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן הַכֹּהֵן. וִיהוֹיָדָע שֶׁהָיָה בִּימֵי יוֹאָשׁ (מלכים ב יא:ד) – בֶּן בְּנָיָהוּ בְּנוֹ שֶׁל יְהוֹיָדָע:


**three thousand and seven hundred** all of them were descendants of Aaron the Priest, and Jehoiada, who was in the time of Joash, was the son of Benaiah the son of Jehoiada.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:29:1)


**וְצָדוֹק נַעַר.** לְפִיכָךְ אַל תִּתְמַהּ שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה נָגִיד:


**And Zadok was a youth** Therefore, do not wonder that he was not the ruler.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:29:2)


**וּבֵית אָבִיו שָׂרִים עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁנָיִם.** זֶה הִיא גְּדֻלָּה גְדוֹלָה, וְעַל כֵּן לָקְחוּ הַכְּהוּנָּה גְדוֹלָה מֵאֶבְיָתָר וּנְתָנוּהָ לְצָדוֹק הַכֹּהֵן (מלכים א ב:כז, לה):


**and his father’s house had twenty-two officers** This is a great honor; therefore, they took the high priesthood from Ebiathar and gave it to Zadok the Priest.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:30:1)


**וּמִן בְּנֵי בִנְיָמִן אֲחֵי שָׁאוּל וְגוֹ׳.** וְאַל תִּתְמַהּ שֶׁלֹּא בָּאוּ אֶלָּא כִּי אִם שְׁלֹשֶׁת אֲלָפִים, כִּי **עַד הֵנָּה מַרְבִּיתָם שֹׁמְרִים מִשְׁמֶרֶת** בְּנֵי **שָׁאוּל** לְעוֹזְרָן:


**And of the sons of Benjamin, Saul’s brethren, etc.** Do not wonder that only three thousand came to him, because “heretofore their majority kept the watch of the house of Saul,” to help them.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:33:1)


**וּמִבְּנֵי יִשָּׂשכָר יוֹדְעֵי בִינָה לַעִתִּים.** שֶׁיּוֹדְעִים לָתֵת עֵצָה לְפִי הָעִנְיָן שֶׁל מַעֲשֶׂה, שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לְדָוִד לְהִתְיָעֵץ הֵיאַךְ יְחַזֵּק הַמַּלְכוּת מִפְּנֵי בְּנֵי שָׁאוּל:


**those who had an understanding of the times** They knew how to give counsel appropriate to the incident; David had to take counsel on how to strengthen the kingdom against the sons of Saul.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:33:2)


**רָאשֵׁיהֶם מָאתַיִם.** לְבַד מִן הַשְּׁאָר:


**their chiefs were two hundred** besides the rest.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:33:3)


**וְכָל אֲחֵיהֶם עַל פִּיהֶם.** עַל עֲצָתָם:


**and all their brethren obeyed their word** on their counsel.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:34:1)


**וְלַעֲדֹר.** לְשׁוֹן עִדּוּר מַמָּשׁ, כְּמוֹ ״כָּל הֶהָרִים אֲשֶׁר בַּמַּעְדֵּר יֵעָדֵרוּן״ (ישעיה ז:כה), וּמִתּוֹךְ כָּךְ יַעַמְדוּ הֵם כְּעוֹרְכֵי הַמִּלְחָמָה. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים **לַעֲדֹר** – כְּמוֹ לַעֲרֹךְ **מִלְחָמָה**:


**wage war** וְלַעֲדר, an expression of digging, like (Isa. 7:25): “And all the mountains that will be dug with a spade (בַּמַעְדֵּר יֵעָדֵרוּן),” and as a result, those who set up the battle array will stand there. Some say that לַעֲדוֹר is like לַעֲרֹך, to set up a battle array.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:34:2)


**בְּלֹא לֵב וָלֵב.** מְפֹרָשׁ בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה: בֵּין בְּמִתְכַּוֵּין בֵּין שֶׁלֹּא בְּמִתְכַּוֵּין הָיוּ מְנַצְּחִין:


**of double heart** It is explained in *Genesis Rabbah* (*Lev. Rabbah* 25:2): “... whether with intention or without intention, they would be victorious.”



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:38:1)


**וְגַם כָּל שְׁרִית יִשְׂרָאֵל לֵב אֶחָד לְהַמְלִיךְ אֶת דָּוִד.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא בָּאוּ לְחֶבְרוֹן; לְכָךְ שְׁאֵרִית חָסֵר אָלֶף, כְּלוֹמַר: אֲפִלּוּ שְׁאֵרִית לֹא הָיָה, שֶׁלֹּא נִשְׁתַּיְּרוּ כִּי אִם מְעַט, כִּי כֻלָּם בָּאוּ לְחֶבְרוֹן בְּשִׂמְחָה:


**and also the entire remnant of Israel was of one heart to crown David** even though they did not come to Hebron. For this reason, שֵׁרִית is missing an “aleph,” to imply that there was not even a remnant. Only a few remained, for they all came to Hebron joyfully.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:39:1)


**וַיִּהְיוּ שָׁם עִם דָּוִיד יָמִים שְׁלֹשָׁה.** וְאַל תִּתְמַהּ הֵיכָן לָקְחוּ כָּל הַחַיִל הַזֶּה כָּל כָּךְ אֲכִילָה וּשְׁתִיָּה שֶׁהָיָה מַסְפִּיק לָהֶם, כִּי אֲחֵיהֶם הֵכִינוּ לָהֶם מִקּוֹדֶם לָכֵן:


**And they were there with David for three days** And do not wonder where this whole army obtained so much food and drink that sufficed them, because their brethren had prepared it for them beforehand.



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 12:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/12:41:1)


**כִּי שִׂמְחָה בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל.** שֶׁהָיָה לָהֶם מֶלֶךְ מַצְלִיחַ וּמַשְׂכִּיל:


**for there was joy in Israel** that they had a prosperous and wise king.



## Chapter 13


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:1:1)


**וַיִּוָּעֵץ דָּוִיד עִם שָׂרֵי הָאֲלָפִים.** שֶׁאָמַר לָהֶם דָּוִיד: כְּבָר עֲסַקְתֶּם בְּתַקָּנַתְכֶם לִהְיוֹת לָכֶם מֶלֶךְ לְהוֹשִׁיעַ לָכֶם, וְעַתָּה תַּעַסְקוּ בִּכְבוֹד אֱלֹהִים:


**And David conferred with the officers of the thousands** He said to them, “You have already engaged in matters that are for your benefit, for you have a king to save you; now you should engage in the honor of God.”



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:2:1)


**נִפְרְצָה נִשְׁלְחָה.** נִפְרְצָה אוֹתָהּ גָּדֵר שֶׁהָיְתָה גְדוּרָה עַד עַתָּה, שֶׁלֹּא דָרַשׁ שׁוּם אָדָם לֵאלֹהִים, וְ**נִשְׁלְחָה עַל אַחֵינוּ הַנִּשְׁאָרִים** מִשּׁוּם כְּבוֹד הָאָרוֹן:


**it has been opened** The fence that was closed until now has been opened, for no one has yet inquired of God. **let us send to our brethren remaining** because of the honor of the Ark.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:5:1)


**מִן שִׁיחוֹר מִצְרַיִם וְעַד לְבֹא חֲמָת.** דֻּגְמָא: ״מִלְּבוֹא חֲמָת עַד נַחַל מִצְרָיִם״ (מלכים א ח:סה; דברי הימים ב ז:ח):


**from Shihor of Egypt and until the approach to Hamath** This parallels (I Kings 8:65): “from the approach to Hamath to the brook of Egypt”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:6:1)


**בַּעֲלָתָה אֶל קִרְיַת.** פִּתְרוֹן: בַּעֲלָתָה שֶׁהִיא קִרְיַת יְעָרִים:


**to Baalah, to Kirjath** The meaning is: to Baalah, which is Kirjath-Jearim.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:6:2)


**לְהַעֲלוֹת מִשָּׁם וְגוֹ׳.** כְּדִכְתִיב (שמואל ב ו:ב) ״אֲשֶׁר נִקְרָא שֵׁם ה׳ צְבָאוֹת יוֹשֵׁב הַכְּרוּבִים עָלָיו״, כְּלוֹמַר: שְׁמוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא נִקְרָא עַל הָאָרוֹן; אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁגָּלָה אָרוֹן לַפְּלִשְׁתִּים, לֹא זָז מִמֶּנּוּ שֵׁם הַזֶּה, שֶׁנִּתְקַדֵּשׁ:


**to bring up from there, etc.** As it is written (II Sam. 6:2): “... which is called a name, the name of the Lord of Hosts, Who dwells upon the cherubim;” i.e., the name of the Holy One, blessed be He, was called upon the Ark. Although the Ark had been exiled to [the land of] the Philistines, this name did not leave it, because it was hallowed.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:7:1)


**וַיַּרְכִּיבוּ אֶת אֲרוֹן הָאֱלֹהִים עַל עֲגָלָה חֲדָשָׁה.** מָה רָאָה דָוִד שֶׁהִרְכִּיבוֹ עַל עֲגָלָה חֲדָשָׁה, וְלֹא בְמַשָּׂא הַלְוִיִּם, כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה כְּשֶׁהֵבִיא אוֹתוֹ מִשָּׁם לְצִיּוֹן בָּאַחֲרוֹנָה? לְפִי שֶׁאָמַר דָּוִד: כָּל עוּמַת שֶׁבָּא אֵלַי כְּשֶׁהֵשִׁיבוּהוּ פְּלִשְׁתִּים – עַל עֲגָלָה חֲדָשָׁה, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וַיָּשִׂימוּ אֶת הָאָרוֹן ה׳ עַל עֲגָלָה וְגוֹ׳״ (שמואל א ו:יא) לְפִיכָךְ נֶעֱנַשׁ:


**And they set the Ark of God on a new cart** Why did David see fit to set it on a new cart rather than to have it borne by the Levites, as he did when he finally brought it from there to Zion? Because David said, “Corresponding to the way it came to me when the Philistines returned it on a new cart, as it is written (I Sam. 6:11): And they placed the Ark of the Lord on the cart, etc.” Therefore, he was punished.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:7:2)


**עֻזָּא וְאַחְיוֹ.** בְּנֵי אֲבִינָדָב הָיוּ, כְּדִכְתִיב בִּשְׁמוּאֵל (שמואל ב ו:ג):


**and Uzza and Ahio** They were the sons of Abinadab, as is written in II Samuel (6:3).


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:7:3)


**נוֹהֲגִים בָּעֲגָלָה.** כְּמִנְהַג נוֹהֲגֵי עֲגָלָה: אֶחָד הוֹלֵךְ לִפְנֵי הָעֲגָלָה וּמְיַשְּׁרָהּ בְּדֶרֶךְ יְשָׁרָה, וְאֶחָד בְּצַד הָעֲגָלָה שֶׁלֹּא תֵּהָפֵךְ לִיפּוֹל; וְכֵן כְּתִיב ״וְאַחְיוֹ הוֹלֵךְ לִפְנֵי הָאָרוֹן״ (שמואל ב ו:ד), וְעֻזָּא בְּצַד הָעֲגָלָה (שמואל ב ו:ד), לְפִיכָךְ שָׁלַח עֻזָּא אֶת יָדוֹ לֶאֱחוֹז בָּאָרוֹן כְּשֶׁ״שָּׁמְטוּ הַבָּקָר״ (שמואל ב ו:ו), וּלְכָךְ נֶעֱנַשׁ (שמואל ב ו:ז):


**drove the cart** as is the custom of cart drivers, one walks in front of the cart and directs it in a straight way, and one is alongside the cart, so that it does not turn over and fall. And so it is written (II Sam. 6: 4): “... and Ahio went before the Ark,” and Uzza was alongside the cart; therefore, Uzza stretched forth his hand to take hold of the Ark when the oxen swayed it, and therefore, he was punished.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:8:1)


כָּאן כְּתִיב **בְּכָל עֹז** וּבִשְׁמוּאֵל כְּתִיב ״בְּכָל עֲצֵי״ (שמואל ב ו:ה)! פִּתְרוֹן: ״בְּכָל עֲצֵי״ כְּלֵי שִׁיר, וְכָאן פִּתְרוֹן: בְּכָל עֹז כְּלֵי שִׁיר:


**with all [their] might** Here it is written: בְּכָל עֹז, with all strength, and in II Samuel (6:5): “בְּכָל עֲצֵי, with all wood.” The meaning is: with all manner of wooden musical instruments, and here the meaning is: with all powerful musical instruments.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:8:2)


**וּבְשִׁירִים וּבְכִנּוֹרוֹת וּבִנְבָלִים וּבְתֻפִּים.** כֻּלָּם כְּלֵי שִׁיר הֵם, אֲבָל מְצִלְתַּיִם כְּלִי שִׁיר הָיָה, וְלֹא הָיָה עָשׂוּי כִּי אִם לְהַשְׁמָעַת קוֹל; וְדֻגְמָתוֹ: כְּלִי שִׁיר שֶׁקּוֹרִין פויקי״ן בְּלַ״א, שֶׁמַּכִּין עָלָיו בְּמַקְלוֹת וְהוּא מַשְׁמִיעַ קוֹל; וְכֵן מוֹכִיחַ לְקַמָּן ״וּבִמְצִלְתַּיִם מַשְׁמִיעִים״ (דה״א טו:כח), וּכְתִיב ״וְהַמְשׁוֹרְרִים הֵימָן אָסָף וְאֵיתָן בִּמְצִלְתַּיִם נְחֹשֶׁת לְהַשְׁמִיעַ״ (דברי הימים א טו:יט), וְעוֹד לְמַטָּה כְּתִיב ״בְּשִׁיר בֵּית ה׳ בִּמְצִלְתַּיִם וְגוֹמֵר״ (דברי הימים א כה:ו); וְעַל שֵׁם כָּךְ נִקְרָא מְצִלְתַּיִם. וְכֵן ״יִירַשׁ הַצְּלָצַל״ (דברים כח:מב) – מִין אַרְבֶּה הַמַּשְׁמִיעַ קוֹל, כְּדִכְתִיב בַּנְּבוּאוֹת עַל הָאַרְבֶּה ״כְּקוֹל מַרְכָּבוֹת עַל רָאשֵׁי הֶהָרִים יְרַקֵּדוּן וְגוֹ׳״ (יואל ב:ה):


**and with songs and with harps and with psalteries and with timbrels** All of these are musical instruments, but cymbals (מְצִלְתַּיִם) were not a musical instrument, and their function was only to make sounds, like the instrument called the kettle drum, *Pauken* in German, which is struck with sticks to make sounds. This is implied further (15: 25): “and with resounding cymbals,” and it is written (ibid. verse 19): “And the singers: Heman, Asaph, and Ethan, with copper cymbals to resound.” And it is written further below (25:6): “with song in the House of the Lord, with cymbals, etc.,” and because of this, they are called מְצִלְתַּיִם. Similarly (Deut. 28:42): “... the locusts (הַצְּלָצַל) will inherit.” This is a species of locust that makes a sound, as it is written in the prophecy of Joel (2:5) about the locusts: “Like the sound of chariots on the mountaintops, they will leap, etc.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:9:1)


**עַד גֹּרֶן כִּידוֹן.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם:


**to Goren Kiddon** the name of a place.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:10:1)


**וַיַּכֵּהוּ עַל אֲשֶׁר שָׁלַח יָדוֹ עַל הָאָרוֹן.** וְהוּא שֶׁכָּתוּב בִּשְׁמוּאֵל ״וַיַּכֵּהוּ שָׁם הָאֱלֹהִים עַל הַשַּׁל״ (שמואל ב ו:ז); פִּתְרוֹן: עַל הַשֶּׁלַח, עַל שֶׁאָחַז בָּאָרוֹן. וְדֻגְמָתוֹ: ״הַכְּלָבִים יֹאכְלוּ אֶת״ בְּשַׂר ״אִיזֶבֶל בְּחֵיל יִזְרְעֶאל״ (מלכים א כא:כג), וּפָסוּק אַחֵר כְּתִיב ״בְּחֵלֶק יִזְרְעֶאל״ (מלכים ב ט:י). כָּךְ נְפָרֵשׁ כָּאן ״עַל הַשַּׁל״ – הַשֶּׁלַח:


**and He struck him down because he stretched forth his hand upon the Ark** This is what is written in II Samuel (6:7): “... and God struck him down there” עַל הַשַּׁל meaning עַל הַשֶּׁלַח, because he stretched forth to hold the Ark. Another example of this is (I Kings 21:23): “The dogs will devour Jezebel” בְּחֵל יִזְרְעֶאל. In another verse it is written: (II Kings 9:10): “בְּחֵלֶק יִזְרְעֶאל,” in the territory of Jezreel. So will we explain עַל הַשַּׁל here as הַשֶּׁלַח


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:10:2)


**וַיָּמָת שָׁם לִפְנֵי אֱלֹהִים.** לִפְנֵי אֲרוֹן אֱלֹהִים; בִּשְׁמוּאֵל כְּתִיב ״וַיָּמָת שָׁם עִם אֲרוֹן הָאֱלֹהִים״ (שמואל ב ו:ז):


**and he died there before God** before the Ark of God, and in (II Samuel 6:7): “...and there he died by the Ark of God.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:12:1)


**וַיִּירָא דָוִיד אֶת הָאֱלֹהִים בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא לֵאמֹר.** מַה הוּא יָרֵא לֵאמֹר? שֶׁאָמַר: **הֵיךְ אָבִיא אֵלַי אֲרוֹן הָאֱלֹהִים**:


**And David feared God, etc. saying** What did he fear? Saying - that he said, “How will I bring the Ark of God to me?”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 13:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/13:13:1)


**הַגִּתִּי.** מִגַּת הָיָה בִּתְחִלָּה, מִקֹּדֶם הָיָה מִתְגּוֹרֵר בְּגַת; וְלֵוִי הָיָה, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וַיֹּאמֶר דָּוִיד לְשָׂרֵי הַלְוִיִּם לְהַעֲמִיד אֶת אֲחֵיהֶם הַמְשֹׁרְרִים״ (דברי הימים א טו:טז), וְכָתִיב בְּסָמוּךְ ״וְעֹבֵד אֱדֹם וִיחִיָּה שׁוֹעֲרִים לָאָרוֹן״ (דברי הימים א טו:כד):


**the Gittite** He was from Gath originally and he previously sojourned in Gath. He was a Levite, as it is written (below 15:16): “And David said to the officers of the Levites to station their brethren, the singers,” and it says shortly after (verse 24): “... and Obed-Edom and Jehiah were gate sentries for the Ark.”



## Chapter 14


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 14:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:1:1)


**וַיִּשְׁלַח חִירָם מֶלֶךְ צוֹר.** מְפֹרָשׁ בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (בראשית רבה פה:ד): לָמוּד הָיָה הָאִישׁ הַזֶּה לִהְיוֹת אוֹהֵב לְשֵׁבֶט יְהוּדָה; כֵּן עָשָׂה חִירָה הָעֲדֻלָּמִי לִיהוּדָה (בראשית לח:יב):


**And Hiram the king of Tyre sent** It is explained in *Genesis Rabbah* (85:4): “This man was accustomed to being friendly to the tribe of Judah; so was Hirah the Adullamite to Judah.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 14:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:1:2)


**וְחָרָשֵׁי קִיר וְחָרָשֵׁי עֵצִים.** אֻמָּנִים:


**and masons and carpenters** craftsmen.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 14:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:2:1)


**וַיֵּדַע דָּוִיד כִּי הֱכִינוֹ ה׳ לְמֶלֶךְ.** כְּשֶׁרָאָה שֶׁשָּׁלְחוּ לוֹ מַלְכֵי עַמִּים תְּשׁוּרוֹת:


**And David knew that the Lord had established him as king** when he saw that the kings of the nations sent him gifts.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 14:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:3:1)


**וַיִּקַּח דָּוִיד וְגוֹ׳.** וְכָל זֶה חָזְקוֹ שֶׁל דָּוִד:


**and David took** All this was David’s strength.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 14:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:8:1)


**וַיִּשְׁמְעוּ פְלִשְׁתִּים כִּי נִמְשַׁח דָּוִיד לְמֶלֶךְ עַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וַיַּעֲלוּ כָל פְּלִשְׁתִּים.** לְפִי שֶׁפְּלִשְׁתִּים הָיוּ מוֹשְׁלִים כָּל הַיָּמִים עַד שֶׁבָּא שָׁאוּל וְדָוִד, כְּדִכְתִיב בְּשִׁמְשׁוֹן ״וַיֹּאמְרוּ לְשִׁמְשׁוֹן הֲלֹא יָדַעְתָּ כִּי מֹשְׁלִים בָּנוּ פְּלִשְׁתִּים״ (שופטים טו:יא), וְכֵן ״הִתְחַזְּקוּ וִהְיוּ לַאֲנָשִׁים פְּלִשְׁתִּים פֶּן תַּעַבְדוּ לָעִבְרִים כַּאֲשֶׁר עָבְדוּ לָכֶם וְגוֹ׳״ (שמואל א ד:ט), וְכֵן לְמַעְלָה בְּפָרָשָׁה זוֹ ״וַיִּרְאוּ כָל אִישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹמֵר וַיַּעַזְבוּ עָרֵיהֶם וַיָּנוּסוּ וַיָּבֹאוּ פְלִשְׁתִּים וַיֵּשְׁבוּ בָּהֶם״ (דברי הימים א י:ז). בְּאוֹתָן שֶׁבַע שָׁנִים שֶׁמָּלַךְ דָּוִד בְּחֶבְרוֹן לְבַד לֹא אָמְרוּ דָּבָר וְלֹא חָשְׁשׁוּ עַל שֶׁמָּלַךְ דָּוִד עַל חֶבְרוֹן, כִּי אָמְרוּ מַה לָּנוּ שֶׁמָּא נְצִיב אוֹ שַׂר מִנָּהוּ לְדָוִד עַל חֶבְרוֹן; אֲבָל כְּשֶׁנִּמְשַׁח לְמֶלֶךְ עַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל, אָז עָלוּ כָל הַפְּלִשְׁתִּים **לְבַקֵּשׁ אֶת דָּוִד**, שֶׁלֹּא רָצוּ שֶׁיְּהֵא מֶלֶךְ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, אֶלָּא שֶׁעוֹד הֵם יִהְיוּ מוֹשְׁלִים עֲלֵיהֶם:


**And the Philistines heard that David had been anointed king over all Israel, and all the Philistines went up** because the Philistines had been ruling all the time until Saul and David came, as it is written (Jud. 15:11) concerning Samson: “Did you not know that the Philistines rule over us?” And likewise, (I Samuel 4:9): “Strengthen yourselves and become men, you Philistines, lest you serve the Hebrews, as they served you, etc.” Likewise, above in this portion (10:7): “And all the men of Israel saw, etc. and they abandoned their cities and fled, and the Philistines came and settled in them.” And in those seven years that David reigned in Hebron alone they did not say a word, and they were not concerned that David reigned over Hebron, because they said, “What do we care? Perhaps a governor or an officer appointed David over Hebron.” But when he was anointed king over all Israel, the Philistines all went up to seek David, for they did not want there to be a king in Israel, but that they should continue ruling over them.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 14:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:10:1)


**וַיִּשְׁאַל דָּוִיד בֵּאלֹהִים.** עַל יְדֵי אוּרִים וְתֻמִּים:


**And David inquired of God** through the Urim and Tummim.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 14:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:11:1)


**וַיַּעֲלוּ בְּבַעַל פְּרָצִים.** שֶׁנִּקְרָא אַחֲרֵי כֵן בַּעַל פְּרָצִים עַל שֵׁם מַעֲשֶׂה אֲשֶׁר **פָּרַץ הָאֱלֹהִים אֶת** אוֹיְבָיו בְּיָדוֹ, שֶׁנָּסוּ וְנִפְרְצוּ זֶה מֵעַל זֶה:


**And they went up to Baal-Perazim** which was later called Baal-Perazim because of the incident that occurred there. **God has broken my enemies in my hand** meaning that they fled and were broken off from one another.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 14:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:11:2)


**כְּפֶרֶץ מָיִם.** כְּגַלֵּי מַיִם שֶׁנִּפְרָצִים מִמְּקוֹמָם וְנוֹפְלִים לְמָקוֹם אַחֵר:


**like a breach of waters** like waves of water that break from their place and fall somewhere else.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 14:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/14:14:1)


**הָסֵב מֵעֲלֵיהֶם.** אָמְרָה מִדַּת הַדִּין לִפְנֵי הַמָּקוֹם: רִבּוֹנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם, לָמָּה הֶעֱבַרְתָּ אֶת שָׁאוּל מִלִּפְנֵי דָוִד? אָמַר לָהּ: עַל שֶׁלֹּא הִמְתִּין לִשְׁמוּאֵל שִׁבְעַת יָמִים כַּאֲשֶׁר צִוָּהוּ (שמואל א יג:ח-יד); אָמַר לָהּ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: אֲנַסֶּה עַתָּה אֶת דָּוִד: הָסֵב מֵעֲלֵיהֶם וְגוֹ׳; וְכָל זֶה שָׁמַר דָּוִד (שוחר טוב כז:ב):


**circle them** The Divine Standard of Justice said to the Omnipresent, “Lord of the universe, why did You dismiss Saul for David?” [God] replied, “Because he did not wait seven days for Samuel as he had commanded him” [as in I Sam. 13:8–14]. The Holy One, blessed be He, said [further], “Now I shall test David: Circle them.” And David observed this.



## Chapter 15


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:2:1)


**כִּי אִם הַלְוִיִּם כִּי בָם בָּחַר.** כְּלוֹמַר: בַּלְוִיִּם בָּחַר וְלֹא בָּעֲגָלָה, שֶׁלְּפִי שֶׁהִרְכַּבְנוּהוּ בָּעֲגָלָה נֶעֱנַשׁ עֻזָּה; וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַטָּה **כִּי לְמִבָּרִאשׁוֹנָה לֹא אַתֶּם פָּרַץ ה׳ אֱלֹהֵינוּ בָּנוּ כִּי לֹא דְרַשְׁנוּהוּ כַּמִּשְׁפָּט** – שֶׁהִרְכַּבְנוּהוּ בָּעֲגָלָה:


**except the Levites, for the Lord chose them** i.e., God chose the Levites [to carry the Ark], and not a cart, for because we placed it in a cart, Uzza was punished, and that is what is stated further: “For from the beginning, [when] you were not [the bearers], the Lord our God made a breach in us, for we had not sought Him properly,” for we placed it in a cart.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:15:1)


**כִּדְבַר ה׳ בִּכְתֵפָם.** לָשֵׂאת אוֹתוֹ; וְלֹא מַמָּשׁ בִּכְתֵפָם, כִּי אִם **בַּמּוֹטוֹת עֲלֵיהֶם**:


**according to the word of the Lord, on their shoulders** to carry it, not actually on their shoulders, but with staves upon them.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:18:1)


**וְעִמָּהֶם אֲחֵיהֶם הַמִּשְׁנִים.** מִשְׁנֶה לָהֶם לַשּׁוֹעֲרִים:


**And with them, their brethren the second in rank** second in rank to them as gate sentries.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:18:2)


**זְכַרְיָהוּ בֵּן.** כָּךְ שְׁמוֹ. וְכָל אֵלּוּ מְשׁוֹרְרִים וְשׁוֹעֲרִים הָיוּ בַּדֶּרֶךְ שֶׁהֵבִיאוּ אֶת הָאָרוֹן, וְהַמְּשׁוֹרְרִים וְהַשּׁוֹעֲרִים שֶׁהֶעֱמִיד דָּוִד לְאַחַר שֶׁנָּתְנוּ הָאָרוֹן אֶל הָאֹהֶל אֲשֶׁר נָטָה לוֹ דָּוִד; וּבְמִשְׁכַּן שִׁילֹה הָיוּ שׁוֹעֲרִים. וּבַדֶּרֶךְ לֹא הָיוּ שׁוֹעֲרִים, לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הֻצְרְכוּ כָּל כָּךְ שׁוֹעֲרִים, לְפִיכָךְ נַעֲשׂוּ מְשׁוֹרְרִים חוּץ מִשְּׁנַיִם: בְּרַכְיָה וְאֶלְקָנָה. וְכָל זֶה הָיָה בַּדֶּרֶךְ, אֲבָל בְּנוֹחַ הָאָרוֹן הָיוּ מְשׁוֹרְרִים שׁוֹעֲרִים:


**Zechariah Ben** That was his name; and all these singers and gate sentries [served] on the road by bringing the Ark. The singers and the gate sentries that David stationed after they placed the Ark into the tent that David had pitched for it and in the Tabernacle of Shiloh, were gate sentries, but they were not gate sentries while on the road, because not that many gate sentries were required. Therefore, they became singers, with the exception of two, Berechiah and Elkanah (verse 23). All this was on the road, but when the Ark rested, the singers became gate sentries.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:19:1)


**וְהַמְשֹׁרְרִים הֵימָן אָסָף וְאֵיתָן וְגוֹ׳ לְהַשְׁמִיעַ.** קוֹל. הֵם הָיוּ מְמֻנִּים עַל אוֹתוֹ כְּלִי שִׁיר:


**And the singers: Heman, Asaph, and Ethan, etc.** To make a resounding voice, they were appointed over those musical instruments.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:20:1)


**וּזְכַרְיָה וַעֲזִיאֵל וְגוֹ׳.** אֵלּוּ שֶׁנֶּאֶמְרוּ בַּפָּסוּק לְמַעְלָה שֶׁהָיוּ **שֹׁעֲרִים**, הֶחֱזִירוּ כָּאן לַעֲשׂוֹת מְשׁוֹרְרִים:


**And Zechariah and Aziel, etc.** Those, mentioned in the preceding verse as being gate sentries, were here transferred to become singers.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:20:2)


**עַל עֲלָמוֹת.** עַל אוֹתָן כְּלֵי שִׁיר שֶׁמְּנַגְּנִין בָּהֶם אוֹתָן מִזְמוֹרִים: ״לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַל עֲלָמוֹת וְגוֹ׳״ (תהלים ט:א; מו:א):


**over alamoth** over those instruments with which they played those psalms, entitled: “To the conductor, on alamoth, etc.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:21:1)


**וּמַתִּתְיָהוּ וֶאֱלִיפְלֵהוּ וְגוֹ׳. עַל הַשְּׁמִינִית לְנַצֵּחַ.** – אוֹתוֹ כְּלִי שִׁיר שֶׁמְּנַגְּנִין עָלָיו אוֹתָן מִזְמוֹרִים: ״לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַל הַשְּׁמִינִית״ (תהלים יב:א; וראה תהלים ו:א):


**And Mattithiah and Eliphelehu, etc. with harps on the sheminith to conduct** those musical instruments upon which they play the psalms entitled: “For the conductor, on the sheminith.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:22:1)


**וּכְנַנְיָהוּ שַׂר הַלְוִיִּם בְּמַשָּׂא.** בַּמֶּה הָיָה שַׂר? בְּמַשָּׂא. בְּנָשְׂאוּת הַשִּׁיר:


**And Chenaniah, the leader of the Levites in song** In what was he a leader? In בַּמַּשָּׂא, in raising [the voice in]song.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:22:2)


**יָסֹר בַּמַּשָּׂא.** מְיַסֵּר וּמוֹכִיחַ אוֹתָם עַל הַמַּשָּׂא בְּנִגּוּן נְעִימוֹת שִׁיר, אִם לְהָרִים קוֹל אִם לְהַשְׁפִּיל:


**he would chastise in song** He would chastise and reprove them concerning raising the voice in the melody of the song, whether to raise the voice or lower [it].


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:22:3)


**כִּי מֵבִין הוּא.** בְּמַשָּׂא, בִּנְשִׂיאוּת הַנִּגּוּן. וְלָשׁוֹן מֵבִין נוֹפֵל עַל לִמּוּד הַשִּׁיר, וְדֻגְמָא: ״וַיְהִי מִסְפָּרָם עִם אֲחֵיהֶם מְלֻמְּדֵי שִׁיר לַה׳ כָּל הַמֵּבִין וְגוֹמֵר״ (דברי הימים א כה:ז), וְכָתוּב ״וַיַּפִּילוּ גּוֹרָלוֹת וְגוֹ׳ מֵבִין עִם תַּלְמִיד״ (דברי הימים א כה:ח):


**because he was an expert** in raising [the voice] in the melody, and the expression of מֵבִין applies to the study of voice. A similar instance is found (below 25:7): “And their number with their brethren, trained in song to the Lord, every expert (מֵבִין) etc.,” and it is written (ibid. verse 8): “and they cast lots, etc.,...expert (מֵבִין) with student.”



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:26:1)


**וַיְהִי בֶּעְזֹר הָאֱלֹהִים.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (סוטה לה.): מִכָּאן שֶׁהָאָרוֹן נוֹשֵׂא אֶת עַצְמוֹ:


**And it came to pass when God helped the Levites** - Heb. בֶּעְֹזר. The “beth” of בֶּעְֹזר is vowelized with a “segol,” but as a rule, it is vowelized with a “pattah.” The meaning of “when [God] helped,” is that they did not err when they carried the Ark as they had erred in the beginning when Uzza died. And in the Book of Samuel (II 6:13), it says that when they had trodden six paces, they made this sacrifice. Our Rabbis derived from here that the Ark carried itself.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:27:1)


**וְדָוִיד מְכֻרְבָּל.** כְּמוֹ בְּדָנִיֵּאל ״בְּכַרְבְּלָתְהוֹן״ (דניאל ג:כא), דֻּגְמָא: כַּרְבַּלְתָּא דְּתַרְנְגוֹלָא (ברכות ז.):


**And David was enwrapped in a linen robe** - Heb. מְכֻרְבָּל enwrapped, as in Daniel (3:21): “their robes (וְכַרְבְּלָתְהוֹן).” A similar case is the comb (כַּרְבַּלְתָּא) of a rooster.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:27:2)


**וְכָל הַלְוִיִּם.** כְּמוֹ כֵן מְכֻרְבָּלִים **בִּמְעִיל בּוּץ**; וּלְפִי שֶׁהָיָה דָּוִד מְשׁוֹרֵר כְּמוֹ לְוִיִּם, לָבַשׁ גַּם הוּא לְבוּשֵׁיהֶם שֶׁלָּבְשׁוּ גַּם הֵם. וּרְאָיָה לְהַבְדִּיל, כָּךְ הָיָה מִנְהַג מְלָכִים וְשָׂרִים שֶׁל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת, שֶׁאוֹכְלִים עִם כֹּהֲנֵיהֶם כְּחֻקָּם, הָיוּ לוֹבְשִׁים לְבוּשֵׁי כֹּהֲנֵיהֶם:


**and all the Levites** were likewise enwrapped in linen robes, and since David was a singer like the Levites, he too wore their raiments, which they wore, and proof of this - to distinguish between the unclean and the clean: It was the custom of the heathen kings and princes, that when they would dine with their priests according to their custom, they would don the garb of their priests.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:27:3)


**וְעַל דָּוִיד אֵפוֹד בָּד.** כְּאוֹתוֹ אֵפוֹד שֶׁל אַהֲרֹן (שמות כח:ד), שֶׁהָיָה כְּמִין שְׁנֵי לְבָדִין, אֶחָד לְפָנִים וְאֶחָד לְאָחוֹר, וּמַגִּיעַ עַד מָתְנָיו, וְהָיָה הָאֵזוֹר אָרוּג מִמֶּנּוּ וְחָגְרוֹ בּוֹ:


**and on David was a linen ephod** like the ephod of Aaron, which was like two felt cloths, one in the front and one in the back, and it reached to his loins, and the girdle was woven from it, and he would gird himself with it.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 15:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/15:29:1)


**וּמִיכַל בַּת שָׁאוּל וְגוֹ׳.** לְפִי שֶׁסֵּפֶר דִּבְרֵי הַיָּמִים בִּשְׁבִיל כְּבוֹד דָּוִד הוּא, לְפִיכָךְ אֵין כָּתוּב כָּאן מַה אָמְרָה מִיכַל לְדָוִד, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בִּשְׁמוּאֵל: ״מַה נִּכְבָּד הַיּוֹם מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲשֶׁר נִגְלָה הַיּוֹם לְעֵינֵי אַמְהוֹת עֲבָדָיו כְּהִגָּלוֹת נִגְלוֹת אַחַד הָרֵיקִים״ (שמואל ב ו:כ), כִּי זִילוּתָא שֶׁל דָּוִד הָיָה שֶׁאָמְרָה לוֹ אִשָּׁה כֵּן:


**And Michal the daughter of Saul, etc.** Since the Book of Chronicles is for David’s honor, it does not write here what Michal said to David, as is written in Samuel (II 6:20): “How esteemed was the king of Israel today, who appeared today in the eyes of the handmaids of his servants, as one of the idlers would appear!” because it was a disgrace for David that a woman said that to him.



## Chapter 16


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:2:1)


**וַיְבָרֶךְ אֶת הָעָם בְּשֵׁם ה׳.** שֶׁנִּקְרָא עַל הָאָרוֹן, כְּדִכְתִיב לְמַעְלָה ״אֶת אֲרוֹן הָאֱלֹהִים ה׳ יוֹשֵׁב הַכְּרוּבִים אֲשֶׁר נִקְרָא שֵׁם״ (דברי הימים א יג:ו):


**and he blessed the people in the name of the Lord** which was called upon the Ark, as is written above (II Sam. 6:2): “the Ark of God, the Lord, upon which a name was called.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:3:1)


**וְאֶשְׁפָּר.** אֶחָד מִשִּׁשָּׁה בְּפָר:


**and a portion of meat** - Heb. אֶשְּׁפָּר, one sixth of a bull.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:3:2)


**וַאֲשִׁישָׁה.** אֶחָד מִשִּׁשָּׁה בְּהִין. **וַאֲשִׁישָׁה** דּוּגְמַת ״אֲשִׁישֵׁי עֲנָבִים״ (הושע ג:א), צְלוֹחִית (פסחים לו:):


**and a flask of wine** - Heb. וַאֲשִּׁישָּׁה one sixth of a “hin,” similar to (Hos. 3:1): “goblets (אֲשִּׁישֵּׁי) of grapes,” a flask.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:4:1)


**וּלְהַזְכִּיר וּלְהוֹדוֹת וּלְהַלֵּל.** לְהַזְכִּיר לוֹמַר אוֹתָן שְׁנֵי מִזְמוֹרִים שֶׁכָּתוּב בָּהֶם ״מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד לְהַזְכִּיר״ (תהלים לח:א) וּלְהוֹדוֹת ״הוֹדוּ לַה׳ קִרְאוּ בִשְׁמוֹ״ (תהלים קה:א), וּלְהַלֵּל וְאוֹתָן מִזְמוֹרִים שֶׁמַּתְחִילִין הַלְלוּיָהּ:


**and to make mention and to give thanks and to praise** “To make mention” means to recite those two psalms in which it is written (Ps. 38, 70): “A song of David to mention”; “and to thank” [means to recite] (ibid. 105): “Give thanks to the Lord, call out in His name”; “and to praise,” [by reciting] those psalms starting with “Hallelujah.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:6:1)


**וּבְנָיָהוּ וִיחַזְיִאֵל הַכֹּהֲנִים בַּחֲצֹצְרוֹת תָּמִיד.** חֲצוֹצְרוֹת כְּלִי שִׁיר שֶׁל כֹּהֲנִים הוּא, דֻּגְמָא לְמַעְלָה: ״וּשְׁבַנְיָהוּ״ וְגוֹ׳ וּבְנָיָהוּ וֶאֱלִיעֶזֶר הַכֹּהֲנִים מְחַצְצְרִים בַּחֲצֹצְרוֹת״ (דברי הימים א טו:כד):


**And Benaiah and Jahaziel the priests with trumpets constantly** Trumpets were the musical instruments of the priests. A similar case is found above (15:24): “And Shebaniah, etc. and Eliezer. the priests, sounding the trumpets.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:7:1)


**לְהוֹדוֹת לַה׳ בְּיַד אָסָף וְאֶחָיו.** שֶׁאָסָף הָיָה מַתְחִיל: **הוֹדוּ לַה׳ קִרְאוּ בִשְׁמוֹ**, וְאַחֲרֵי כֵן כָּל אֶחָיו עוֹנִים אַחֲרָיו; וְדֻגְמָא בְּעֶזְרָא: ״וּמַתַּנְיָה בֶן מִיכָא בֶּן זַבְדִּי בֶּן אָסָף רֹאשׁ הַתְּחִלָּה יְהוּדֶה לַתְּפִלָּה״ (נחמיה יא:יז) – לִכְלֵי שִׁיר הִידוֹת שְׁמוֹ, כְּדִכְתִיב בְּעֶזְרָא: ״הַלְוִיִּם יֵשׁוּעַ בִּנּוּי קַדְמִיאֵל שֵׁרֵבְיָה יְהוּדָה מַתַּנְיָה עַל הַיְּדוֹת״ (נחמיה יב:ח):


**to give thanks to the Lord by the hand of Asaph and his brethren** Asaph would commence, “Give thanks to the Lord, call out in His name,” and afterwards all his brethren would respond after him. A similar instance is found in Ezra (Neh. 11:17): “And Mattaniah the son of Micha, the son of Zabdi, the son of Asaph the chief to begin the thanksgiving in prayer,” on the instrument of the huyedoth, as it is written in Ezra (ibid. 12:8): “And the Levites were Jeshua, Binnui, Kadmiel, Sherebiah, Judah, Mattaniah over the huyedoth.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:8:1)


**הוֹדִיעוּ בָעַמִּים עֲלִילֹתָיו.** מַעֲשֵׂה גְּבוּרוֹתָיו וְנִפְלְאוֹתָיו שֶׁפָּעַל עִם הָאָרוֹן:


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:8:2)


**קִרְאוּ בִשְׁמוֹ.** קִרְאוּ: ה׳ יַעֲזֹר לַאֲרוֹנוֹ; דֻּגְמָא: ״וּקְרָאתֶם בְּשֵׁם אֱלֹהֵיכֶם וַאֲנִי אֶקְרָא בְּשֵׁם ה׳״ (מלכים א יח:כד) – אֵצֶל ה׳ נוֹפֵל לוֹמַר לְשׁוֹן קְרִיאָה שֶׁנִּפְלָאוֹת עָשָׂה:



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:10:1)


**הִתְהַלְלוּ בְּשֵׁם קָדְשׁוֹ.** לוֹמַר: כָּל מִי שֶׁמַּאֲמִין בֵּאלֹהֵינוּ הוּא יַעַזְרֵנוּ; דֻּגְמָא: ״וּבִקְדוֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל תִּתְהַלָּל״ (ישעיהו מא:טז):


**Glory in His holy name** saying that whoever believes in our God - He will help him. A similar instance is (Isa. 41:16): “... with the Holy One of Israel shall you praise yourself.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:10:2)


**בְּשֵׁם קָדְשׁוֹ.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לְךָ אֱלוֹהַּ קָדוֹשׁ, וְכֵן ״כַּה׳ אֱלֹהֵינוּ בְּכָל קָרְאֵנוּ אֵלָיו״ (דברים ד:ז):


**of His holy name** that you have a holy God like our God, and so (Deut. 4:7): “... like the Lord our God whenever we call out to Him.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:10:3)


**יִשְׂמַח לֵב מְבַקְשֵׁי ה׳.** וּבְהֵיטִיב יָכוֹל לִשְׂמוֹחַ לִבּוֹ מִי שֶׁמְּבַקֵּשׁ לַה׳ בְּלֵב שָׁלֵם. לְכָךְ:


**may the heart of those who seek the Lord rejoice** and when he does good, the heart of he who seeks the Lord with a whole heart is able to rejoice. Therefore...



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:11:1)


**דִּרְשׁוּ ה׳ וְעֻזּוֹ.** מִקְדָּשׁ וְאָרוֹן נִקְרְאוּ עֻזּוֹ, דִּכְתִיב ״וַיִּתֵּן לַשְּׁבִי עֻזּוֹ״ (תהלים עח:סא), שֶׁנָּתַן אֲרוֹנוֹ לַהֲבִיאוֹ בַּשְּׁבִי, וּכְתִיב ״קוּמָה ה׳ לִמְנוּחָתֶךָ אַתָּה וַאֲרוֹן עֻזֶּךָ״ (תהלים קלב:ח). וְעַל כֵּן נִקְרָא אֶלְעָזָר הַשּׁוֹמְרוֹ ״עֻזָּא״, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁשָּׁמַר הָאָרוֹן שֶׁנִּקְרָא עֹז; דּוּגְמָא: עַל שֵׁם ״אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת שֶׁקֶל כֶּסֶף״ (בראשית כג:טו), וְהוּא חֶשְׁבּוֹן גִּמַּטְרִיָּא שֶׁל ״עֶפְרֹן״ (בראשית כג:טז); וְכֵן הַרְבֵּה: ״דָּן יָדִין עַמּוֹ״ (בראשית מט:טז); ״יְהוּדָה יוֹדוּךָ אַחֶיךָ״ (בראשית מט:ח):


**Search for the Lord and His might** The Sanctuary and Ark are called “His might,” as it is written (Ps. 78:61): “And He delivered His might into captivity,” i.e., He delivered His Ark to bring it into captivity, and it is written (ibid. 132:8): “Arise, O Lord, to Your resting place, You and the Ark of Your might.” For that reason, Eleazar, its watchman, was called Uzza, because he guarded the Ark, which was called might (עֹז). An example of this is that [Ephron was called by this name] because of the four hundred silver shekels, which is the numerical value of the name Ephron. And so, many others [as in] (Gen. 49:16): “Dan (דָן) will champion (יָדִין) the right of his people”; (ibid. verse 8): “Judah, (יְהוּדָה) you are the one; to you your brothers do homage (יוֹדוּךָ).”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:12:1)


**זִכְרוּ נִפְלְאוֹתָיו.** מַכַּת עַכְבָּרִים, וְשֶׁלָּקְחוּ פָּרוֹת עָלוֹת וּבְנֵיהֶם כָּלוּ בַּבַּיִת – ״וַיִּשַּׁרְנָה הַפָּרוֹת״ (שמואל א ו:יב) וְלֹא חָזְרוּ לַאֲחוֹרֵיהֶם, וּפֶרֶץ בְּעֻזָּא (דברי הימים א יג:ט-יא). וְכָל הַשִּׁיר נַעֲשָׂה עַל הָאָרוֹן (דברי הימים א יג: ט-יא), וְהָיוּ אוֹמְרִים אוֹתוֹ עַל תָּמִיד שֶׁל שַׁחַר, **וְשִׁירוּ לַה׳** – עַל תָּמִיד שֶׁל בֵּין הָעַרְבַּיִם (סדר עולם רבה יד). וּלְמִי אֲנִי אוֹמֵר שֶׁיִּזְכְּרוּ נִפְלְאוֹתָיו שֶׁל תְּמִים דֵּעִים:


**Remember His wonders** the plague of the mice, and they took milch cows and shut up their young in the house, and the cows went straight (I Sam. 6:10-12), and they did not turn back, and the breach of Uzza. The entire song was composed concerning the Ark, and they would say it for the daily sacrifice in the morning, and “Sing to the Lord,” for the daily sacrifice in the afternoon (*Seder Olam* ch. 14). And to whom do I say that they should remember the wonders of the One Who is perfect in knowledge?

### Verse 15

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:15:1)


**זִכְרוּ לְעוֹלָם בְּרִיתוֹ דָּבָר צִוָּה לְאֶלֶף דּוֹר.** הַתּוֹרָה אֲשֶׁר צִוָּה לְהוֹדִיעַ בָּעוֹלָם אַחַר אֶלֶף דּוֹר, וְרָאָה שֶׁאֵין הָעוֹלָם מִתְקַיֵּים בְּלֹא תוֹרָה, וְהֶעֱבִיר מֵהֶם תְּשַׁע מֵאוֹת וְשִׁבְעִים וְאַרְבָּעָה דּוֹרוֹת, וְעַתָּה מִסְפָּר מַה הוּא:

### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:18:1)


**לֵאמֹר לְךָ אֶתֵּן אֶרֶץ כְּנָעַן.** שֶׁעִם כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד כָּרַת בְּרִית לָתֵת לָהֶם אֶת אֶרֶץ כְּנָעַן:



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:19:1)


**בִּהְיוֹתְכֶם מְתֵי מִסְפָּר.** אָז נִשְׁבַּע לָתֵת לָהֶם אֶת **אֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן חֶבֶל נַחֲלַתְכֶם**. וְזֶהוּ דָּבָר גָּדוֹל, שֶׁאֵין לוֹמַר מֵרוֹב עַם שֶׁהֱיִיתֶם תְּפַשְׂתֶּם אֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן, אֶלָּא בִּהְיוֹתְכֶם מְתֵי מִסְפָּר. וְאִם תֹּאמַר: אֲפִילוּ לְאֶלֶף וְיוֹתֵר יֵשׁ מִסְפָּר, וּמַהוּ שֶׁכָּתוּב בָּהּ בִּהְיוֹתְכֶם מְתֵי מִסְפָּר! לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר: **כִּמְעַט וְגָרִים בָּהּ**. פִּתְרוֹן: אִם לִמְיֻשָּׁב אֶחָד הָיִיתִי אוֹמֵר, לְאַבְרָהָם לְבַד, לְיִצְחָק בִּלְבַד, לְיַעֲקֹב בִּלְבַד; לְאֶחָד מֵהֶם הָיָה אוֹמֵר **לְךָ אֶתֵּן אֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן**, לֹא הָיָה תֵּימַהּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי מְיֻשָּׁבִים בָּהּ הָיוּ, אֶלָּא הוּא נִשְׁבַּע לָתֵת לָהֶם אֶת הָאָרֶץ בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהָיוּ אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם **גֵּרִים בָּהּ**, דִּכְתִיב ״הִיא חֶבְרוֹן אֲשֶׁר גָּר שָׁם אַבְרָהָם וְיִצְחָק״ (יהושע טו:יג) וְעוֹד: אִם לְגֵר תּוֹשָׁב הָיָה אוֹמֵר ״לְךָ וּלְזַרְעֲךָ אֶתֵּן אֶת הָאָרֶץ הַזֹּאת״, לֹא הָיָה פֶּלֶא כָּל כָּךְ בָּזֶה, שֶׁאֲפִילוּ גֵּרִים וְתוֹשָׁבִים לֹא הָיוּ, אֶלָּא:


**When you were few in number** [It was] then that He swore to give them the land of Canaan, the portion of your heritage. And this is a great thing, so that they cannot say that because of your great numbers, you seized the land of Canaan, but when you were few in number. Now if you say that even a thousand or more have a number, what is this about which it is written: “When you were few in number”? Therefore, it says: “hardly dwelling in it.” The meaning is that if He would say to one settler, e.g., to Abraham alone, to Isaac alone, to Jacob alone, to one of them He would say, “To you I will give the land of Canaan,” it would not be a wonder, because they were settled in it! But He swore to give you the land at a time when your ancestors were strangers sojourning in it, as it is written (Gen. 35:27): “... that is Hebron, where Abraham and Isaac sojourned.” And furthermore, if He would say to a stranger who is settling, “To you and to your seed I will give this land,” it would not be such a wonder. Therefore, he says that they were not even strangers who were settling, but...



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:20:1)


**וַיִּתְהַלְּכוּ מִגּוֹי אֶל גּוֹי.** אַבְרָהָם הָלַךְ לְמִצְרַיִם וְלִגְרָר, וְכֵן יִצְחָק, וְכֵן יַעֲקֹב הָיָה בְּבֵית לָבָן עֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה; וְגַם עַתָּה, כִּי וַיֵּלְכוּ לֹא נֶאֱמַר, אֶלָּא וַיִּתְהַלְּכוּ – פולאיט״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**And when they walked from nation to nation** Abraham went to Egypt and to Gerar, and so did Isaac, and so was Jacob in Laban’s house for twenty years, and now, too, for it does not say: וַיֵּלְכוּ but וַיִּתְהַלְּכוּ (*poraler* in Old French, to wander about)



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:21:1)


**לֹא הִנִּיחַ לְאִישׁ לְעָשְׁקָם.** אַף־עַל־פִּי כֵן שֶׁהָלְכוּ מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם, וּמִנְהָג לְהַזִּיק עוֹבְרֵי דְרָכִים, כְּשֶׁבָּאִין לַמָּלוֹן מוֹצְאִין עֲלֵיהֶן עֲלִילוֹת, אֲבָל אֵלּוּ לֹא הִנִּיחַ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְהַזִּיקָם וּלְעָשְׁקָם; דּוּגְמָא: ״אוֹ עָשַׁק אֶת עֲמִיתוֹ״ (ויקרא ה:כא). וְאִם תֹּאמַר: מַה שֶּׁלֹּא עֲשָׁקוּם חוֹמְסֵי דְרָכִים וּשְׁאָר הָאֲרָצוֹת שֶׁעָבְרוּ אֵלּוּ בְּתוֹכָן, לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הִרְגִּישׁוּ בָּם, אֲבָל אִם הָיוּ מַרְגִּישִׁים בָּם הָיוּ עוֹשְׁקִים אוֹתָם, לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר: **וַיּוֹכַח עֲלֵיהֶם מְלָכִים**. כְּלוֹמַר: וַדַּאי עֲשָׁקוּם, כְּגוֹן פַּרְעֹה לְאַבְרָהָם (בראשית יב:יז) וַאֲבִימֶלֶךְ לְיִצְחָק (בראשית כ:יח) וְלָבָן וּשְׁכֶם לְיַעֲקֹב (בראשית ל-לא; לד) וְאָמַר לָהֶם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא (שוחר טוב קה:ד):


**He let no man oppress them** Although they walked from place to place and it is customary to harm wayfarers, and when they arrive to their inn, they are falsely accused, but these the Holy One, blessed be He, did not let [anyone] hurt them or oppress them. An example is (Lev. 5:21): “or oppressed (עָשַּׁק) his neighbor.” Now if you ask if the reason that the highwaymen and [the people of] the other lands in whose midst they passed did not harm them was that they did not notice them, but if they had noticed them, they would have oppressed them, therefore, it says: “... and He reproved kings on their account,” i.e., they did oppress them, as Pharaoh [did] to Abraham and Abimelech to Isaac, and Laban and Shechem to Jacob, and the Holy One, blessed be He, said to them...



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:22:1)


**אַל תִּגְּעוּ בִמְשִׁיחָי וּבִנְבִיאַי אַל תָּרֵעוּ.** בְּאַבְרָהָם כְּתִיב ״וַיְנַגַּע ה׳ אֶת פַּרְעֹה״ (בראשית יב:יז), וּכְתִיב ״וַיָּבֹא אֱלֹהִים אֶל אֲבִימֶלֶךְ בַּחֲלוֹם הַלַּיְלָה וַיֹּאמֶר לוֹ הִנְּךָ מֵת וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית כ:ג); בְּיַעֲקֹב כְּתִיב ״וַיָּבֹא אֱלֹהִים אֶל לָבָן הָאֲרַמִּי בַּחֲלוֹם הַלַּיְלָה וַיֹּאמֶר לוֹ הִשָּׁמֶר לְךָ וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית לא:כד), וְעוֹד בְּיַעֲקֹב שֶׁבָּאוּ אֵלָיו לְהָרְגוֹ עַל שֶׁהָרְגוּ שְׁכֶם וַחֲמוֹר, וְכָתִיב ״וַיִּסָּעוּ וַיְהִי חִתַּת אֱלֹהִים״ (בראשית לה:ה). וּבְסֵפֶר תְּהִלִּים מְפֹרָשׁ יוֹתֵר, כִּי הַמִּזְמוֹר אֵינוֹ מְסֻיָּם כָּאן כִּדְכְתִיב הָתָם ״וַיִּקְרָא רָעָב עַל הָאָרֶץ וְגוֹ׳״ (תהלים קה:טז), כְּדֵי לְהַגְלוֹתָם לְמִצְרָיִם:


**Do not touch My anointed ones, and do not harm My prophets** Concerning Abraham it says (Gen. 12:17): “And the Lord plagued Pharaoh,” and it is written (ibid. 20:3): “And the Lord came to Abimelech in a dream at night and said to him: You shall die, etc.” Concerning Jacob it says: (ibid. 31: 24): “And God came to Laban the Aramean in a dream at night, and said to him: Beware, etc.” And further, concerning Jacob, when they came to kill him because they killed Shechem and Hamor, it is writ ten: (ibid. 35:5): “Then they journeyed on, and the terror of God was, etc.” The Book of Psalms is more explicit, for the psalm does not terminate here, as it is written there (105:16): “He called a famine upon the land,” in order to exile them to Egypt.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 16:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/16:24:1)
