**And the Lord commanded the angel** to return his sword, and the sword was returned to its sheath. Similar to this is (Jonah 2:11): “And the Lord said to the fish, and it spewed Jonah, etc.”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 21:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/21:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/21:28:1)


**בָּעֵת הַהִיא בִּרְאוֹת דָּוִיד כִּי עָנָהוּ ה׳.** פָּרָשָׁה זוֹ לֹא נִכְתְּבָה בִּשְׁמוּאֵל (שמואל ב כד), וּמִשּׁוּם כְּבוֹד דָּוִד נִכְתְּבָה כָּאן, שֶׁעָשָׂה מִזְבֵּחַ; וְכָל פְּסוּקֵי דְּפָרָשָׁה זוֹ דָּבוּק פָּסוּק אֶל פָּסוּק כְּאִלּוּ כֻּלּוֹ פָּסוּק אֶחָד:


**At that time, when David saw that the Lord had answered him** This section was not written in II Samuel, but because of David’s honor, it was written here that he made an altar, and all the verses of this section are connected to one another, as if they were all one verse.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 21:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/21:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/21:29:1)


**וּמִזְבַּח הָעוֹלָה.** שֶׁל מֹשֶׁה עֲדַיִן **בָּעֵת הַהִיא בַּבָּמָה** אֲשֶׁר **בְּגִבְעוֹן**:


**and the altar of the burnt offerings** of Moses was still at that time in the high place, which was in Gibeon.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 21:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/21:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/21:30:1)


**וְלֹא יָכֹל דָּוִיד.** אָז כְּשֶׁהָיָה אוֹתוֹ מַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁצִּוָּה לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְהַעֲלוֹת קָרְבָּן, לֹא הָיָה דָוִד יָכוֹל **לָלֶכֶת לְפָנָיו לִדְרֹשׁ אֱלֹהִים**; כְּלוֹמַר: לִפְנֵי הַמִּזְבֵּחַ אֲשֶׁר **בְּגִבְעוֹן**:


**But David could not** Then, when that incident occurred that the Holy One, blessed be He, commanded him to offer up a sacrifice, David could not go before it to inquire of God, i.e., before the altar that was in Gibeon.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 21:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/21:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/21:30:2)


**כִּי נִבְעַת מִפְּנֵי חֶרֶב מַלְאַךְ ה׳.** וְהָלַךְ דָּוִד כְּדִבְרֵי ה׳, וּבָנָה מִזְבֵּחַ וְעָנָהוּ ה׳:


**because he was frightened by the sword of the angel of the Lord** and David went according to the words of the Lord and built an altar, and the Lord answered him.



## Chapter 22


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 22:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:1:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר דָּוִיד זֶה הוּא בֵּית ה׳ הָאֱלֹהִים וְזֶה מִזְבֵּחַ לְעֹלָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל.** מִכָּאן וָאֵילָךְ, שֶׁכָּל שֶׁמְּבַקֵּשׁ ה׳ לְהַקְרִיב לוֹ קָרְבָּן, יֵצֵא וְיַקְרִיב עַל אוֹתוֹ מִזְבֵּחַ:


**And David said, etc.** from now on, anyone seeking the Lord to offer a sacrifice to Him shall go forth and sacrifice on that altar.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 22:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:2:1)


**לִכְנוֹס אֶת הַגֵּרִים.** גֵּרִים שֶׁנִּתְגַּיְּרוּ, שֶׁלֹּא רָצָה לְהַעֲבִיד בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל עֲבוֹדַת פֶּרֶךְ; וְכֵן מָצִינוּ בִּשְׁלֹמֹה לְמַטָּה ״וַיִּסְפֹּר שְׁלֹמֹה וְגוֹ׳ וַיַּעַשׂ מֵהֶם שִׁבְעִים אֶלֶף״ נוֹשֵׂא ״סַבָּל וְגוֹ׳״ (דה״ב ב:טז-יז), וְעוֹד כְּתִיב ״וְכָל הָעָם הַנּוֹתָר מִן הַחִתִּי וְגוֹ׳״ (דה״ב ח:ז-ח) – שְׁנֵי פְסוּקִים, וְעוֹד כְּתִיב ״וּמִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל... לֹא נָתַן שְׁלֹמֹה לַעֲבָדִים וְגוֹ׳״ (דה״ב ח:ט):


**to gather the strangers** who had converted, because he did not wish to make Israelites perform rigorous labor. And so we find with Solomon below (II Chron. 2:1): “And Solomon counted, etc.”; (ibid. verse 17): “And he made of them seventy thousand who bear burdens.” And it is written further (ibid. 8:7): “And all the people remaining from the Hittites, etc., who were not of Israel, etc.”; (ibid. verse 9): “And of the Children of Israel, whom Solomon did not make slaves, etc.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 22:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:3:1)


**לַמִּסְמְרִים לְדַלְתוֹת הַשְּׁעָרִים.** נֶעגְ״ל בִּלְשׁוֹן אַשְׁכְּנַז:


**for the nails, for the doors of the gates** in German, *negel*, nails.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 22:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:3:2)


**וְלַמְחַבְּרוֹת.** הֵכִין דָּוִד הַבַּרְזֶל שֶׁמְּחַבְּרִין וּמַחֲזִיקִים הַ**דְּלָתוֹת** וְ**הַשְּׁעָרִים** בָּהֶם:


**And David prepared... for the couplings** the iron [implements] with which they join and reinforce the doors and the gates.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 22:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:4:1)


**וְהַצּוֹרִים.** עִם חִירָם מֶלֶךְ צוֹר:


**and the Tyrians** with Huram, the king of Tyre.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 22:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:5:1)


**נַעַר וָרָךְ.** לְפִי שֶׁאֲפִילוּ בֶּן אַרְבָָָּעִים וּשְׁתַּיִם שָׁנָה קָרוּי נַעַר, דִּכְתִיב: ״וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ בֶּן נוּן נַעַר לֹא יָמִישׁ וְגוֹ׳״ (שמות לג), שֶׁהֲרֵי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ לֹא מָלַךְ אַחֲרֵי מֹשֶׁה כִּי אִם עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁמוֹנֶה שָׁנָה וְחַי מֵאָה וָעֵשֶׂר שָׁנָה, נִמְצָא בְּשָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה כְּשֶׁהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בַּמִּדְבָּר הֵעִיד עָלָיו הַכָּתוּב ״וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ בֶּן נוּן נַעַר״, מֵאָז הָיָה בֶּן אַרְבָָָּעִים וּשְׁתַּיִם שָׁנָה, עַל כֵּן הֻצְרַךְ לִכְתֹּב רָךְ, שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה כִּי אִם בֶּן שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה כְּשֶׁמָּלַךְ:


**is young and tender** נַעַר, because even one [who is] forty-two years old is called נַעַר, for it is written: (Exod. 33:11): “... and his young (נַעַר) servant, Joshua the son of Nun, did not depart, etc.,” for Joshua reigned after Moses only twenty-eight years, and he lived one hundred and ten years. We find (in the first year that the Israelites were in the desert) that Scripture testifies about him: “and Joshua the son of Nun, his young [servant],” and at that time he was forty-two years old. Therefore, it was necessary to write, “tender,” because he [Solomon] was only twelve years old when he reigned.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 22:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/22:5:2)


**לְהַגְדִּיל לְמַעְלָה.** גָּדֵל מְאֹד, שֶׁהִגְבִּיהוֹ שְׁלֹמֹה עַד מֵאָה וְעֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וְהָאוּלָם עַל פְּנֵי הֵיכַל הַבַּיִת וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים א ו:ג):


**magnified on high** It was very tall, for Solomon raised it up to one hundred and twenty cubits, as it is written: (I Kings 6:3): “And the porch before the Temple of the House, etc.”



## Chapter 23


### Verse 27

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 23:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/23:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/23:27:1)


**הֵמָּה מִסְפַּר בְּנֵי לֵוִי מִבֶּן עֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה וּלְמָעְלָה.** כְּלוֹמַר: אָז תִּקֵּן דָּוִד מִשְׁמָרוֹת אֵלּוּ, כְּשֶׁיָּבֹא שְׁלֹמֹה בְּנוֹ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מְזוּמָּנִים לוֹ:


**the sons of the Levites were numbered from twenty years old and upwards** Then David established these watches, so that when Solomon his son would come, they would be prepared for him.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 23:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/23:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/23:28:1)


**כִּי מַעֲמָדָם לְיַד בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן וגו׳.** דֻּגְמָא: ״וְיִלָּווּ עָלֶיךָ וִישָׁרְתוּךָ״ (במדבר יח:ב):


**For their station was alongside the sons of Aaron, etc.** This resembles (Num. 18:2): “... and they shall join with you and minister to you.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 23:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/23:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/23:28:2)


**וְעַל טָהֳרַת לְכָל קֹדֶשׁ.** שֶׁאִם יִטָּמֵא הַבַּיִת וְכֵלָיו, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הֵם מְמֻנִּים לְטַהֲרוֹ, שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ בְּחִזְקִיָּהוּ כְּדִכְתִיב ״וַיְקַבְּלוּ הַלְוִיִּם לְהוֹצִיא לְנַחַל קִדְרוֹן חוּצָה״ (דברי הימים ב כט:טז), וּכְתִיב ״וַיָּחֵלּוּ בְּאֶחָד לַחֹדֶשׁ הָרִאשׁוֹן לְקַדֵּשׁ וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב כט:יז):


**and over the purity of all sacred things** so that if the House and its vessels become unclean, they will be appointed to purify it, for so we find with Hezekiah, as it is written (below II Chron. 29:16): “And the Levites accepted to take out to the Kidron Valley outside,” and it is written (ibid. verse 17): “And they commenced on the first of the first month to sanctify, etc.”



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 23:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/23:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/23:31:1)


**וּלְכָל הַעֲלוֹת עֹלוֹת לַה׳ לַשַּׁבָּתוֹת לֶחֳדָשִׁים.** פִּתְרוֹן: הֵם יִהְיוּ מְמֻנִּים עַל הַלְּשָׁכוֹת וְגַם עַל לִשְׁכַּת בֵּית הַטְּלָאִים שֶׁהַקָּרְבָּנוֹת שָׁם (משנה תמיד ג, ג), וְהֵם יָבִיאוּ אֶל הַכֹּהֲנִים תָּמִיד בְּכָל יוֹם בְּמִסְפָּר מַה שֶּׁצְּרִיכִים בְּכָל יוֹם, בֵּין בְּחוֹל בֵּין בַּשַּׁבָּתוֹת וּבֶחֳדָשִׁים וּבַמּוֹעֲדִים שֶׁמּוֹסִיפִים עַל הַתָּמִיד; כָּל אֵלֶּה יָבִיאוּ **בְּמִסְפָּר כְּמִשְׁפָּט**. וְגַם מְסַיְּעִים לַכֹּהֲנִים לְהַפְשִׁיט הַזֶּבַח, וְכֵן מָצִינוּ בְּחִזְקִיָּהוּ ״רַק הַכֹּהֲנִים הָיוּ לִמְעָט וְלֹא יָכְלוּ לְהַפְשִׁיט אֶת כָּל הָעוֹלוֹת וַיְחַזְּקוּם אֲחֵיהֶם הַלְוִיִּם וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב כט:לד):


**And for every offering up of burnt offerings to the Lord on Sabbaths, on New Moons** The meaning is: They will be appointed over the chambers and also over the chamber of the house of the lambs, where the sacrificial animals are kept, and they will bring [them] to the priests constantly every day, according to the number that is required for each day, whether on weekdays, whether on Sabbaths, and on New Moons and festivals, when additional sacrifices are offered up with the daily offerings - all these they will bring in number according to the ordinance, and they will also assist the priests in flaying the sacrifice. And so we find with Hezekiah (II Chron. 29:34): “Only the priests were few, and they could not flay all the burnt offerings; so their brethren the Levites strengthened them, etc.”



## Chapter 24


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 24:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:1:1)


**וְלִבְנֵי אַהֲרֹן.** פִּתְרוֹן: אֵלֶּה בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן וְגוֹמֵר, עַל **מַחְלְקוֹתָם**:


**And of the sons of Aaron** The meaning is: These are the sons of Aaron, etc. according to their divisions.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 24:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:2:1)


**וַיָּמָת נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא וְגוֹ׳ וּבָנִים לֹא הָיוּ לָהֶם.** לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ לָהֶם בָּנִים כִּהֲנוּ אֶלְעָזָר וְאִיתָמָר בִּמְקוֹמָם, אֲבָל אִם הָיוּ לְנָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּ בָּנִים, הָיוּ קוֹדְמִים לְאֶלְעָזָר וְאִיתָמָר בִּכְהוּנָּה גְּדוֹלָה; שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי יְהוֹצָדָק אָבִיו שֶׁל יְהוֹשֻׁעַ הָיָה (עזרא ג:ח), וִיהוֹצָדָק וְעֶזְרָא בְּנֵי שְׂרָיָה הָיוּ, דִּכְתִיב ״וּשְׂרָיָה הוֹלִיד אֶת יְהוֹצָדָק״ (דברי הימים א ה:מ) וּבְעֶזְרָא כְּתִיב ״עֶזְרָא בֶּן שְׂרָיָה״ (עזרא ז:א), וִיהוֹצָדָק הָלַךְ בַּגּוֹלָה לְבָבֶל, וּכְשֶׁעָלוּ מִבָּבֶל עָלָה יְהוֹשֻׁעַ וְשִׁמֵּשׁ וְלֹא שִׁמֵּשׁ עֶזְרָא, לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה יְהוֹצָדָק הַבְּכוֹר וְקִדְּמוֹ בֵּין אֶחָיו הַבְּכוֹר, כְּדִכְתִיב בִּנְבוּאַת חַגַּי ״יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בֶּן יְהוֹצָדָק הַכֹּהֵן הַגָּדוֹל״ (חגי א:א):


**And Nadab and Abihu died, etc., and they had no sons** Since they had no sons, Eleazar and Ithamar served as priests in their stead, but if Nadab and Abihu had had children, they would have preceded Eleazar and Ithamar in the high priesthood, for so we find with Ezra (3:9), for Jehozadak was Joshua’s father, and Jehozadak and Ezra were the sons of Seraiah, as it is written: (above 5:41): “... and Seraiah begot Jehozadak.” And in Ezra (7:1), it is written: “Ezra the son of Seraiah.” And Jehozadak went into exile to Babylon, and when they came up from Babylon, Joshua went up and served, but Ezra did not serve because Jehozadak was the firstborn, and the son of his firstborn brother preceded him, as is written in the prophecy of Haggai (1:1): “Joshua the son of Jehozadak, the High Priest.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 24:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:3:1)


**וַאֲחִימֶלֶךְ מִן בְּנֵי אִיתָמָר.** זֶה אֲחִימֶלֶךְ בֶּן אֶבְיָתָר, כְּדְמוּכַח בְּסָמוּךְ **וַיִּכְתְּבֵם שְׁמַעְיָה וְגוֹ׳**, עַד **וַאֲחִימֶלֶךְ בֶּן אֶבְיָתָר**:


**and Ahimelech was of the sons of Ithamar** This is Ahimelech the son of Ebiathar, as is proven immediately following (verse 6): “And Shemaiah, etc. wrote them...” until “and Ahimelech the son of Ebiathar.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 24:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:4:1)


**וַיְּחַלְּקוּם לִבְנֵי אֶלְעָזָר רָאשִׁים לְבֵית אָבוֹת שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר.** מִתְּחִלָּה בְּמִשְׁכַּן שִׁילֹה לֹא הָיוּ כִּי אִם שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה מִשְׁמָרוֹת, שְׁמֹנֶה מֵאֶלְעָזָר וּשְׁמֹנֶה מֵאִיתָמָר, כַּמְפֹרָשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת תַּעֲנִית (כז.); וּכְשֶׁרָאָה דָוִד שֶׁהָיוּ **רָאשֵׁי** הַגְּבוּרִים לְאֶלְעָזָר רַבִּים, חִלְּקָם לְכָל מִשְׁמָר וּמִשְׁמָר שֶׁל אֶלְעָזָר לִשְׁנַיִם, וְהֶעֱמִידָם עַל שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה מִשְׁמָרוֹת, וּמִשְׁמֶרֶת בְּנֵי אִיתָמָר הֶעֱמִיד עַל מַעֲמַד מִשְׁמָר כְּבָרִאשׁוֹנָה עַל שְׁמֹנֶה מִשְׁמָרוֹת. וְכֵן מוֹכִיחַ בְּסָמוּךְ: **בֵּית אָב אֶחָד אָחֻז לְאֶלְעָזָר וְאֶחָד אָחֻז לְאִיתָמָר** – כְּלוֹמַר: כְּאָחוּז לְמַעֲמָד לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת שֶׁבַּמִּשְׁכָּן כְּמוֹ כֵן הֶעֱמִידָן דָּוִד עַתָּה בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**and thus they were divided: of the sons of Eleazar there were sixteen heads of the fathers’ houses** Originally, in the Tabernacle of Shiloh, there were no more than sixteen watches: eight from Eleazar and eight from Ithamar, as is delineated in Tractate *Taanith* (27), but when David saw that the men of Eleazar were many, he divided each watch of Eleazar into two and made them sixteen watches, but the watch of the sons of Ithamar he left as the watches had originally been, viz. of eight watches. This is proven immediately following (verse 6): “... one father’s house established for Eleazar, and what was established for Ithamar remained established,” i.e., as it was established [that they] be stationed as a watch in the Tabernacle, so did David station them now in the Temple.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 24:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:5:1)


**וַיַּחְלְקוּם בְּגוֹרָלוֹת אֵלֶּה עִם אֵלֶּה.** לְמִי יִפּוֹל הַגּוֹרָל לִהְיוֹת רִאשׁוֹן אוֹ שֵׁנִי אוֹ שְׁלִישִׁי אוֹ רְבִיעִי, וְכֵן כֻּלָּם:


**And they divided them by lots, these with those** to whom the lot would fall to be first or second or third or fourth, and so all of them.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 24:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:6:1)


**וַיִּכְתְּבֵם שְׁמַעְיָה.** לִהְיוֹת לָהֶם לְזִכָּרוֹן מִי רִאשׁוֹן וּמִי שֵׁנִי, וְכֵן כֻּלָּם:


**And Shemaiah... wrote them** to be a reminder for them who was first, who second, and so all of them.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 24:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/24:19:1)


**אֵלֶּה פְקֻדָּתָם לַעֲבֹדָתָם.** אֵלּוּ שֶׁכְּתוּבִין כָּאן, רָאשֵׁי מִשְׁמָרוֹת פְּקֻדָּתָם עַל מִשְׁמָרוֹת הָיוּ:


**These are their appointment[s] to their service** Those who are written here are the heads of the watches. Their appointments were over the watches.



## Chapter 25


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:1:1)


**וַיַּבְדֵּל דָּוִיד וְשָׂרֵי הַצָּבָא לַעֲבוֹדָה.** לְשׁוֹרֵר הַשִּׁיר:


**Then David separated for the service** to sing the songs.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:1:2)


**לִבְנֵי אָסָף וְגוֹ׳.** מִכָּל הַלְוִיִּם לֹא הִבְדִּיל כִּי אִם בְּנֵי אָסָף:


**the sons of Asaph, etc.** Out of all the Levites he separated only the sons of Asaph, etc.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:1:3)


**וְהֵימָן וִידוּתוּן הַנְּבָאִים.** כְּשֶׁהָיוּ מְנַגְּנִים בִּכְלֵי שִׁירִים הַלָּלוּ הָיוּ מִתְנַבְּאִים, דֻּגְמָא בֶּאֱלִישָׁע ״קְחוּ לִי מְנַגֵּן״ (מלכים ב ג:טו) וְהָיָה כְּנַגֵּן הַמְנַגֵּן וַתְּהִי עָלָיו יַד ה׳:


**Heman, and Jeduthun, who prophesied** When they would play these musical instruments, they would prophesy. A similar incident [is told] of Elisha: (II Kings 3:15): “’And now fetch me a musician.’ And when the musician played, the hand of the Lord came upon him.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:1:4)


**וַיְהִי מִסְפָּרָם אַנְשֵׁי מְלָאכָה.** כְּלוֹמַר הַמִּשְׁמָרוֹת עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע עַל עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע מִשְׁמָרוֹת, אַרְבָּעָה בָּנִים שֶׁל אָסָף וְשִׁשָּׁה שֶׁל יְדוּתוּן וְאַרְבָּעָה עָָָשָׂר שֶׁל הֵימָן, הֲרֵי עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע. לְמַטָּה כָּלַל מִסְפַּר כֻּלָּן, כְּדִכְתִיב **וַיְהִי מִסְפָּרָם עִם אֲחֵיהֶם וְגוֹ׳**:


**and the number of those men expert in their work... was:** i.e., There were twenty- four watches, corresponding to the twenty-four watches [of the priests (Moharich)]: viz. the four sons of Asaph, the six of Jeduthun, and the fourteen of Heman, totaling twenty-four. Below, he sums them all up, as it is written (verse 7): “And their number with their brethren, etc.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:3:1)


**וּמַתִּתְיָהוּ שִׁשָּׁה.** וְלֹא תִמְצָא אֶלָּא חָמֵשׁ! אִשְׁתּוֹ הָיְתָה מְעֻבֶּרֶת אֶת שִׁמְעִי, וְרָאָה בְּרוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ שֶׁגַּם הוּא עָתִיד לִהְיוֹת רֹאשׁ מִשְׁמָרָה, לְפִיכָךְ אָמַר: שִׁשָּׁה; וְזֶהוּ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לְפָנֵינוּ **הָעֲשִׂירִי שִׁמְעִי**. זֶהוּ שֶׁיִּסֵּד הַפַּיְּטָן (בקדשת של יוצר דפרשת שקלים) ״רָשׁוּם מִבֶּטֶן לְהִפָּקֵד בְּמִשְׁמֶרֶת הַקֹּדֶשׁ״:


**and Mattithiah - six** But you will find only five. [The answer is that] his wife was pregnant with Shimei, and he saw through the holy spirit that he too was destined to be the chief of a watch; therefore he says “six.” And that is what is stated further: (verse 17): “The tenth: Shimei.” This is what the liturgical poet set down (in the Kedushah of Yozer of Parashath Shekalim): “Inscribed from the womb to be counted in the watch of the Sanctuary.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:3:2)


**עַל יְדֵי אֲבִיהֶם יְדוּתוּן.** דֻּגְמָא: ״עַל יָדוֹ הֶחֱזִיק״ (נחמיה ג:ח):


**beside their father Jeduthun** עַל יְדֵי, similar to (Neh. 3:8): “And beside him (עַל יָדוֹ)... repaired.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:3:3)


**הַנִּבָּא עַל הוֹדוֹת וְהַלֵּל.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְנַגְּנִים בְּכִנּוֹר מִזְמוֹרֵי ״הוֹדוּ״ וּמִזְמוֹרֵי ״הַלְלוּיָהּ״, וְהָיָה מִתְנַבֵּא:


**who prophesied with [songs of] thanksgiving and praise** For on the harp they would play songs of “Give thanks (הוֹדוּ)” and songs of “Hallelujah (הַלְלוּיָהּ),” and he would prophesy.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:4:1)


**וְרוֹמַמְתִּי עֶזֶר.** שֵׁם אֶחָד הוּא:


**and Romamti Ezer** This is one name.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:5:1)


**חֹזֵה הַמֶּלֶךְ בְּדִבְרֵי הָאֱלֹהִים לְהָרִים קָרֶן.** קֶרֶן הַנְּבוּאָה שֶׁמִּתְנַבֵּא לַמֶּלֶךְ; דּוּגְמָא: ״וְיָרֵם קֶרֶן מְשִׁיחוֹ״ (שמואל א ב:י):


**the king’s seer, in words of God to raise the horn** the horn of the prophecy, for he would prophesy for the king. A similar instance of this is (I Sam. 2:10): “... and raise the horn of His anointed one.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:5:2)


**וַיִּתֵּן הָאֱלֹהִים לְהֵימָן.** כְּלוֹמַר: אִם לֹא שֶׁנָּתַן לוֹ אֱלֹהִים הַבָּנִים, אִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁהָיוּ לוֹ אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר בָּנִים וְשָׁלוֹשׁ בָּנוֹת וְכֻלָּן רְאוּיִין לִהְיוֹת רָאשֵׁי מִשְׁמָרוֹת. דּוּגְמָא: ״וַיִּתֵּן ה׳ לָהּ הֵרָיוֹן וַתֵּלֶד בֵּן״ (רות ד:יג) – אִם לֹא שֶׁהָיְתָה מַתָּנָה זוֹ מֵה׳, אִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיּוֹלִיד בֹּעַז שֶׁהָיָה זָקֵן; וְכֵן ״וַיַּרְאֵהוּ ה׳ אֶת כָּל הָאָרֶץ״ (דברים לד:א) – אִם לֹא מֵאֵת ה׳ הָיְתָה רְאוּיָה זֹאת, אִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהַבִּיט כָּל כָּךְ לְמֵרָחוֹק:


**and God gave Heman** Had not God given him children, it would have been impossible for him to have fourteen sons and three daughters, with all of them fit to be chiefs of watches. A similar case is (Ruth 4:13): “... and the Lord gave her conception, and she bore a son.” Had not this been a gift from the Lord, it would have been impossible for Boaz to father a son, because he was old. Similarly, (Deut. 34:1): “and the Lord showed him all the land.” Had this vision not been from the Lord, it would have been impossible to see so far.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:7:1)


**כָּל הַמֵּבִין.** לְעִנְיַן שִׁירָה נוֹפֵל לוֹמַר ״מֵבִין״, כְּדִכְתִיב ״מֵבִין עִם תַּלְמִיד״ (דברי הימים א כה:ח); ״וּכְנַנְיָהוּ שַׂר הַלְוִיִּם בְּמַשָּׂא יָסֹר בְּמַשָּׂא כִּי מֵבִין הוּא״ (דברי הימים א טו:כב):


**every expert** - Heb. הַמֵּבִין. In regard to song, the expression is appropriate, for it is written: (below verse 8): “expert (מֵבִין) with pupil.” [Also] (15:22): “And Chenaniah, the leader of the Levites in song; he would chastise in song because he was an expert (מֵבִין).”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:8:1)


**וַיַּפִּילוּ גוֹרָלוֹת מִשְׁמֶרֶת לְעֻמַּת.** פִּתְרוֹן: מִשְׁמֶרֶת לְעֻמַּת מִשְׁמֶרֶת; וְדוּגְמָא לְמַעְלָה: ״וָאֶהְיֶה מִתְהַלֵּךְ מֵאֹהֶל אֶל אֹהֶל וּמִמִּשְׁכָּן״ (דברי הימים א יז:ה) – פִּתְרוֹן: מִמִּשְׁכָּן אֶל מִשְׁכָּן:


**And they cast lots, a watch against** The meaning is a watch against a watch, and a similar [case] is [found] above (16:5): “... but I have walked from tent to tent and from tabernacle,” which means: “and from tabernacle to tabernacle.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:8:2)


**כַּקָּטֹן כַּגָּדוֹל מֵבִין עִם תַּלְמִיד.** פִּתְרוֹן: הִפִּילוּ **גוֹרָלוֹת** עַל הַמִּשְׁמָרוֹת, זֶה לְעֻמַּת זֶה, מִשְׁמֶרֶת לְעֻמַּת מִשְׁמֶרֶת, כַּקָּטֹן כַּגָּדוֹל, מֵבִין – הָרַב, עִם תַּלְמִיד, כֻּלָּם הָיוּ שָׁוִין בָּזֶה לְמִי שֶׁיַּגִּיעַ הַגּוֹרָל הָרִאשׁוֹן: אִם לְקָטָן – כֵּן יַקְדִּים הוּא לִהְיוֹת רִאשׁוֹן, וְאִם אַחֲרוֹן – יִהְיֶה אַחֲרוֹן; וְכֵן כֻּלָּם: בֵּין מֵבִין בֵּין תַּלְמִיד, בֵּין קָטָן בֵּין גָּדוֹל. וּמִשְׁמָרוֹת אֵלּוּ שֶׁל לְוִיִּם הוֹלְכִים אַחַר מִשְׁמָרוֹת שֶׁל כֹּהֲנִים (דברי הימים א כד:ז-יח), לְמִי שֶׁנּוֹפֵל הַגּוֹרָל הָאֶחָד – מִשְׁמֶרֶת רִאשׁוֹן עִם מִשְׁמֶרֶת יְהוֹיָרִיב הַכֹּהֵן, וּלְמִי שֶׁיִּפֹּל הַגּוֹרָל הַשֵּׁנִי, יְשַׁמֵּשׁ הוּא וּמִשְׁמַרְתּוֹ עִם מִשְׁמֶרֶת יְדַעְיָה הַכֹּהֵן:


**like the small one so the great one, scholar with pupil** The meaning is that they cast lots for the watches, this one against that one, a watch against a watch, the small one was like the great one, the expert master with the pupil, all of them were equal in this, for the one on whom the first lot fell, if to the small one, he would come first to be the first one, and if last, he would be the last one, and so all of them, whether a scholar or a pupil, whether a small person or a great one. And these watches of the Levites followed the watches of the priests: the one to whom the first lot fell had the first lot, and his watch was with the watch of Jehoiarib the priest, and the one to whom the second lot fell waited, and his watch was with the watch of Jedaiah the priest.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:9:1)


**וַיֵּצֵא הַגּוֹרָל הָרִאשׁוֹן לְאָסָף לְיוֹסֵף.** בְּנוֹ רִאשׁוֹן:


**And the first lot of Asaph came out to Joseph** his first son.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:9:2)


**גְּדַלְיָהוּ הַשֵּׁנִי.** גְּדַלְיָהוּ בְּכוֹר יְדוּתוּן הוּא (לעיל, ג):


**Gedaliah the second** Gedaliah was the firstborn of Jeduthun.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:9:3)


**וְאֶחָיו וּבָנָיו שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר וְכוּ׳.** פִּתְרוֹן: עַל כָּל מִשְׁמָרָה וּמִשְׁמָרָה יָבֹאוּ שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר לְוִיִּם, שֶׁהֲרֵי הָיוּ כֻּלָּם מָאתַיִם שְׁמוֹנִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה, וּכְשֶׁתְּחַלְּקֵם לְעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה חֲלָקִים יִהְיֶה כָּל חֵלֶק וְחֵלֶק שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר לְוִיִּם. נִרְאֶה לִי: וְזֶהוּ אֶחָיו וּבָנָיו מוּסָב **לְיוֹסֵף** וְלִ**גְדַלְיָהוּ**, וּלְפִי שֶׁבְּפָסוּק אֶחָד שְׁנֵיהֶם נִזְכָּרִים, כָּתַב לְבַד פַּעַם אַחַת **הוּא** וּבָנָיו וְאֶחָיו, וְכָל אֶחָיו וַאֲחֵיהֶם שֶׁבָּעִנְיָן לָאו אֶחָיו מַמָּשׁ, אֶלָּא חֲבוּרָה אַחַת קוֹרֵא אַחִים, (ברודי״ר שפ״ט) בְּלָשׁוֹן אַשְׁכְּנַז:


**he and his brethren and his sons, twelve** The meaning is that for every watch twelve Levites would come, for all of them equalled two hundred and eighty-eight, and if you divide them into twenty-four parts, each part will consist of twelve Levites. So it appears to me. And this is the meaning of “and his brethren and his sons”: referring to Joseph and to Gedaliah. And since they are both mentioned in one verse, he wrote only once, “he and his sons and his brethren.” And not every “his brethren,” and “their brethren” in this context mean his actual brothers; but he calls one group brethren (in German, *Bruderschaft*).



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:10:1)


**הַשְּׁלִישִׁי זַכּוּר.** בְּכוֹר אָסָף (לעיל, ב):


**The third: Zaccur** the firstborn of Asaph.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:11:1)


**הָרְבִיעִי לַיִּצְרִי.** שֵׁנִי שֶׁל יְדוּתוּן (לעיל, ג:)


**The fourth to Izri** the second son of Jeduthun.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 25:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/25:12:1)


**הַחֲמִישִׁי נְתַנְיָהוּ.** שְׁלִישִׁי שֶׁל אָסָף; כָּךְ נָפַל הַגּוֹרָל מִזֶּה לָזֶה:


**The fifth: Nethaniah** the third son of Asaph. So the lot fell from this one to that one.



## Chapter 26


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:1:1)


**לְמַחְלְקוֹת לְשֹׁעֲרִים.** אֵין חוֹזְרִים חֲלִילָה, אֶלָּא כְּנֶגֶד שְׁעָרִים חִלְּקִים, וְשׁוּב הִפִּילוּ גּוֹרָלוֹת מִי לְשַׁעַר זֶה מִי לְשַׁעַר אַחֵר; כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ לְקַמָּן:


**Of the divisions of the gate sentries: of the Korahites** They do not rotate, but they distributed them according to the gates, and they later cast lots [to determine] who would serve at this gate and who at another gate. In this way it is explained further (verses 13-19).


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:1:2)


**לַקָּרְחִים מְשֶׁלֶמְיָהוּ בֶן קֹרֵא מִן בְּנֵי אָסָף.** וְדֻגְמָא לְמַעְלָה: ״וְשַׁלּוּם בֶּן קוֹרֵא וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים א ט:יט):


**of the Korahites: Meshelemiah the son of Kore of the sons of Asaph** And [we find] a similarity above (9:19): “And Shallum the son of Kore, etc.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:5:1)


**כִּי בֵרֲכוֹ אֱלֹהִים.** כְּדַאֲמַר ״וַיְבָרֶךְ ה׳ אֶת בֵּית עוֹבֵד אֱדוֹם וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים א יג:יד):


**for God had blessed him** as is stated above (13:14): “... and the Lord blessed the house of Obed- Edom, etc.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:6:1)


**הַמִּמְשָׁלִים לְבֵית אֲבִיהֶם.** פִּתְרוֹן: אוֹתָם מוֹשְׁלִים הָיוּ עַל יַד אֲבִיהֶם; וְדוּגְמָא בְּסוֹף דָּנִיֵּאל: ״וּמָשַׁל מִמְשָׁל רָב״ (דניאל יא:ג):


**who ruled through their father’s house** The meaning is that those ruling were doing so through their father, and a similarity is [found] at the end of Daniel (11:3): “and rule a great dominion (וּמָשַל מִמְשַל רַב).”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:8:1)


**אִישׁ חַיִל.** לֹא בְּכֹחַ וְלֹא בְּמִלְחָמָה, אֶלָּא כְּאָדָם שֶׁאוֹמֵר עָלָיו פְּלוֹנִי חָשׁוּב; וְכֵן ״הֲלֹא בֹעַז מוֹדַעְתָּנוּ״ (רות ג:ב); ״אִישׁ גִּבּוֹר חַיִל״ (רות ב:א):


**valiant men** - Heb. אִיש חַיִל, not with strength and not in war but like a man about whom someone says, “So-and-so is of high esteem,” and similarly, (Ruth 2:1): “... a mighty man of valor, etc., and his name was Boaz.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:12:1)


**מִשְׁמָרוֹת לְעֻמַּת אֲחֵיהֶם.** רָאשֵׁי עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע מִשְׁמָרוֹת:


**watches corresponding to their brethren** the chiefs of twenty-four watches.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:13:1)


**וַיַּפִּילוּ גוֹרָלוֹת כַּקָּטֹן כַּגָּדוֹל לְבֵית אֲבוֹתָם לְשַׁעַר וָשָׁעַר.** לְמִי יָבוֹא שַׁעַר זֶה וּלְמִי שַׁעַר זֶה, לַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב לַצָּפוֹן לַדָּרוֹם, וְכֵן לְכָל שְׁעָרִים שֶׁבָּעֲזָרָה:


**And they cast lots, the small like the great, to their fathers’ houses for each gate** who will receive this gate, and who will receive this gate, to the east, to the west, to the north, to the south, and so to all the gates in the forecourt.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:17:1)


**וְלָאֲסֻפִּים.** מָקוֹם שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ אֲסוּפִּים:


**and to the Asuppim** a place named Asuppim.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:18:1)


**שְׁנַיִם לַפַּרְבָּר.** וּמְפֹרָשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת תָּמִיד (כז.): אָמַר רָבָא: לַפַּרְבָּר כְּלַפֵּי בָּר:


**Toward the outside** Heb. לַפַּרְבָּר. This is explained in Tractate *Tamid* (ad loc.): “Said Rava: לַפַּרְבָּר means כְּלַפֵּי בָּר, toward the outside.”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:20:1)


**וְהַלְוִיִּם אֲחִיָּה עַל אוֹצְרוֹת וְגוֹ׳.** הוּא הָיָה מְמֻנֶּה עַל מָעוֹת שֶׁנִּקְנִין מֵהֶם קָרְבָּנוֹת לְבֵית אֱלֹהִים, כְּגוֹן קָרְבַּן קֳדָשִׁים:


**And the Levites: Ahiah over the treasuries, etc.** He was appointed over the money with which sacrifices were purchased for the House of God, such as the holy sacrifices (sic).



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:21:1)


**בְּנֵי לַעְדָּן.** לְלַעְדָּן הַגֵּרְשֻׁנִּי הָיָה רֹאשׁ הָאָבוֹת יְחִיאֵל; הוּא ״לַעְדָּן״ הָאָמוּר לְעֵיל (דברי הימים א כג:ח):


**The sons of Ladan** To Ladan the Gershonite was the chief of the fathers, Jehiel. He is Ladan mentioned above (23:7-9).



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:22:1)


**וְיוֹאֵל אָחִיו.** אָחִיו שֶׁל יְחִיאֵל, כְּדִכְתִיב לְמַעְלָה ״יְחִיאֵל וְזֵתָם וְיוֹאֵל״ (דברי הימים א כג:ח):


**and Joel his brother** The brother of Jehiel, as is written above (23:8): “Jehiel, and Zetham, and Joel.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:22:2)


**עַל אוֹצְרוֹת בֵּית ה׳.** אוֹצָרוֹת הַרְבֵּה הָיוּ בָּעֲזָרָה, כָּל אוֹצָר וְאוֹצָר לְתִקּוּנוֹ, וְעַל כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד הָיָה מְמֻנֶּה אֶחָד:


**over the treasuries of the House of the Lord** There were many treasuries in the forecourt, each treasury for its individual purpose, and over each one was one appointee.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:24:1)


**וּשְׁבוּאֵל בֶּן גֵּרְשׁוֹם בֶּן מֹשֶׁה נָגִיד עַל הָאֹצָרוֹת.** כְּמוֹ כֵן נָגִיד עַל אֶחָד מֵאוֹצְרוֹת הָעֲזָרָה; וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים: **נָגִיד עַל הָאֹצָרוֹת**, כָּל הָאֲמַרְכָּלִין כֻּלָּם הָיוּ תַּחְתָּיו:


**And Shebuel the son of Gershom was the ruler over the treasuries** Similarly, [this means] the ruler over one of the treasuries of the forecourt. Others explain [that he was literally] the ruler over the treasuries. All the Temple trustees were under him.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:29:1)


**לַיִּצְהָרִי כְּנַנְיָהוּ וּבָנָיו לַמְּלָאכָה הַחִיצוֹנָה.** מְלָאכָה שֶׁעוֹשִׂין חוּץ לָעִיר בַּיְּעָרִים וּבֶהָרִים, וְלִכְרוֹת אֲרָזִים וְלַחְצֹב אֲבָנִים וְלַחֲרֹשׁ שְׂדֵה שֶׁל הֶקְדֵשׁ:


**Of the Izharites: Chenaniah and his sons to the outside work** to the work that was done outside the city, in the forests and in the mountains; to hew cedars and to quarry stones and to plow fields belonging to the Sanctuary.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:30:1)


**לַחֶבְרוֹנִי חֲשַׁבְיָהוּ.** עַד **לְכֹל מְלֶאכֶת ה׳** – הֵם שׁוֹטְרִים עַל אוֹתָם שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂים מְלֶאכֶת ה׳ בְּעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן:


**Of the Hebronites: Hashabiah... to all the work of the Lord** They are the officers over those who did the work of the Lord in trans-Jordan.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:30:2)


**וְלַעֲבֹדַת הַמֶּלֶךְ.** אַף הֵם מְמֻנִּים עַל יוֹשְׁבֵי נַחֲלַת הַמֶּלֶךְ, אוֹצְרוֹת הַמֶּלֶךְ, וְכֵן לְפָנֵינוּ **לְכָל דְּבַר הָאֱלֹהִים וּדְבַר הַמֶּלֶךְ**:


**and to the service of the king** They were also appointed over the dwellers of the king’s heritage and the king’s treasuries, and so [we find] further (v. 32): “for every matter of God and matter of the king.”



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 26:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/26:31:1)


**בִּשְׁנַת הָאַרְבָּעִים וְגוֹ׳.** וְגַם כָּל חֶשְׁבּוֹנוֹת הַלָּלוּ בִּשְׁנַת הָאַרְבָּעִים הָיוּ:


**to his generations to the fathers in the fortieth year, etc.** And also all these computations were in the fortieth year.



## Chapter 27


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:1:1)


**לְכָל דְּבַר הַמַּחְלְקוֹת.** כָּל זֶה הָיָה מִתְּחִלַּת מַלְכוּתוֹ וְאֵילָךְ; וְאַגַּב שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר מִסְפַּר הַלְוִיִּם, מְסַפֵּר וְהוֹלֵךְ מִי הָיוּ שָׂרֵי מַחְלֹקֶת דָּוִד:


**in every matter of the divisions** All this was from the beginning of his reign and onwards, and since the number of the Levites is stated, he proceeds to relate who the leaders of David’s division were.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:1:2)


**הַבָּאָה וְהַיּוֹצֵאת חֹדֶשׁ בְּחֹדֶשׁ לְכֹל חָדְשֵׁי הַשָּׁנָה.** שֶׁהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל חֲלוּקִים לִשְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֲלָקִים, וְכָל חֵלֶק וְחֵלֶק שִׁמֵּשׁ אֶת הַמֶּלֶךְ חֹדֶשׁ, וְהַמֶּלֶךְ נוֹתֵן לָהֶם הוֹצָאוֹתָם, כְּדִכְתִיב בְּסֵפֶר מְלָכִים ״וְלִשְׁלֹמֹה שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר נְצִיבִים עַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְכִלְכְּלוּ אֶת הַמֶּלֶךְ וְאֶת בֵּיתוֹ״ (מלכים א ד:ז), וְהֵם מְזֻמָּנִים לִלְחוֹם מִלְחַמְתּוֹ וּלְכָל עֲבוֹדָתוֹ בָּזֶה הַחֹדֶשׁ; וְכָל זֶה תְּחִלַּת מַלְכוּתוֹ הָיָה:


**the one coming and the one leaving, month by month, for all the months of the year** for all the Israelites were divided into twelve parts, and each segment served the king for a month, and the king would provide them with their expenses, as it is written in the Book of Kings (I 4:7): “And Solomon had twelve officers over all Israel, who provided victuals for the king and his household.” They were ready to fight his war and to [perform] all his service in that month. All this was [instituted] at the beginning of his reign.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:1:3)


**עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה אָלֶף.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה פְּעָמִים עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה אֲלָפִים, דָּוִד לֹא בָחַר אֶלָּא אַנְשֵׁי חַיִל וַעֲשִׁירֵי הָעָם אֲשֶׁר יוּכַל לַעֲזֹב עִסְקֵיהֶם וְלַעֲסֹק בְּעִסְקֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ, אֲבָל עֲנִיֵּי הָעָם הַצְּרִיכִין לְפַרְנֵס עַצְמָם לֹא לָקַח דָּוִד:


**twenty-four thousand** Although Israel was more than twelve times twenty-four thousand, David chose only the mighty warriors and the rich people, who could leave their affairs and engage in the king’s affairs; David did not take the poor people, who had to support themselves.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:3:1)


**הָרֹאשׁ לְכָל שָׂרֵי הַצְּבָאוֹת. יָשְׁבְעָם מִבְּנֵי פֶרֶץ** הָיָה, וְהָיָה רֹאשׁ לְכָל שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר שָׂרֵי צְבָאוֹת, וּלְפִי שֶׁהָיָה **מִן בְּנֵי פֶרֶץ** מִנָּהוּ דָּוִד רֹאשׁ:


**the chief of all the leaders of the hosts** Jashabeam was [one] of the sons of Perez, and he was the chief of all the twelve leaders, and because he was [one] of the sons of Perez, David appointed him chief.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:4:1)


**וּמִקְלוֹת הַנָּגִיד.** בַּר מַחֲלֹקְתּוֹ הַמְּסַיֵּעַ עִמּוֹ וּמִקְלוֹת:


**Umikloth was the leader** His fellow division member, who assisted him, was Umikloth.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:5:1)


**שַׂר הַצָּבָא הַשְּׁלִישִׁי.** מִשּׁוּם כְּבוֹד **בְּנָיָהוּ** קוֹרֵהוּ שַׂר הַצָּבָא יוֹתֵר מֵהַשְּׁאָר, לְבַד מִ**יָּשְׁבְעָם** שֶׁהָיָה מִבְּנֵי **פֶּרֶץ**, שֶׁקּוֹרֵהוּ כְּמוֹ כֵן רֹאשׁ, דִּכְתִיב **הָרֹאשׁ לְכָל שָׂרֵי הַצְּבָאוֹת**, מִפְּנֵי כְּבוֹדוֹ שֶׁל דָּוִד שֶׁהָיָה מִבְּנֵי **פֶּרֶץ** כְּמוֹהוּ:


**The leader of the third host** Heb. שַׂר הַצָּבָא הַשְּׁלִישִי. Because of the honor of Benaiah, he calls him the leader of the host more than the others, with the exception of Jashabeam, who was of the sons of Perez, who is likewise called (verse 3): “the chief of all the leaders of the hosts,” because of David’s honor, who was, like him, [one] of the sons of Perez.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:5:2)


**הַכֹּהֵן רֹאשׁ.** שֶׁהָיָה רֹאשׁ. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים שֶׁהָיָה כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל; וְאֵינוֹ כֵּן, שֶׁהֲרֵי צָדוֹק וְאֶבְיָתָר הָיוּ כֹּהֲנִים גְּדוֹלִים:


**the priest, the chief** who was the chief, and some say that he was the high priest, but it is not so, because Zadok and Ebiathar were high priests.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:6:1)


**גִּבּוֹר הַשְּׁלֹשִׁים.** כְּדִלְעֵיל, דִּכְתִיב ״מִן הַשְּׁלֹשִׁים הִנּוֹ נִכְבָּד וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים א יא:כה):


**the mightiest of the thirty** as above (11:25): “Of the thirty, he was the most honored, etc.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:7:1)


**הָרְבִיעִי עֲשָׂהאֵל אֲחִי יוֹאָב וּזְבַדְיָה בְנוֹ אַחֲרָיו.** שֶׁהֲרֵי עֲשָׂהאֵל נֶהֱרַג בִּתְחִלַּת מַלְכוּתוֹ, שֶׁהֲרָגוֹ אַבְנֵר (שמואל ב ב:כג), וְנָתַן זְבַדְיָה בְּנוֹ אַחֲרָיו תַּחְתָּיו עַל מַחֲלֻקְתּוֹ:


**The fourth, etc., was Asahel, the brother of Joab, and Zebadiah his son after him** for Asahel was assassinated at the beginning of his reign, for Abner assassinated him, (II Sam. 2:23) and he [David] appointed Zebadiah his son in his stead over his division.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:8:1)


**הַשַּׂר שַׁמְהוּת הַיִּזְרָח.** הַזַּרְחִי:


**the leader was Shamhuth the Zerahite** - Heb. הַיִּזְרָח



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:16:1)


**וְעַל שִׁבְטֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** כְּבָר אָמַרְנוּ רָאשֵׁי הַמַּחֲלֹקֶת אֲשֶׁר לְדָוִיד, וְעַתָּה מְסַפֵּר מִי הָיוּ שָׂרֵי שִׁבְטֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, שַׂר כָּל שֵׁבֶט וָשֵׁבֶט:


**And over the tribes of Israel** We already stated the chiefs of David’s divisions, and now he tells who the leaders of the tribes of Israel were, the leader of each tribe.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:23:1)


**וְלֹא נָשָׂא דָוִיד מִסְפָּרָם לְמִבֶּן עֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה וּלְמָטָּה.** פִּתְרוֹן: כְּבָר אָמַרְנוּ (דברי הימים א כא:ה) שֶׁמָּנָה יִשְׂרָאֵל וְחִלְּקָן לְמַחְלְקוֹת, וְגַם הַלְוִיִּם מָנָה וַיְשִׂימֵם עַל עֲבוֹדָתָם, וְכָל אֵלּוּ שֶׁמָּנָה, לֹא מָנָה אֶחָד מֵהֶם פָּחוֹת מִבֶּן עֶשְׂרִים:


**Now David did not take their count from twenty years old and below** The meaning is: We already stated that he counted Israel and divided them into divisions, and he also counted the Levites and appointed them upon their service; and all these that he counted he did not count any of them less than twenty years old.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:23:2)


**כְּכוֹכְבֵי הַשָּׁמָיִם.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁאֵין יָכוֹל אִישׁ לִמְנוֹת כּוֹכְבֵי הַשָּׁמָיִם, כָּךְ אֵין יָכוֹל לִמְנוֹתָם:


**like the stars of the heavens** Just as man cannot count the stars of the heavens, so he cannot count them.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:24:1)


**וְלֹא כִלָּה.** פִּתְרוֹן: וְעוֹד אֲנִי אוֹמֵר לְךָ: הֲלֹא **יוֹאָב** הִתְחִיל פַּעַם אַחֶרֶת **לִמְנוֹת** הָעָם לְמַעְלָה מֵעֶשְׂרִים, וְלֹא כִלָּה:


**but did not finish** The meaning is: And moreover, I say to you: Did not Joab commence on another occasion to count the people above twenty and not finish?


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:24:2)


**וַיְהִי בָזֹאת קֶצֶף עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל.** מֵאוֹתוֹ קְצָת שֶׁמָּנָה הָיָה קֶצֶף עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל, לְפִי שֶׁעָלָה בְמַחֲשַׁבְתּוֹ לִמְנוֹת כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל, לַעֲבוֹר עַל הַפָּסוּק ״אֲשֶׁר אִם יוּכַל אִישׁ לִמְנוֹת אֶת עֲפַר הָאָרֶץ וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית יג:טז), וְלַעֲבוֹר הַפָּסוּק ״הַבֶּט נָא הַשָּׁמַיְמָה וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית טו:ה):


**and there was wrath upon Israel because of this** Because of the small segment that he counted, there was wrath upon Israel, because it entered his mind to count all Israel and to transgress the verse (Gen. 13:16): “...so that if anyone can count the dust of the earth, etc.” and to transgress the verse (ibid. 15:5): “Look, please, toward heaven, etc.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:24:3)


**וְלֹא עָלָה הַמִּסְפָּר.** פִּתְרוֹן: אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֲנִי אוֹמֵר **הֵחֵל לִמְנוֹת וְלֹא כִלָּה** – וְלֹא מָנָה כִּי אִם מְעַט, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא עָלָה מִסְפַּר הַמְּנוּיִין בְּסֵפֶר שְׁמוּאֵל כְּמִסְפַּר הַמְּנוּיִין כָּאן: ״אֶלֶף אֲלָפִים וּמְאָה אֶלֶף״ וְ״יְהוּדָה אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְשִׁבְעִים אֶלֶף״ (דה״א כא:ה), וּבִשְׁמוּאֵל ״שְׁמֹנֶה מֵאוֹת אֶלֶף״ וְ״יְהוּדָה חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת אֶלֶף״ (שב כד:ט). וְאִם תֹּאמַר: שְׁנֵי מִסְפָּרִים הָיוּ, שֶׁפַּעֲמַיִם מְנָאָן, אַחַת בִּשְׁמוּאֵל וְאַחַת כָּאן – אֶלָּא כָּךְ הוּא: **לֹא עָלָה הַמִּסְפָּר בְּמִסְפַּר דִּבְרֵי הַיָּמִים**, כִּי אוֹתוֹ הַמִּסְפָּר שֶׁמָּנָה, לֹא כָתַב בִּשְׁמוּאֵל כִּי אִם קְצָת מִן הַמִּסְפָּר, מִשּׁוּם צַעַר וַאֲבֵילוּת שֶׁנִּגְּפוּ אֲפִלּוּ עַל אוֹתָן מְעַט, וְכָאן מִשּׁוּם כְּבוֹד דָּוִד, אֲפִלּוּ אִם הָיָה הַמִּסְפָּר יוֹתֵר, הָיָה אוֹמְרוֹ, לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה מַשְׁמַע שֶׁלֹּא עַל חִנָּם הָיָה **קֶצֶף עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל**, שֶׁהֲרֵי כְּבָר מָנָה הַרְבֵּה מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל; וְעוֹד, מִשּׁוּם כְּבוֹדוֹ, שֶׁהָיוּ כֻּלָּם לוֹ לַעֲבָדִים כְּאִישׁ אֶחָד:


**and the number did not come up to** The meaning is: Although I say, “commenced to count but did not finish,” he counted only a small segment, for the number of the counted ones in the Book of Samuel did not come up to the number in Chronicles, (21:5) for that number that he counted he did not write in Samuel, but a little of the number, and here, because of David’s honor, even if the number were more, he would have stated it, because it implies that not for nought was there wrath on Israel because he already counted many of Israel, and moreover, because of his honor, that they were all his servants, like one man.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:25:1)


**וְעַל אוֹצְרוֹת הַמֶּלֶךְ.** אוֹצְרוֹת כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב; כָּל זֶה הוֹלֵךְ וּמְסַפֵּר לִגְדֻלַּת דָּוִד, וַאֲפִלּוּ מִי שֶׁהָיָה מְמֻנֶּה עַל הָאֲתוֹנוֹת שֶׁלּוֹ (לקמן, ל):


**And over the treasuries of the king** the treasuries of silver and gold. All this he proceeds to relate for David’s greatness, and even the one who was appointed over his she-donkeys.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:25:2)


**וְעַל הָאוֹצָרוֹת בַּשָּׂדֶה.** וְעַל מַה שֶּׁגָּדֵל בַּשָּׂדֶה, תְּבוּאוֹת יַיִן וְשֶׁמֶן וְכָל מִינֵי אוֹכְלִין וּמַשְׁקִין:


**and over the storehouses in the field** and over what grew in the field: grains, wine, and oil, and all kinds of foods and drinks.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:25:3)


**בֶּעָרִים.** לְחַמֵּשׁ הֶעָרִים וּלְחַמֵּשׁ עָרֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ וּמִגְדְּלוֹתָיו:


**in the cities** to supply the cities and to supply the cities of the king and his towers.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:26:1)


**וְעַל עֹשֵׂי מְלֶאכֶת הַשָּׂדֶה לַעֲבֹדַת הָאֲדָמָה.** לַחֲרֹשׁ הָאֲדָמָה:


**And over those who do the work of the field for the work of the soil** to plow the soil.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:26:2)


**עֶזְרִי בֶּן כְּלוּב.** הָיָה מְמֻנֶּה עַל כָּךְ לְצַוּוֹת לַחֲרוֹשׁ הָאֲדָמָה, וְהוּא נוֹתֵן לָהֶם בָּקָר וְכָל כְּלֵי הַמַּחֲרֵישָׁה:


**Ezri the son of Chelub** was appointed to command to plow the soil, and he would give them the cattle and all the implements of the plow.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:27:1)


**וְעַל הַכְּרָמִים.** לַעֲדוֹר וְלִזְמוֹר הַכְּרָמִים:


**And over the vineyards** to hoe and prune the vineyards.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:27:2)


**וְעַל שֶׁבַּכְּרָמִים.** הוּא **אוֹצְרוֹת הַיַּיִן**:


**and over what was in the vineyards** i.e., the storehouses of the wine.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:28:1)


**וְעַל הַזֵּיתִים.** לְנוֹטְעָן וְלִמְסוֹק:


**And over the olive trees** to plant them and gather the olives.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:29:1)


**וְעַל הַבָּקָר הָרֹעִים בַּשָּׁרוֹן שִׁרְטַי הַשָּׁרוֹנִי.** הַבָּקִי בְּעִנְיָנָם:


**And over the cattle that pasture in the Sharon was Shirtai the Sharonite** who was knowledgeable in matters concerning them.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:30:1)


**הַיִּשְׁמְעֵאלִי.** הַיּוֹדֵעַ בְּטוֹב עִסְקָן שֶׁל **גְּמַלִּים**:


**the Ishmaelite** who knew the character of the occupation of camels.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:31:1)


**וְעַל הַצֹּאן יָזִיז הַהַגְרִי.** כִּי הַהַגְרִים יוֹשְׁבֵי אֹהֶל בַּשָּׂדֶה, לָכֵן מִנָּהוּ עַל צֹאנוֹ:


**And over the flocks was Jaziz the Hagarite** Since the Hagarites were tent dwellers in the field, he appointed him over his flocks.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:32:1)


**וִיחִיאֵל בֶּן חַכְמוֹנִי עִם בְּנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ.** מְגַדְּלָן וְאוֹמֵן שֶׁלָּהֶם, מְלַמְּדָם תַּרְבּוּת:


**And Jehiel the son of Hachmoni was with the king’s sons** He was their nurturer and their nurse, who taught them culture.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:33:1)


**וְחוּשַׁי הָאַרְכִּי רֵעַ הַמֶּלֶךְ.** שׁוֹשְׁבִינוֹ; ״קִרְיַת סֵפֶר״ (יהושע טו:טו) תַּרְגּוּם: ״קִרְיְתָא דְאַרְכֵי״. וּמִדְרָשׁ רַבָּה (שוחר טוב נה:א): כְּשֶׁחָטָא דָּוִד בְּבַת שֶׁבַע שָׁאַל לְחוּשַׁי הָאַרְכִּי, אִם יָשׁוּב בִּתְשׁוּבָה אִם יְקַבְּלֵנוּ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בִּתְשׁוּבָה, לָתֵת לוֹ אֲרוּכָה לְחֶטְאוֹ, וְאָמַר לוֹ כֵּן. ״לְמֵרֵעֵהוּ אֲשֶׁר רֵעַ לוֹ״ דְּשִׁמְשׁוֹן (שופטים יד:כ) תַּרְגּוּמוֹ ״שׁוֹשְׁבִינֵיהּ״:


**And Hushai the Archite** - Heb. קִרְיַת סֵפֶר (Jos. 15:15, Jud. 1:11) is translated by the Targum as קִרְיְתָא דְּאַרְכֵי. In *Midrash Rabbah* we find that when David sinned with Bathsheba, he asked Hushai the Archite whether, if he would repent, the Holy One, blessed be He, would accept his repentance to grant him an extension for his sin, and he said, “Yes.” **the king’s companion** His best man (Jud. 14: 20): “to his companion (לְמֵרֵעֵהוּ), who had accompanied (רֵעָה) him,” written regarding Samson; it is translated by the *Targum*: שׁוֹשְׁבִינֵיהּ his best man.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 27:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/27:34:1)


**וְאַחֲרֵי אֲחִיתֹפֶל.** לְאַחַר שֶׁמֵּת, לָקַח לוֹ דָּוִד אֶת **יְהוֹיָדָע וְאֶבְיָתָר** בִּמְקוֹמוֹ:


**And after Ahithophel** After he died, David took for himself Jehoiada and Ebiathar in his place.



## Chapter 28


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:1:1)


**וַיַּקְהֵל דָּוִיד אֶת כָּל שָׂרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** אֵלּוּ הַשָּׂרִים **שָׂרֵי שְׁבָטִים**, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וְעַל שִׁבְטֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לָרְאוּבֵנִי״ (דה״א כז:טז):


**And David assembled all the leaders of Israel** These are the leaders, the leaders of the tribes, as it is written (above 27:16): “And over the tribes of Israel, the leader of the Reubenites was...”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:1:2)


**וְשָׂרֵי הַמַּחְלְקוֹת.** אֵלּוּ שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה מַחְלְקוֹת שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ (דברי הימים א כז:א), ״וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִסְפָּרָם וְגוֹ׳״:


**and the leaders of the divisions** These are the twelve divisions of the king, as above (27:1): “And the Children of Israel to their number, etc.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:1:3)


**וְשָׂרֵי הָאֲלָפִים.** כִּדְלְעֵיל (דברי הימים א יג:א):


**and the leaders of the thousands** as above.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:1:4)


**וְשָׂרֵי הַמֵּאוֹת.** שׁוֹטְרֵיהֶם:


**and the leaders of the hundreds** their officers.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:1:5)


**וְשָׂרֵי כָל רְכוּשׁ וּמִקְנֶה לַמֶּלֶךְ.** אוֹתָן שֶׁמַּזְכִּיר לְעֵיל (דברי הימים א כז:כה-לא), ״עַל אוֹצְרוֹת הַמֶּלֶךְ״ וְ״עַל הַכְּרָמִים״ וְ״עַל הַזֵּיתִים״ וְהַמִּקְנֶה – הַשָּׂרִים שֶׁהָיוּ עַל הַבָּקָר וְעַל הַגְּמַלִּים וְעַל הַצֹּאן; שֶׁכָּל מִין הַבְּהֵמָה קָרוּי מִקְנֶה, כְּדִכְתִיב ״גַּם מִקְנֵה בָּקָר וָצֹאן הַרְבֵּה הָיָה לִי״ דְּקֹהֶלֶת (קהלת ב:ז):


**and the officers over all the property and the livestock of the king** those whom he mentions above (27:25-31): “And over the treasuries of the king... and over the vineyards... and over the olive trees... and the livestock”; and the officers who were in charge of the cattle, and of the camels, and of the flocks, for every species of domestic animal is called מִקְנֶה, as it is written (Ecc. 2:7): “I also had much livestock (מִקְנֶה) consisting of cattle and flocks.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:8:1)


**כָל יִשְׂרָאֵל.** כַּגֵּר כָּאֶזְרָח:


**all Israel** the stranger like the native.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:8:2)


**וּבְאָזְנֵי אֱלֹהֵינוּ.** לִפְנֵי אֱלֹהֵינוּ; דּוּגְמָא: ״אַתֶּם נִצָּבִים הַיּוֹם כֻּלְּכֶם לִפְנֵי ה׳ אֱלֹהֵיכֶם״ (דברים כט:ט), וְכֵן ״וַיַּעֲמֹד הַמֶּלֶךְ עַל הָעַמּוּד״ אֲשֶׁר ״לִפְנֵי ה׳״ (מלכים ב כג:ג):


**and in the ears of our God** Before our God, similar to (Deut. 29:9): “You are standing today, all of you, before the Lord your God.” Also, (II Kings 23:3): “And the king stood on his place, etc. before the Lord.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:8:3)


**שִׁמְרוּ וְדִרְשׁוּ כָּל מִצְוֹת ה׳ אֱלֹהֵיכֶם.** זֶה אָמַר לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל יַחַד, אֲבָל לִשְׁלֹמֹה אָמַר לִפְנֵי כָל יִשְׂרָאֵל: **וְעָבְדֵהוּ**:


**observe and seek all the commandments of the Lord your God** He said this to all Israel together, but to Solomon he said it before all Israel [as in the following verse].



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:9:1)


**בְּלֵב שָׁלֵם.** בְּלֵבָב שָׁלֵם לֹא נֶאֱמַר, שֶׁיְּהֵא מַשְׁמַע: שְׁנֵי לְבָבוֹת, אֶלָּא בְּלֵב שָׁלֵם, שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה לְךָ אֶלָּא לֵבָב אֶחָד לְאָבִינוּ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַיִם; וְכֵן יָסַד הַפַּיָּט (בקדושתא דמוסף של יום ראשון דראש השנה): ״שְׁנֵי לְבָבוֹת לְךָ כְּאֶחָד לְאֶחָד״. וְדֻגְמָתוֹ: ״וְהָיָה לְבַבְכֶם שָׁלֵם עִם ה׳ אֱלֹהֵינוּ״ (מלכים א ח:סא) – שְׁלֵמִים לֹא כְתִיב, אֶלָּא ״שָׁלֵם״, כָּל שְׁנֵי לְבוֹתָם יִהְיוּ לֵב אֶחָד שָׁלֵם. וְכֵן מְפֹרָשׁ בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (בראשית רבה מח:א): ״וְסַעֲדוּ לִבְּכֶם אַחַר תַּעֲבֹרוּ״ (בראשית יח:ה) – מְלַמֵּד שֶׁאֵין לַמַּלְאָכִים אֶלָּא לֵב אֶחָד. וְגַם לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא יִהְיֶה כֵּן, כְּדִכְתִיב ״שִׁיתוּ לִבְּכֶם לְחֵילָה״ (תהלים מח:יד):


**and worship Him with a whole heart** Heb. בְּלֵב שָׁלֵם. It is not stated בְּלֵבָב שָׁלֵם, which would mean two hearts, but בְּלֵב שָׁלֵם, that you shall have but one heart for our Father Who is in heaven. And so did the liturgical poet set down (in the Kedushah of Musaph of the first day of Rosh Hashanah): “You have two hearts like one for the One.” Similar to this, [we find in] (I Kings 8:61): “Let your heart [therefore] be whole (שָׁלֵם) with the Lord our God.” It is not written שְׁלֵמִים but שָׁלֵם: your two hearts will be one whole heart. And so it is explained (Gen. 18:5): “... and refresh your heart (לִבְּכֶם), afterwards you may pass on.” This teaches us that the angels have only one heart, and also in the future it will be so, as it is written: (Ps. 48:14): “Give heed (לִבְּכֶם lit., your heart) to its walls.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:9:2)


**כִּי כָל לְבָבוֹת דּוֹרֵשׁ ה׳.** כָּאן נוֹפֵל לוֹמַר לְבָבוֹת: כָּל לְבָבוֹת דּוֹרֵשׁ ה׳, בֵּין טוֹב וּבֵין רַע:


**for the Lord seeks all hearts** Here it is appropriate to say לְבָבוֹת ; the Lord seeks all hearts, whether good or bad.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:9:3)


**וְכָל יֵצֶר מַחֲשָׁבוֹת מֵבִין.** בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה שָׁנִינוּ (בראשית רבה ט:ג): קוֹדֶם שֶׁנּוֹצַר יְצִיר מֵבִין הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מַה שֶּׁאָדָם עָתִיד לַחְשׁוֹב:


**and He understands the thoughts of every creation** In *Genesis Rabbah* (9:3), we learned: “Before a creature is created, the Holy One, blessed be He, understands what the person is destined to think.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:9:4)


**אִם תִּדְרְשֶׁנּוּ יִמָּצֵא לָךְ.** לְכָךְ אֲנִי אוֹמֵר **וְדִרְשׁוּ כָּל מִצְוֹת ה׳**:


**if you seek Him, He will be found to you** Therefore, I say, (v. 8): “and seek all the commandments of the Lord.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:9:5)


**יַזְנִיחֲךָ לָעַד.** יַעַזְבְךָ אֵין כְּתִיב כָּאן, אֶלָּא יַזְנִיחֲךָ, שֶׁהוּא קָשֶׁה מִמֶּנּוּ; כְּמוֹ ״וְהֶאֶזְנִיחוּ״ (ישעיה יט:ו); ״זָנַח ה׳ מִזְבְּחוֹ״ (איכה ב:ז):


**He will abandon you forever** Heb. יַזְנִיחֲךָ. It is not written here: He will forsake you (יַעַזָבְךָ), but He will abandon you (יַזְנִיחֲך), which is [a] harsher [term] like (Isa. 19: 6): “And they will abandon (הֶאֶזְנִיחוּ) the rivers”; (Lam 2:7): “The Lord abandoned (זָנַַח) His altar.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:10:1)


**כִּי ה׳ בָּחַר בְּךָ לִבְנוֹת בַּיִת לַמִּקְדָּשׁ.** לֹא לִבְנוֹת לוֹ לָדוּר בּוֹ, ״כִּי הַשָּׁמַיִם וּשְׁמֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם לֹא יְכַלְכְּלוּהוּ״ (דברי הימים ב ב:ה), אֶלָּא לַמִּקְדָּשׁ – לְצֹרֶךְ הָאָרוֹן שֶׁנִּקְרָא מִקְדָּשׁ, דִּכְתִיב ״וְנָסְעוּ הַקְּהָתִים נֹשְׂאֵי הַמִּקְדָּשׁ״ (במדבר י:כא), וְהוּא הָאָרוֹן. וְלֹא בָּחַר בְּךָ מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאַתָּה מְשֻׁבָּח מִשְּׁאָר אֲנָשִׁים, אֶלָּא לְמַעֲנוֹ וּלְמַעַן רַחֲמָיו, לְכָךְ **חֲזַק וַעֲשֵׂה**:


**for the Lord has chosen you to build a house for the Sanctuary** not to build it for Him to dwell therein, for the heavens and the heavens of the heavens cannot contain Him, but for the Sanctuary, for the requirement of the Ark, which is called מִקְדָּשׁ, as it is written (Num. 10:21): “And the Kehathites, the bearers of the מִקְדָּשׁ traveled,” and that is the Ark. He did not choose you because you are more praiseworthy than other men, but for His sake and for the sake of His mercies; therefore be strong and do it.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:11:1)


**תַּבְנִית הָאוּלָם.** אָרְכּוֹ וְרָחְבּוֹ וְגָבְהוֹ, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וְהָאוּלָם עַל פְּנֵי הֵיכַל הַבַּיִת וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים א ו:ג):


**the pattern of the porch** Its length, its width, and its height as is written in I Kings (6:3): “And the porch before the Temple of the House, etc.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:11:2)


**וְגַנְזַכָּיו.** מָקוֹם שֶׁאוֹצְרוֹת הַקֳּדָשִׁים מוּנָחִים:


**and its treasuries** the place where the treasuries of the hallowed objects were deposited.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:12:1)


**אֲשֶׁר הָיָה בָרוּחַ עִמּוֹ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר הֶרְאָהוּ שְׁמוּאֵל הָרוֹאֶה, כְּדִכְתִיב לְקַמָּן ״הֵמָּה יִסַּד דָּוִיד וּשְׁמוּאֵל הָרֹאֶה״ (דברי הימים א ט:כב). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (זבחים נד:): ״וַיִּשְׁאַל וַיֹּאמֶר אֵיפֹה שְׁמוּאֵל וְדָוִד וַיֹּאמֶר הִנֵּה בְנָיוֹת בָּרָמָה״ (שמואל א יט:כב) – עוֹסְקִין הָיוּ בְּבִנְיָנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם, הוּא בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, וְלָמַד לוֹ אוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה מַה שֶּׁאֵין תַּלְמִיד וָתִיק יָכוֹל לִלְמֹד בְּכַמָּה שָׁנִים (מדרש שמואל כב:ד):


**that he had by the spirit** As Samuel the Seer showed him, as it is written (above 9:22): “... whom David and Samuel established permanently.” Our Rabbis said (*Zeb.* 54b) (I Sam. 19:22): “... and he asked and said, ‘Where are Samuel and David?’ And one said, ‘Behold at Naioth in Ramah.’” They were engaged in the building of the world. That refers to the Temple. And he taught him at that time what a faithful student cannot learn in many years. (*Mid. Sam.* 22:4)


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:12:2)


**סָבִיב לְאוֹצְרוֹת בֵּית הָאֱלֹהִים וּלְאוֹצְרוֹת הַקֳּדָשִׁים.** וְלֹא מְפָרֵשׁ אֵיזֶה מֵהַקֳּדָשִׁים ״אֲשֶׁר הִקְדִּישׁ דָּוִיד וְרָאשֵׁי הָאָבוֹת״ (דברי הימים א כו:כו) שֶׁבְּאוֹתוֹ פֶּרֶק הָיָה מְמֻנִּים עָלָיו עַד שֶׁיִּבְנוּ בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, אֲבָל אוֹצָרוֹת אֵלּוּ תָּמִיד הָיוּ, כָּל עוֹד שֶׁהַבִּנְיָן קַיָּם:


**round about, of the treasuries of the House of God and of the treasuries of the hallowed things** but he does not explain which of the hallowed things that David and the chiefs of the fathers’ houses hallowed [he is referring to]; and at that time they were appointed over it until they would build the Temple, but these treasuries were always in existence as long as the Temple existed.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:13:1)


**וּלְמַחְלְקוֹת הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם.** שֶׁחָלַק לְעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע מִשְׁמָרוֹת כִּדְלְעֵיל (דברי הימים א כד:א-יט), כָּל זֶה מָסַר לוֹ:


**And of the divisions of the priests and the Levites** for he divided them into twentyfour watches, as above; all this he gave over to him.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:13:2)


**וּלְכָל כְּלֵי עֲבוֹדַת בֵּית ה׳.** מִשְׁקַל מָה וְכַמָּה כָּל כְּלִי וּכְלִי, כַּמְפֹרָשׁ:


**and of all the vessels of the service of the House of the Lord** the weight of what and how much each vessel is, as is explained.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:14:1)


**לַזָּהָב בַּמִּשְׁקָל לַזָּהָב לְכָל כְּלֵי עֲבוֹדָה וַעֲבוֹדָה.** וְכֵן **לְכָל כְּלֵי הַכֶּסֶף בְּמִשְׁקָל**:


**Of gold, in weight for gold, for all the vessels of every service** and so for all silver vessels by weight.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:15:1)


**וְנֵרֹתֵיהֶם זָהָב.** מִשְׁקָל לְכָל נֵר וָנֵר:


**and their lamps of gold** the weight of each lamp.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:15:2)


**בְּמִשְׁקַל מְנוֹרָה וּמְנוֹרָה.** כִּי עֲשָׂרָה מְנוֹרוֹת עָשָׂה (דברי הימים ב ד:ז):


**of the weight of each candelabrum** for he made ten candelabra.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:15:3)


**כַּעֲבוֹדַת מְנוֹרָה וּמְנוֹרָה.** וּמִשְׁקָל כְּלֵי עֲבוֹדַת מְנוֹרָה, מַלְקָחֶיהָ וּמַחְתּוֹתֶיהָ (שמות כה:לח); וְגַם מִשְׁקָל:


**in the service of every candelabrum** and the weight of the service vessels of the candelabrum, its tongs and its charcoal pans, and also the weight.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:16:1)


**שֻׁלְחֲנוֹת הַמַּעֲרֶכֶת.** לַעֲרֹךְ עָלָיו לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים, **לְמִשְׁקַל שֻׁלְחָן וְשֻׁלְחָן**, כִּי עֲשָׂרָה שֻׁלְחָנוֹת שֶׁל זָהָב עָשָׂה בַּהֵיכָל (דברי הימים ב ד:ח); וּמִשְׁקַל הַכֶּסֶף לַשֻּׁלְחֲנוֹת שֶׁבָּעֲזָרָה שֶׁמַּפְשִׁיטִין עֲלֵיהֶם הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת, וּמִשְׁקַל **הַמִּזְלָגוֹת וְהַמִּזְרָקוֹת**:


**for the tables of the showbread** upon which to arrange the showbread. [He instructed him] about the weight of each table, for he made ten golden tables in the Heichal and the weight of the silver for the tables in the forecourt, upon which they would flay the sacrifices, and the weight of the forks and the basins.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:17:1)


**וְלִכְפוֹרֵי הַזָּהָב בְּמִשְׁקָל.** כְּפוֹרֵי כְּלֵי שָׁרֵת, לְקַנֵּחַ בַּכֵּלִים הַדָּם מֵהֶם; וְדוּגְמָא: בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא דְּבָבָא מְצִיעָא (בבא מציעא כד.): הַהוּא גַּבְרָא דְּכָפַר יְדֵיהּ בִּגְלִימָא דְּחַבְרֵיהּ; וְכָל כַּפָּרָה – לְשׁוֹן קִנּוּחַ וְחִטּוּי הוּא:


**and for the basins of gold in weight** Heb. כְּפוֹרֵי כְּפוֹרֵי. [They] are service vessels with which to wipe the blood from the vessels, and an example is in the first (sic) chapter of *Baba Mezia* (24a): “The man who wiped (דְּכָפַר) his hands with his neighbor’s cloak,” and every [expression of] כַּפָּרָה is an expression of wiping and purification.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:18:1)


**זָהָב מְזֻקָּק.** כְּמוֹ ״כֶּסֶף מְזֻקָּק״ (דברי הימים א כט:ד):


**refined gold** - Heb. זָהָב מְזֻקָּק, like refined silver (כֶּסֶף מְזֻקָּק).


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:18:2)


**וְלִתַבְנִית הַמֶּרְכָּבָה.** הֵם הַכְּרוּבִים שֶׁהַשְּׁכִינָה רוֹכֶבֶת עֲלֵיהֶם:


**and for the pattern of the chariot** They are the cherubim upon which the Shechinah “rides.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:18:3)


**לְפֹרְשִׂים.** כְּלוֹמַר: הַמְפוֹרָשִׂים וְהַסּוֹכְכִים עַל הָאָרוֹן, כְּדַאֲמַר ״וְהָיוּ הַכְּרֻבִים פֹּרְשֵׂי כְנָפַיִם וְגוֹ׳״ (שמות כה:כ):


**which spread out [their wings] and covered** Heb. לְפרְשִׂים. i.e., which were spreading and covering over the Ark, as is stated (Ex. 25: 20): “The cherubim shall spread their wings, etc.”



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:19:1)


**הַכֹּל בִּכְתָב.** פִּתְרוֹן: הַכֹּל בִּכְתָב סִדֵּר לוֹ, סְכוּם מִשְׁקָל וּמִשְׁקָל שֶׁל כָּל כְּלִי וּכְלִי, שְׁמוּאֵל הָרוֹאֶה (שמואל א ט:ט):


**All was in writing** The meaning is: He arranged everything in writing for him: the sum of every weight of every vessel, from Samuel the Seer.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:19:2)


**כָּל מְלַאֲכוֹת הַתַּבְנִית.** תַּבְנִית הַבַּיִת חֲכָמַנִי וְהִשְׂכִּילַנִי, כְּמוֹ תַּבְנִית אֹרֶךְ וְרֹחַב וְגֹבַהּ. וְהַכֹּל דָּרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל מִן הַתּוֹרָה בְּרוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ (מידות ב:א): הַר הַבַּיִת הָיָה חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת אַמָּה עַל חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת אַמָּה; דָּרַשׁ: ״אֹרֶךְ הֶחָצֵר מֵאָה בָאַמָּה וְרֹחַב חֲמִשִּׁים בַּחֲמִשִּׁים״ (שמות כז:יח) – חֲמִשִּׁים פְּעָמִים חֲמִשִּׁים הֵם עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת; נִרְאֶה לוֹמַר: חֲתָכָם לְחָמֵשׁ רְצוּעוֹת לִהְיוֹת חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת עַל חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת. וְהוּא מְיֻסָּד בְּסִלּוּק קְרוֹבְץ שֶׁל שַׁבָּת חֲנֻכָּה, הַמַּתְחִיל ״מְנַשֶּׁה אֶת אֶפְרַיִם וְאֶפְרַיִם אֶת מְנַשֶּׁה״. וְהַכֹּל דָּרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל בְּרוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ וְלִמֵּד לְדָוִד כֵּן:


**all the works of the pattern** He endowed me with the wisdom and the understanding of the pattern of the House, e.g., the pattern of the length, the width, and the height, and Samuel derived everything from the Torah with the holy spirit, as we learned (*Middoth* 2:1): “The Temple Mount was five hundred cubits by five hundred cubits.” He expounded (Ex. 27:18): “The length of the forecourt was one hundred cubits; the free width: fifty by fifty.” Fifty times fifty are twentyfive hundred cubits. It appears to me that he cut them into five strips to be five hundred by five hundred cubits. This was set down in the final liturgical poem for the Sabbath of Hanukkah, which commences: “Manasseh, etc.” Samuel derived all this with the holy spirit and taught it to David.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:20:1)


**חֲזַק וֶאֱמַץ.** כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּתְקַיֵּם מַלְכוּתְךָ כְּמוֹ שֶׁהִבְטִיחֲךָ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: **וַהֲכִינוֹתִי מַלְכוּתוֹ עַד עוֹלָם אִם יֶחֱזַק לַעֲשׂוֹת מִצְוֹתַי וְגוֹ׳**:


**Be strong and of good courage** in order that your kingdom endure as the Holy One, blessed be He, has promised you, (verse 7): “... and I shall prepare his kingdom to eternity if he strengthens himself to perform My commandments, etc.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:21:1)


**וְהִנֵּה מַחְלְקוֹת הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם לְכָל עֲבוֹדַת בֵּית הָאֱלֹהִים עִמְּךָ בְּכָל מְלָאכָה.** כְּלוֹמַר: הַכֹּל עָרַכְתִּי וְסִדַּרְתִּי וְזִמַּנְתִּי לְךָ, מַחְלְקוֹת וּפוֹעֲלִים, וְאֵין לְךָ לְעַכֵּב כִּי אִם מִיָּד לִבְנוֹת הַבַּיִת:


**And behold the divisions of the priests and the Levites for all the service of, etc.** That is to say: I arranged and set everything up, and I prepared for you the divisions and the laborers, and you have not to delay it but to build the House immediately.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:21:2)


**וְהַשָּׂרִים.** הַמְּמֻנִּים עַל הַפּוֹעֲלִים וְשׁוֹטְרֵיהֶם:


**and the leaders** who are appointed over the laborers and their officers.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 28:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/28:21:3)


**וְכָל הָעָם לְכָל דְּבָרֶיךָ.** לַעֲשׂוֹת צִוּוּיֶיךָ:


**and all the people for all your words** to perform your command.



## Chapter 29


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 29:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:1:1)


**שְׁלֹמֹה בְנִי אֶחָד וְגוֹ׳.** וַאֲפִלּוּ אִם הָיוּ מֵאָה לֹא דַּי לִבְנוֹתוֹ, וְעוֹד שֶׁהוּא **נַעַר וָרָךְ**:


**My son Solomon is alone, etc.** And even if he were a hundred [people], he would not be able to build it, and furthermore, he is young and tender.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 29:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:1:2)


**וְהַמְּלָאכָה גְדוֹלָה.** וְאִם תֹּאמַר: תַּקְטִין הַבַּיִת! זֶה אִי אֶפְשָׁר, **כִּי לֹא לְאָדָם הַבִּירָה כִּי** אִם **לַה׳ אֱלֹהִים** הַגָּדוֹל וְהַנּוֹרָא, אֲשֶׁר לֹא יְכַלְכְּלוּהוּ הַשָּׁמַיִם וּשְׁמֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם (דברי הימים ב ב:ה); וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵּן אַל תִּבָּהֵל לִבְנוֹת בַּיִת גָּדוֹל כָּזֶה, כִּי אֲנִי בְּ**כָל כֹּחִי הֲכִינוֹתִי לְבֵית אֱלֹהִים הַזָּהָב לַזָּהָב וְהַכֶּסֶף לַכֶּסֶף וְגוֹ׳**, לְפִיכָךְ אֵין לְךָ טֹרַח גָּדוֹל כָּל כָּךְ:


**and the work is great** And if you say that the work should be lessened, that is impossible... **for the palace is not for man but for the Lord God** Who is great and awesome, Whom the heavens and the heavens of the heavens cannot contain, but nevertheless, do not fear to build a great house like this, because “with all my strength I prepared for the House of God (sic), the gold for the gold and the silver for the silver, etc.” Therefore, you do not have so much toil.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 29:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:2:1)


**אַבְנֵי שֹׁהַם וּמִלּוּאִים.** דּוּגְמַת הַמִּשְׁכָּן: ״אַבְנֵי שֹׁהַם וְאַבְנֵי וְגוֹ׳״ (שמות כה:ז):


**onyx stones and filling stones** on the model of the Tabernacle (Ex. 25:7): “Onyx stones and filling stones, etc.”


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 29:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:2:2)


**אַבְנֵי פוּךְ וְרִקְמָה וְכָל אֶבֶן יְקָרָה.** לָתֵת בְּקִירוֹת הַזָּהָב וּבִכְלֵי הַכֶּסֶף:


**carbuncle stones and embroidery, and all precious stones** to put onto the golden walls and into the silver vessels.


###### Rashi on I Chronicles 29:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:2:3)


**וְאַבְנֵי שַׁיִשׁ.** לְעַמּוּדִים וְשֻׁלְחָנוֹת וְלָרִצְפָּה:


**and marble stones** for the pillars, for the tables, and for the floor.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Chronicles 29:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Chronicles/r/29:3:1)
